# Peekaboo Portal — Complete Training Guide

Everything a nursery team needs to use the Peekaboo portal, in one document.

**The portal:** https://peek.peek-a-boo.app
**Audience:** nursery staff — principals, registrars, teachers, assistants, accountants and administrators.

This guide combines all eight training documents. Use the contents below to jump
to a section, or search the page for what you need.

## Contents

1. **Welcome to Peekaboo** — what the portal is, signing in, finding your way around
2. **Training by role** — a learning path for each job
3. **How to — everyday tasks** — 115 step-by-step procedures
4. **Frequently asked questions** — 339 answers in plain language
5. **Getting unstuck** — what to do when something looks wrong
6. **Guide to each area** — what every part of the portal is for
7. **Words and statuses** — the terms and labels you will see
8. **Every page, one click away** — direct links to all 35 portal pages

# Welcome to Peekaboo

> ### If you only read one thing
>
> **1. Sign in at [https://peek.peek-a-boo.app](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/login).** Use
> the username and password your administrator gave you. Your username is not necessarily
> your email address. You must also complete the **"I'm not a robot"** check before
> **Sign In** will work.
>
> **2. Your menu only shows what your role allows.** The sidebar — the menu running down
> the side of the screen — lists exactly the pages you are permitted to open, and nothing
> else. A colleague's menu will look different from yours. That is normal and it is not a
> fault.
>
> **3. Nothing is saved until you press the save button.** On most screens that button is
> **Save**, **Update** or **Save Changes**, and it sits at the bottom of the form. If you
> leave a page without pressing it, your typing is gone.
>
> **4. On a shared computer, always click Logout.** It is the red-outlined button at the
> far end of the top bar, across the top of the screen. Closing the browser does **not**
> sign you out, so the next person would land in your account.
>
> **5. Illness, injury and temperature go in the Note to parents box — and to the room
> lead in person.** Write it in words in the **Note to parents** box on the child's daily
> report, then tell the room lead face to face the same day. Never rely on the portal
> alone to pass on a health concern.

This guide is for anyone who is about to use Peekaboo for the first time. You do not need any technical background. Read it once from top to bottom, then keep it beside you for your first week.

---

## What Peekaboo is

Peekaboo is the staff system for running a nursery. It is the screen the people who run the setting sit in front of: the principal in the office, the registrar at the front desk, the teacher in the room, the accountant doing the books. One Peekaboo installation serves one nursery organisation, which may have more than one centre.

Think of it as your filing cabinet, register, curriculum folder, noticeboard and cash book combined into a single website. Attendance, daily reports to parents, curriculum and assessment, admissions enquiries, invoicing, staff records and centre details all live in the same place, and they all point at the same child record. That is the real benefit: you can answer "is this child up to date on fees, and did they attend last week?" without cross-referencing three notebooks.

Peekaboo runs in your web browser (the program you use to visit websites — Chrome, Safari,
Edge or Firefox). There is nothing to install. The address is
**[https://peek.peek-a-boo.app](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/login)**. It works in **English** and **Arabic**, and it has a light and a dark colour scheme. One thing to know from the start: Peekaboo is the **staff** portal. Parents do not log in here. Parents use a separate app, and Peekaboo publishes to it — daily reports and messages you send reach families through that separate app, not through this one.

---

## What a normal day looks like in Peekaboo

Before you learn any single screen, it helps to picture the whole day. Here is the rhythm of a typical nursery day and the pages it passes through, in order.

### Morning — the register

The day starts on **Children Attendance**, under **Operations** in the sidebar. As children arrive, you mark each one present, and record who is absent, late or excused. If you are also responsible for the team, **Staff Attendance** clocks employees in the same way.

One thing to build into your habit: both attendance pages work on **today only**. There is no date picker on them. Mark the register as the morning happens, not at the end of the week. To look up a past date, you use **Attendance Reports** instead, which is a read-only history.

### Through the day — the daily report

**Daily Reports**, also under **Operations**, is the care record that goes home to the parent. Across the day you record mood, meals and drinks, nap times, milk, nappies and items the child needs, and you write the note to parents.

You can save it as a draft and keep adding to it. When it is finished you **Publish** it, and when you want the parent to receive it by email you use **Publish & Email**. Those are two different things, and the distinction matters — publishing marks the report as final in the portal, emailing is what actually sends it to the family.

### Through the day — observations

When a teacher notices a child doing something worth capturing — a first attempt at scissors, a moment of turn-taking — that goes into **Observations**, under **Early Childhood**. You name the child, describe what you saw, and tag it against the learning objectives it demonstrates, each at an attainment level. Over a term these observations build up, and at the end of the term **Assessment Report** turns them into the printable per-child summary that parents receive.

### Any time — messages to parents

**Messages**, under **Communication**, is the nursery's noticeboard to families. You publish announcements, newsletters, photos and videos to selected children, and those items appear on the parents' timeline in the parent app.

Messages is a **one-way broadcast**. There is no inbox and parents cannot reply to it. If a parent needs to reach you, that happens the way it always has — by phone, by email, or at the door.

### Any time — admissions follow-ups

**CRM Leads**, under **Admissions CRM**, is where a new family's enquiry lives. Somebody rings about a place in September; the registrar records the enquiry, books the tour, adds notes after the visit, and sets a follow-up task so nobody is forgotten. The lead moves through its statuses until the family either enrols or does not.

When a family is accepted, creating their actual child record is a separate step you do yourself on the **Children** page. Reaching the end of the lead pipeline does not create the child for you — plan for that handover.

### End of the day and end of the month — money

**Invoices**, under **Account**, bills a named child for one or more services. **Receipts** records the money that comes back in. **Balance by Class** shows what each child still owes, worked out as billed minus received.

Money going out lives under **Finance**: **Suppliers**, **Purchases**, **Expenses** and **Payment Voucher**.

### And underneath all of it

**Children** and **Staff**, under **People**, are the two rosters everything else refers to. **My Center**, under **Center**, holds your nursery's own details — name, address, phone numbers, logo and session times.

---

## Signing in

1. Open **[https://peek.peek-a-boo.app](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/login)** in your browser. You land on the **Login** screen.
2. In the **Username** box, type your username. This is a **username**, not necessarily an email address — your administrator issues it to you.
3. In the **Password** box, type your password. There is no reveal button (an eye-shaped icon that shows the characters you typed) on this box, so type carefully.
4. Optionally tick **Remember me**.
5. Tick the **"I'm not a robot"** box. This is a security check that confirms a person is signing in rather than a computer, and it sits just below the **Password** box.
6. This step is compulsory — **Sign In** will not proceed without it.
7. Click **Sign In**. The button reads **"Signing in..."** while it works.

You will see a green **"Signed in successfully"** message and land on the **Live Dashboard**.

### If sign-in does not work

- **"Please complete the captcha"** means you skipped step 5. "Captcha" is the name for the **"I'm not a robot"** check. Tick the box and try again.
- A red message about your credentials means the username or password was not accepted. Check both carefully. **The security check resets after every failed attempt**, so tick it again before you retry.
- If the message mentions an incomplete response, simply try once more. If it keeps happening, contact whoever supports your portal.

### If you have forgotten your password

1. On the Login screen, click the small **Forgot?** link. It sits to the right of the word **Password**, just above the password box.
2. **Step 1 of 3 — Username.** Type your username into the **Username** box.
3. Tick the **"I'm not a robot"** check. On this screen it sits *below* the button rather than above it.
4. Click **Continue**.
5. **Step 2 of 3 — Verify.** Check your email inbox for a six-digit code, then type it into the **Email verification code** box.
6. Click **Verify Email**.
7. **Step 3 of 3 — Reset.** The code carries over into the **Authentication code** box by itself, so you do not need to retype it.
8. In **New password**, enter a password of **at least 6 characters**.
9. Type the same password again into **Confirm password**.
10. Click **Reset Password**.

You will see **"Password reset successfully."** and go straight into the portal — you are not sent back to the login form.

Two practical points. Do not refresh your browser part-way through, as that returns you to a blank step 1. And if the reset does not complete at the last step, the most likely reason is that the code was mistyped or has expired — go back and use **Resend Code** to get a fresh one.

If you cannot get into your account at all, your administrator can set a new password for you from the **Staff** page.

### Signing out — please read this one

**Closing the browser does not sign you out.** Your session stays open, so the next person to open the portal on that computer lands in your account with your access.

On any shared computer — reception, a staffroom laptop, the tablet in the room — always click **Logout** at the end of your turn. It is the red-outlined button at the far end of the top bar. There is no "are you sure?" prompt; one click and you are out.

---

## Finding your way around

Once you are signed in, every page sits inside the same frame: a **sidebar** down the side of the screen, and a **top bar** across the very top of the screen that stays in place as you scroll.

### The sidebar

The sidebar is your menu. It runs down the **left-hand edge** of the screen in English, and down the **right-hand edge** in Arabic. It is organised into groups, always in this order:

- **Center** — My Center, Packages, and (for the principal) Centers
- **Operations** — Live Dashboard, Children Attendance, Staff Attendance, Daily Reports, Calendar
- **People** — Children, Staff
- **Communication** — Messages
- **Early Childhood** — Classes, Curriculum, Age Groups, Attainment, Learn Area, Assessments, and indented beneath Assessments: Observations and Assessment Report
- **Finance** — Suppliers, Expenses, Expense Types, Payment Voucher, Purchases
- **Account** — Services, Receipts, Invoices, Payment Modes, Balance by Class
- **Reports & Admin** — Attendance Reports, Roles & Access
- **Admissions CRM** — CRM Leads, Referrals & Sources

Scroll to the very bottom of the sidebar and you will find a panel labelled **Theme** with the sub-text **Light - Dark**.

You can collapse the sidebar (shrink it so it takes less room) to a narrow strip of icons. Click the small square panel button at the very start of the top bar — the far left-hand end in English, the far right-hand end in Arabic. Alternatively, press **Ctrl+B** on a Windows computer, or **Cmd+B** on a Mac. When it is collapsed, rest your mouse pointer on any icon and its name appears. Your choice is remembered. On a phone or narrow tablet the sidebar slides out over the page instead, and closes again as soon as you tap a link.

### The top bar

Reading across from the logo end — the left-hand end in English, the right-hand end in Arabic:

- **The Peekaboo logo** — click it at any time to return to the **Live Dashboard**.
- **Chat** — a speech-bubble button. See the note below.
- **Two badges** (small oval labels) — a **Peekaboo Care** badge, which is simply branding, and next to it **a badge showing your role**, for example **Teacher** or **Accountant**. These appear on wider screens; if your window is narrow they are hidden.
- **The language picker** — a button showing a flag and the current language.
- **The light/dark toggle** — a small square icon button.
- **Logout** — outlined in red, at the far end of the top bar.

There is no profile menu, no avatar, no notifications bell and no search box covering the whole portal in the top bar. Each page shows its own heading in the large area below the top bar, which is where the page's own content appears.

### The single most important rule

**The sidebar only shows what your role is allowed to open.**

Anything your role cannot open is not greyed out — it is simply absent. And if your role has nothing at all inside a group, the whole group heading disappears too. A colleague in a different role will see a differently shaped menu from yours, with groups you do not have and without groups you do.

**This is normal and it is not a fault.** Your sidebar is an honest and complete list of the pages you are allowed to use. If you cannot see something in the menu, you are not meant to be using it from your account.

What each role can open is configured for your nursery, so the exact shape of your menu is a local decision. If you genuinely need a page that is not in your sidebar, the change is not one you can make yourself — ask your administrator, who can adjust it for your role. After they make a change, **sign out and sign back in** to pick it up.

If you ever type or bookmark an address you do not have access to, you will see a short "access denied" panel. That simply means the page belongs to another role. Your session is fine.

---

## Switching to Arabic

The language picker is in the **top bar**, near the far end beside **Logout**. It shows a flag and the current language — **🇺🇸 English** or **🇸🇦 العربية**.

1. Click the language button. A small dropdown (a short list that drops down below the button) opens.
2. Click **English** or **العربية**. The one currently in use is marked with a small dot.

The whole portal changes immediately. In Arabic the entire layout mirrors: **the sidebar moves to the right-hand edge of the screen**, text aligns to the right, and arrow icons flip direction. Everything works exactly as before; it is simply the other way round.

Two things to expect:

- **Your language choice is saved per browser and per device, not on your account.** Choosing Arabic on the reception computer does not change it on your own laptop. Set it once on each device you use.
- **The Login and Forgot Password screens have no language picker.** They appear in English until you have been inside the portal on that browser and chosen Arabic there.
- A few items stay in English in Arabic mode — the **Peekaboo** wordmark itself is deliberately left as it is, and some short labels and month names appear in English. Nothing is missing or broken; where a phrase has no Arabic wording yet, the English is shown rather than a blank.

---

## Light and dark mode

Peekaboo has two colour schemes. Pick whichever is easier on your eyes; it changes nothing about how the portal works.

There are two ways to switch, and they do exactly the same thing:

- Click the **small square icon button** in the top bar. It sits between the language picker and **Logout**.
- Or scroll right down to the bottom of the sidebar, to the panel labelled **Theme** / **Light - Dark**, and click the toggle (a small switch) there.

The icon shows the mode you will switch **to**, not the one you are in — so a **moon** means you are currently in light mode, and a **sun** means you are currently in dark mode.

Your choice is saved in that browser and restored next time. If you have never chosen, Peekaboo follows your computer's own light/dark setting. The toggle is also available on the Login screen before you sign in.

---

## A note about the chat button

The **Chat** button in the top bar opens a sample panel that is not yet connected, so nothing typed into it is sent or stored. Please do not use it to contact anyone.

The real way to communicate with families is the **Messages** page, under **Communication** in the sidebar.

---

## Your first 30 minutes

Work through these in order on your first day. Do them on the computer you will actually be using.

1. **Sign in.** Go to **[https://peek.peek-a-boo.app](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/login)** and use the username and password your administrator gave you.
2. **Complete the security check and sign in.** Tick the **"I'm not a robot"** box, then click **Sign In**.
3. **Look at your role badge.** In the top bar, find the outlined badge next to **Peekaboo Care**. That is your role. Say it out loud — it explains everything else you are about to see.
4. **Read your sidebar from top to bottom.** Note which groups you have. This is the complete list of what you can open. Do not worry about what is not there.
5. **Set your language.** If you want Arabic, set it now with the language picker in the top bar. Remember to do the same on any other computer or tablet you use.
6. **Set light or dark mode.** Use the small square icon button in the top bar, or the **Theme** toggle at the very bottom of the sidebar.
7. **Try collapsing the sidebar.** Click the small square panel button at the very start of the top bar, or press **Ctrl+B** on Windows / **Cmd+B** on a Mac. Then expand it again.
8. **Open the Live Dashboard.** Click the Peekaboo logo at the start of the top bar. Have a look at the figures. Nothing here can be edited — it is a summary screen, and it is the one page that updates its own numbers as you watch.
9. **Open the first page of your own job.** For most people that is [Children Attendance](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/attendance), [Daily Reports](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/daily-reports), [CRM Leads](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/crm/leads) or [Invoices](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/invoices). Just look; do not enter anything yet.
10. **Find your role track.** Open *Training Tracks by Role* and read the track for your role from beginning to end.
11. **Practise signing out.** Click the red-outlined **Logout** button at the far end of the top bar.
12. **Sign back in again.** Get comfortable with this — on a shared computer it is the habit that matters most.


---


<!-- ================= 02-role-tracks ================= -->

# Training Tracks by Role

Peekaboo gives each member of staff a role, and your role decides which pages appear in your sidebar. This document gives each role its own training path: what you will use daily, what you will use now and then, what belongs to your colleagues, the order to learn things in, and how to tell when you are ready.

## How to use this document

Find your role, read that track from beginning to end, then work through its learning path in order. Each step points at a section of the how-to library, and the steps are sequenced so that each one builds on the last. Do not skip ahead — several tasks depend on set-up that an earlier step covers.

The portal itself is at **[https://peek.peek-a-boo.app](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/login)**. Every page named in the tracks below has a direct link in *Every Page, One Click Away*, so you can open it while you read.

## Before you start — four things that apply to everyone

**1. Your sidebar is the honest answer.** The sidebar is the menu running down the side of the screen — the left-hand edge in English, the right-hand edge in Arabic. It lists exactly the pages you are allowed to open, and nothing else. Groups you have no access to disappear entirely, heading and all.

**2. These tracks describe the typical setup.** What each role can actually open is configured per nursery. Your administrator may have given your role more or less than the track below describes. If a page named in your track is not in your sidebar, it simply has not been enabled for your role here — ask your administrator, and treat your sidebar as the final word.

**3. The Principal sees everything.** The Principal role opens every page in the portal regardless of how anything else is configured.

**4. Two areas belong to specific roles and cannot be shared out.** Only the **Admin** can open **Roles & Access**, where what each role may see is decided, and only the Admin can open **Referrals & Sources**. Only the **Principal** can open the multi-centre **Centers** screen. Everyone else — including the Admin — manages their own nursery's details through **My Center**.

## A note on recording health, illness and injury

This applies to every role that touches a daily report, and it is the most important procedural point in this document.

The daily report form shows a **Health Status** selector and a **Temperature** box. **These two selections are not part of the saved report.** Whatever you pick there will not be in the record the parent reads or the record your colleagues see tomorrow.

So the correct procedure is:

1. **Write it in the Note to parents box on the daily report.** You will find this box in the **Teacher Note** section, towards the bottom of the report form. Anything about illness, injury, a temperature reading or a child being sent home goes in there, written out in words. That is the part that is saved and that the parent receives.
2. **Tell the room lead in person, the same day.** Do not rely on the portal to pass a health concern between staff.
3. **For anything ongoing** — an allergy, a medical condition, a special diet — record it on the child's own record. Open [Children](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/children), open the child, then click the **Health** tab across the top of their record.
4. That is where long-term health information belongs and where it stays. It is also what raises the red allergy warning on the child's card.

Teach this as normal procedure from day one, to everyone who writes reports.

---

# Principal

## Who this is for

You run the nursery. You are accountable for whether it is full, whether it is safe, whether the books balance and whether families are happy. You spend your day moving between people, and you dip into the portal to check on things rather than to sit and process records. You also need to be able to open anything, anywhere, when somebody is away or something needs checking.

## What you will use every day

- **Live Dashboard** — your morning glance. Occupancy, attendance headline numbers, late arrivals and upcoming events on the **Main Dashboard** tab; billed against collected on the **Finance** tab; the admissions funnel on the **CRM** tab. It is the one page in the portal that refreshes its own figures while you watch.
- **Children Attendance** — who is actually on the premises right now.
- **Daily Reports** — spot-check that reports are being written and going out. This is quality control on the single thing parents judge you by.
- **CRM Leads** — the enquiries in flight and whether anybody is following them up.
- **Balance by Class** — who owes what.

## What you will use occasionally

- **Children** and **Staff** — the two rosters, when you need a record or a contact detail.
- **Messages** — nursery-wide announcements, closures, newsletters.
- **Calendar** — trips, parents' evenings, holidays.
- **Assessment Report** and **Assessments** — at the end of a term, and when a parent asks about progress.
- **Attendance Reports** — the history grid, for a past date or a pattern of absence.
- **Invoices**, **Receipts**, **Expenses** — oversight of the accountant's work.
- **My Center** — your nursery's own name, address, phone numbers, logo and session times.
- **Packages** — your nursery's own subscription to the Peekaboo software. This is not parent billing.
- **Centers** — the multi-centre screen, yours alone. This is where a new nursery on the platform is set up, together with the login for its administrator. Most principals open this rarely or never.

## What you probably will not see

Nothing — your role opens every page in the portal. That is a responsibility as much as a convenience. Because you can open the finance pages, the staff records and the admissions pipeline, be deliberate about which screens you leave open on a shared display, and be careful on pages like **Roles & Access** where a change affects everybody holding that role.

## Your learning path

1. **Get oriented.** Read *Welcome to Peekaboo* end to end, then sign in and complete *Your first 30 minutes*.
2. **Read the Live Dashboard** — see *How to read the Live Dashboard and switch its tabs*, then *How to change the dashboard's date range*.
3. **Understand the register** — see *How to mark a child present for today*, so you know what your team does each morning and what "today only" means.
4. **Look up a past date** — see *How to view (not edit) attendance for a past date* and *How to generate and export an attendance report*.
5. **Read a daily report** — see *How to review a daily report for a past date*, then read the health and temperature procedure at the top of this document.
6. **Watch the admissions pipeline** — see *How to change a lead's status* and *How to search, sort and filter the leads list*.
7. **Check the money** — see *How to view outstanding balances by class*, then *How to search and sort invoices or receipts*.
8. **Learn what a progress report contains** — see *How to generate an assessment report* and *How to export or print an assessment report*.
9. **Send a nursery-wide message** — see *How to message a whole class (or the whole nursery)*.
10. **Keep the centre record right** — see *How to update your centre's details* and *How to add a session (daily timing)*.
11. **Understand how access is decided** — see *How to change what a role can access*, so you can have an informed conversation with your administrator even though this is their day-to-day responsibility.
12. **Learn the multi-centre screen** — see *How to create a new centre*. Only you can open this, so nobody can cover it for you.

## You are ready when you can…

- Open the Live Dashboard and explain this morning's occupancy, late arrivals and outstanding balance to a colleague.
- Look up any child's attendance for a date last month.
- Open yesterday's daily report for any child and say whether it was published and emailed.
- State where a member of staff must record a temperature or an injury, and why the note to parents is the part that counts.
- Find every lead currently at the tour stage and say who is following each one up.
- Update your nursery's contact details and session times on **My Center** without help.

---

# Registrar

## Who this is for

You are the front desk and the face of admissions. You take the call when a parent rings about a place, you show families round, you chase the ones who went quiet, and when a family joins you are the one who turns them into a real child record with the right parent details, the right pickup people and the right class. Accuracy on your screens is what everything downstream depends on.

## What you will use every day

- **CRM Leads** — every enquiry, from the first phone call to the day the family enrols. Statuses, notes, follow-up tasks and the tour booking all live here.
- **Children** — the child roster, and behind it the largest form in the portal: the child's own details, parent and guardian details, health information, authorised pickup people, and the registration that puts the child in a class.
- **Children Attendance** — the front desk is often where arrivals and departures are actually recorded.
- **Live Dashboard** — the **CRM** tab for your funnel, the **Main** tab for today's numbers.

## What you will use occasionally

- **Classes** — to see which rooms exist, their age group and capacity, before registering a child into one.
- **Calendar** — booking open days, tours and events into the nursery calendar.
- **Messages** — sending information to a family or a class.
- **Attendance Reports** — when a parent asks about their child's attendance record.
- **Staff Attendance** — the same permission that opens the children's register also opens the staff clock-in sheet, so it may well be in your sidebar. Use it if front desk is where your team clocks in.
- **My Center** — if you are the person who keeps the nursery's contact details current.

## What you probably will not see

The **Finance** and **Account** groups — invoicing, receipts, suppliers and expenses belong to the accountant. The curriculum and assessment pages beyond Classes belong to teachers and whoever leads on curriculum. **Roles & Access** and **Referrals & Sources** belong to the administrator — so if a new "how did you hear about us?" option is needed, that is a request you make to them rather than something you add yourself. **Centers** belongs to the principal; your nursery's own details are on **My Center**.

## Your learning path

1. **Get oriented.** Read *Welcome to Peekaboo*, sign in, and complete *Your first 30 minutes*.
2. **Learn the table controls once** — see *How to use the standard table controls (search, sort, page size, paging)*. Every list you touch works this way.
3. **Take an enquiry** — see *How to add a new lead (enquiry)*, then *How to edit a lead*.
4. **Work the list** — see *How to search, sort and filter the leads list*.
5. **Book a visit** — see *How to book and record a nursery tour*.
6. **Keep the history** — see *How to add a note to a lead*, then *How to create a task (follow-up) on a lead*. Nothing in the portal chases a family for you; the task is your reminder and it is only as good as your habit of setting one.
7. **Move the lead along** — see *How to change a lead's status*, then *How to record that a lead has been accepted* and *How to mark a lead as lost or rejected*.
8. **Attribute the enquiry** — see *How to attach a referral or source to a lead*.
9. **Create the child** — see *How to move an accepted family into enrolment*, then *How to add a new child*. Note that an accepted lead does not become a child record on its own — creating the child is your step, and it is the one most often forgotten.
10. **Complete the family record** — see *How to add a parent / guardian*, then *How to add an authorised pickup person*, then *How to record an allergy, medical condition or special diet*.
11. **Put the child in a room** — see *How to register a child into a class*.
12. **Keep the roster clean** — see *How to search and filter the children list*, then *How to edit a child's details* and *How to deactivate or delete a child*.

## You are ready when you can…

- Take a phone enquiry and have it recorded as a lead, with a source and a follow-up task, before the call ends.
- Book a tour for a family and find it again a week later.
- Turn an accepted family into a complete child record: child, parent, pickup people, health information and a class registration.
- List every lead that has gone quiet for more than two weeks.
- Find any child on the roster in a few seconds and confirm who is authorised to collect them.
- Explain to a parent how their child's daily report reaches them.

---

# Teacher

## Who this is for

You run a room. Your day is the children in front of you, and the portal exists to take three jobs off your shoulders: the register, what you tell parents, and the evidence of each child's learning. Everything in your track is something you do while the room is running, so speed and habit matter more than depth.

## What you will use every day

- **Children Attendance** — marking your class present, absent, late or excused, and checking children out at the end of the day. This page works on **today only**, so mark it as the morning happens.
- **Daily Reports** — the care record that goes home: mood, meals and drinks, nap times, milk, nappies, items needed, and your note to parents.
- **Observations** — what you saw a child do, tagged against the objectives it demonstrates.
- **Children** — looking up a child's details, allergies or who is authorised to collect them.

## What you will use occasionally

- **Assessments** — the overview of how your class is progressing.
- **Assessment Report** — the per-child, per-learning-area printable summary at the end of a term.
- **Messages** — sending a note, photo or video to your class's parents.
- **Calendar** — trips and events for your room.
- **Classes** — seeing your room's setup and which rows appear on its daily report.
- **Attendance Reports** — when you need a past date rather than today.
- **Staff Attendance** — the same permission that opens the children's register also opens the staff clock-in sheet, so it may appear in your sidebar.

## What you probably will not see

The **Finance** and **Account** groups belong to the accountant. **CRM Leads** belongs to the registrar. **Staff** records, **Roles & Access** and **Referrals & Sources** belong to administration. The curriculum framework pages — **Curriculum**, **Learn Area**, **Age Groups**, **Attainment** — are usually set up once by whoever leads on curriculum, and you work inside the framework they built rather than changing it.

## Your learning path

1. **Get oriented.** Read *Welcome to Peekaboo*, sign in, and complete *Your first 30 minutes*.
2. **Mark your class present** — see *How to mark a child present for today*.
3. **Handle the exceptions** — see *How to mark a child absent or excused*, then *How to correct a child's attendance times or notes (today)*.
4. **See only your room** — see *How to filter the attendance board by classroom*.
5. **Close the day** — see *How to check a child out at the end of the day*.
6. **Write a report** — see *How to create and save a daily report as a draft*. Before you do, read the health and temperature procedure at the top of this document, and get into the habit of writing the **Note to parents** box first.
7. **Send it home** — see *How to publish a daily report (without emailing it)*, then *How to publish a daily report and email it to the parent in one step*. Learn the difference: publishing marks it final, emailing is what reaches the parent.
8. **Fix and re-send** — see *How to edit the teacher note on a report already sent*, then *How to email an already-saved daily report*.
9. **Look back** — see *How to review a daily report for a past date*.
10. **Record what you saw** — see *How to record an observation*, then *How to link objectives to an observation*.
11. **Maintain your evidence** — see *How to view, edit or delete a saved observation*.
12. **Report on progress** — see *How to read the Assessments summary page*, then *How to generate an assessment report*, *How to edit comments on a saved assessment report* and *How to export or print an assessment report*.

## You are ready when you can…

- Mark your whole class present, absent or late within a few minutes of the morning settling.
- Write, publish and email a complete daily report for one child without checking the steps.
- State exactly where a temperature, an illness or a bump goes — in the note to parents, and to the room lead in person.
- Record an observation and tag it against at least one objective at the right attainment level.
- Produce and print an end-of-term assessment report for a child in your room.
- Look up any child's allergies and authorised pickup people while standing at the door.

---

# Assistant

## Who this is for

You support a room alongside the teacher. In the portal your day-to-day work looks much like theirs — the register and the daily reports — but you will usually be given a narrower set of pages, focused on recording what happens in the room rather than on assessment and reporting. You are very often the person who actually enters the day's detail, so the quality of what parents read depends on you.

## What you will use every day

- **Children Attendance** — marking the room present, absent, late or excused, and checking children out. **Today only**; mark it as it happens.
- **Daily Reports** — the care record: mood, meals and drinks, nap times, milk, nappies, items needed, and the note to parents.
- **Children** — looking up allergies, dietary needs and who may collect a child.

## What you will use occasionally

- **Calendar** — what is happening in the room this week.
- **Messages** — where your nursery has you sending photos or notes to parents.
- **Observations** — where your nursery includes assistants in recording observations.
- **Attendance Reports** — looking up a past date.
- **Live Dashboard** — the day's headline numbers.

## What you probably will not see

The **Finance** and **Account** groups, **CRM Leads**, **Staff** records and the administration pages all belong to other roles. The curriculum framework and assessment reporting pages usually sit with the teacher and the curriculum lead. Your track is deliberately narrow — it is the part of the portal that runs the room.

## Your learning path

1. **Get oriented.** Read *Welcome to Peekaboo*, sign in, and complete *Your first 30 minutes*.
2. **Mark the room present** — see *How to mark a child present for today*.
3. **Handle the exceptions** — see *How to mark a child absent or excused*.
4. **Correct a mistake** — see *How to correct a child's attendance times or notes (today)*.
5. **See only your room** — see *How to filter the attendance board by classroom*.
6. **Close the day** — see *How to check a child out at the end of the day*.
7. **Learn the health procedure** — read the health and temperature note at the top of this document before you write a single report.
8. **Write a report** — see *How to create and save a daily report as a draft*. Fill the **Note to parents** box first, every time.
9. **Send it home** — see *How to publish a daily report (without emailing it)*, then *How to publish a daily report and email it to the parent in one step*.
10. **Look back** — see *How to review a daily report for a past date*.
11. **Know the children** — see *How to search and filter the children list*, then *How to record an allergy, medical condition or special diet* so you can read a child's health record confidently.

## You are ready when you can…

- Mark the room present and handle absences without asking for help.
- Complete a full daily report for a child, including a clear note to parents.
- Say out loud where a temperature or an injury is recorded, and who you tell in person.
- Check a child out correctly at home time.
- Find a child's allergies and authorised pickup people in seconds.

---

# Accountant

## Who this is for

You keep the books. Fees going out to families, money coming back in, suppliers being paid, costs being recorded and categorised, and a clear view of who is in arrears. Most of your work is in two sidebar groups — **Account** for money in, **Finance** for money out — and a few catalogue pages that need setting up once and maintaining thereafter.

## What you will use every day

- **Invoices** — billing a named child for one or more services.
- **Receipts** — recording money received from a family.
- **Balance by Class** — what each child still owes, worked out as billed minus received. This is your arrears list.
- **Expenses** — costs incurred, with the receipt scan attached and a category against each.
- **Live Dashboard** — the **Finance** tab, for billed against collected and outstanding balances.

## What you will use occasionally

- **Services** — the catalogue of billable things: tuition, transport, uniform, each with a default fee and VAT treatment.
- **Payment Modes** — the tender types you accept, such as cash, cheque and transfer.
- **Expense Types** — the categories your costs are filed under.
- **Suppliers** — the vendor list.
- **Purchases** — what was ordered, line by line.
- **Payment Voucher** — money actually leaving the bank, split across payment lines.
- **Children** — to confirm a child's details before invoicing.
- **My Center** — the VAT number and the bank or payment details parents see.
- **Packages** — the nursery's own software subscription, if that is yours to manage.

Two points worth knowing before you start. **Suppliers, Purchases, Expenses and Payment Voucher are four independent records that happen to share a supplier** — a purchase does not flow into an expense, and an expense does not flow into a voucher. Record each one where it belongs. And payment is reconciled at the **child** level, in **Balance by Class**, rather than invoice by invoice — so a family's position is the total billed minus the total received, and that is the figure to quote when a parent asks what they owe.

## What you probably will not see

**CRM Leads** belongs to the registrar. The daily reports, observations, assessments and curriculum pages belong to the teaching team. **Staff** records, **Roles & Access** and **Referrals & Sources** belong to administration. **Centers** belongs to the principal.

Note one wide door in the other direction: because the arrears view sits alongside invoices and receipts, several colleagues may be able to see every child's balance. Treat that screen as sensitive in a shared office.

## Your learning path

1. **Get oriented.** Read *Welcome to Peekaboo*, sign in, and complete *Your first 30 minutes*.
2. **Learn the table controls once** — see *How to use the standard table controls (search, sort, page size, paging)*, then *How to export a finance table (Copy / PDF / Print / CSV)*. You will use both constantly.
3. **Build the fee catalogue** — see *How to add a service*. Do this before any invoicing; an invoice bills services, so the catalogue has to exist first.
4. **Set up how you take money** — see *How to add a payment mode*.
5. **Raise a bill** — see *How to create an invoice*.
6. **Send it** — see *How to print or email an invoice*.
7. **Take the money** — see *How to record a payment (create a receipt)*, then *How to print or email a receipt*.
8. **Reconcile** — see *How to view outstanding balances by class*, and read *How to mark an invoice as paid* to understand why a family's position is read at the child level rather than per invoice.
9. **Find anything again** — see *How to search and sort invoices or receipts*.
10. **Set up the cost side** — see *How to add a supplier*, then *How to create an expense type*.
11. **Record what you spend** — see *How to record an expense*, attaching the receipt scan.
12. **Order and pay** — see *How to raise a purchase*, then *How to create a payment voucher*.

## You are ready when you can…

- Add a new service with the correct fee and VAT, and use it on an invoice the same day.
- Invoice a child, email the invoice, record the receipt when the family pays, and confirm the balance has moved.
- Produce this month's arrears list from **Balance by Class** and export it.
- Record an expense with its receipt attached and the right expense type.
- Raise a purchase against a supplier and record the payment voucher when the money leaves.
- Explain the difference between the nursery's own software subscription under **Packages** and the fees you bill to parents.

---

# Admin

## Who this is for

You are the system administrator for the nursery. You are the person colleagues come to when they cannot see a page they need, when a new member of staff needs an account, or when the enquiry source list needs a new entry. Your job is less about daily records and more about making sure everybody else's portal is shaped correctly.

Two areas belong to you alone: **Roles & Access**, where what each role can open is decided, and **Referrals & Sources**, the "how did you hear about us?" lists. No amount of configuration can give those to another role, so if you are away, nobody can cover them.

## What you will use every day

- **Roles & Access** — the roles in your nursery and the modules each one may open.
- **Staff** — the employee roster, where accounts are created, roles are assigned, and passwords are set.
- **Live Dashboard** — the general picture.

## What you will use occasionally

- **Referrals & Sources** — maintaining the enquiry source lists that the registrar attaches to leads.
- **My Center** — your nursery's name, address, contact details, logo, VAT details and session times.
- **Packages** — the nursery's own subscription to the Peekaboo software.
- **Children** — when you need a record for support purposes.
- **Attendance Reports** — for a compliance or inspection request.
- **Calendar** and **Messages** — where you handle nursery-wide communication.

## What you probably will not see

The daily teaching pages — daily reports, observations and assessments — belong to the teaching team, and the invoicing and expense pages belong to the accountant, unless your nursery has specifically given you those too. **Centers**, the multi-centre screen, belongs to the principal alone; your own nursery's settings are on **My Center**.

One important limit to know about your own role: being the Admin does **not** by itself give you every other page. Beyond your two exclusive screens, you see what has been enabled for your role like anybody else.

## Four things to internalise before you change anything

1. **Permissions are per role, never per person.** Changing a role changes what every single person holding that role can see. If only one person should get something, create a role for that purpose and move them to it.
2. **Other people do not pick up your change until they sign out and sign back in.** Your own changes apply to you immediately, which is why a change can look like it worked for you and not for them. Always tell the person to sign out and back in before concluding anything.
3. **A brand-new role starts with nothing selected.** Anyone assigned to it will be able to open almost nothing until you grant its modules. Create the role and grant its access in one sitting.
4. **Some access is decided by which role a person holds, not by ticking modules.** **Roles & Access** and **Referrals & Sources** require the Admin role. **Centers** requires the Principal role. Ticking boxes will never grant those three — the person needs the role itself, which is set on their **Staff** record.

## Your learning path

1. **Get oriented.** Read *Welcome to Peekaboo*, sign in, and complete *Your first 30 minutes*.
2. **Understand how access works from the user's side** — see *How to find out what your role is and what you can access*. Every support question you get starts here.
3. **Create a staff account** — see *How to add a staff member (and create their portal login)*. This is where a person's role is set.
4. **Maintain the roster** — see *How to edit a staff member*, then *How to search and filter the staff list, and switch card/table view*.
5. **Reset a password** — see *How to change a staff member's password*. Note that staff cannot change their own password inside the portal; their only self-service route is the **Forgot?** link on the login screen. Treat the Staff page as sensitive, because passwords are visible there.
6. **Handle the "no role" case** — see *What to do if you see "Your role could not be determined"*. This is the single most common new-starter problem, and the fix is on the Staff record.
7. **Create a role** — see *How to create a role*.
8. **Grant its access** — see *How to change what a role can access*. Do this immediately after step 7.
9. **Answer "I can't see X"** — see *How to give a user access to a module*. Work out the module, find the person's role, grant it, and have them sign out and back in.
10. **Retire a role safely** — see *How to deactivate or delete a role*. Check the **Staff** page first to see who holds it; the portal will not warn you about the people affected, and the status toggle acts on a single click with no confirmation.
11. **Maintain the CRM lists** — see *How to add a referral or a communication source*. Only you can do this, so agree a turnaround with your registrar.
12. **Keep the centre record right** — see *How to update your centre's details*, then *How to add a session (daily timing)*. Sessions matter beyond settings: they are the timing options the registrar picks from when registering a child into a class, so if that list is empty, this is the fix.
13. **Manage the subscription** — see *How to buy or renew a subscription package*, if that is yours.
14. **Deactivate a leaver** — see *How to deactivate or delete a staff member*.

## You are ready when you can…

- Create a new member of staff with a working login and the correct role, and talk them through their first sign-in.
- Create a role and grant it exactly the pages that job needs.
- Take "I can't see the Invoices page" from report to resolution, including telling the person to sign out and back in.
- Explain why some access cannot be granted by ticking a module, and what has to change instead.
- Add a new referral source and confirm the registrar can attach it to a lead.
- Add an active session and confirm it appears as a timing option when a child is registered into a class.
- Say, without looking it up, that permissions are per role and never per person.

---

# Guest

## Who this is for

You have been given a deliberately limited account — perhaps you are a visiting inspector, an auditor, a trustee, or someone joining for a short project. You have been granted a small, specific set of pages for a specific purpose, and you are expected to look rather than to change.

Of all the roles, this is the one where local variation matters most. There is no standard guest setup; your nursery decides exactly what your account can open, and it may be a single page.

## What you will use every day

Whatever has been enabled for your visit. In practice a guest account is most often given some combination of:

- **Live Dashboard** — headline occupancy, attendance and finance figures.
- **Attendance Reports** — the read-only attendance history across a date range, for children or staff.
- One or two further pages relevant to why you are here.

## What you will use occasionally

Very little. If your work needs a page you do not have, ask your host rather than trying to reach it another way — it will not open.

## What you probably will not see

Most of the portal. The children's and staff records, the daily reports, the curriculum and assessment pages, the invoicing and expense pages, the admissions pipeline and all of the administration screens belong to the nursery's own staff. Your narrow menu is the account working exactly as intended.

## Your learning path

1. **Get oriented.** Read the *What Peekaboo is* and *What a normal day looks like* sections of *Welcome to Peekaboo*, so you understand the system you are looking at.
2. **Sign in** — see *How to sign in to the portal*.
3. **Find your role and your scope** — see *How to find out what your role is and what you can access*. Read your sidebar carefully; it is the complete list of what your visit covers.
4. **Set language and theme** — see *How to change the portal language (English / العربية)* and *How to switch between light and dark mode*.
5. **Read the summary figures** — see *How to read the Live Dashboard and switch its tabs*, then *How to change the dashboard's date range*.
6. **Work the tables** — see *How to use the standard table controls (search, sort, page size, paging)*.
7. **Pull an attendance history** — see *How to generate and export an attendance report*, if that is part of your remit.
8. **Print what you need** — see *How to print a page or record*.
9. **Sign out properly** — see *How to sign out*. Please use the **Logout** button every time, particularly if you are working on a nursery computer.

## You are ready when you can…

- Sign in, read your role badge and describe what your account covers.
- Navigate the sidebar and reach your permitted pages without help.
- Produce an attendance history for a date range and export or print it.
- Sign out cleanly at the end of each session.

---

# If your account has no role yet

Occasionally a brand-new account exists before a role has been set on it. If that is you, you will sign in successfully but see a message telling you that your role could not be determined, with a short grey line of detail beneath it.

This simply means the setup of your account is not finished. Nothing is wrong with your password and nothing is lost.

**What to do:**

1. Note the small grey line of detail shown beneath the message.
2. Send it to your administrator and ask them to set your role.
3. Once they confirm it is done, **sign out and sign back in**.

Signing out and back in on its own will not fix it — the role genuinely has to be set first. Once it is, you will land on the Live Dashboard normally, and you can start on the track above that matches your job.


---


<!-- ================= 03-how-to ================= -->

# How To — Everyday Tasks

This is your step-by-step library for the Peekaboo portal. Each entry is short and
self-contained. Bold text is exactly what you will see on screen, so you can scan a
page and match it.

The portal is at **[https://peek.peek-a-boo.app](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/login)**.
Each how-to below carries an **Open this page** link that takes you straight there. If
you have not used the portal yet today, you may be asked to sign in first.

A few things that apply everywhere:

- Pop-up confirmation messages appear in the **top-right corner of the screen** and fade
  after about four seconds. Green means saved, red means something needs your attention.
- The sidebar — the menu running down the left-hand edge of the screen in English, or the
  right-hand edge in Arabic — only lists the pages your role is allowed to open. Your menu
  may therefore look different from a colleague's. That is normal.
- Buttons for editing and deleting are small round icons on each row — an **amber
  pencil** to edit and a **red bin** to delete.
- **Nothing is saved until you press the save button.** It is usually called **Save**,
  **Update** or **Save Changes**, and it usually sits at the bottom of the form. If you
  leave the page without pressing it, your typing is lost.

### A few words used in these steps

- A **dropdown** is a short list that drops down when you click a box, so you can pick
  one option from it.
- A **toggle** is a small switch you click to turn a setting on or off.
- A **pill** or a **chip** is a small oval label, often coloured, showing a status or a
  name. Some can be clicked to filter a list.
- A **tab** is one of a row of headings across the top of a form or page. Clicking one
  shows a different set of fields, and switching between them keeps what you have typed.
- A **dialog** is a small window that opens on top of the page and must be dealt with
  before you carry on.
- A **CSV** is a plain spreadsheet file. It opens in Excel perfectly well.
- **VAT** is value added tax. **TRN** is your nursery's tax registration number.
- **SEN** means special educational needs.
- A **pop-up** is a window your browser opens on top of the current one. Printing uses
  one, so if nothing happens when you print, your browser has blocked it.
- The **clipboard** is your computer's temporary memory for copied text. Copy puts the
  rows there, and you paste them into a spreadsheet.

---

## 1. Getting started

### How to sign in to the portal
**Who this is for:** Everyone with a Peekaboo staff account.
**Before you start:** A username and password from your administrator. Your username is
not necessarily your email address.
**Open this page:** [Sign in](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/login)
**Steps:**
1. Open the portal address in your browser. The **Login** screen appears.
2. Type your username into **Username**.
3. Type your password into **Password**.
4. Tick **Remember me** if you wish.
5. Complete the **"I'm not a robot"** security check. This step is compulsory.
6. Click **Sign In**. The button reads **"Signing in..."** while it works.
**You will know it worked when:** A green **"Signed in successfully"** message appears
and the **Live Dashboard** opens.
**Good to know:**
- The security check resets after every unsuccessful attempt, so tick it again before
  you retry.
- There is no eye icon to reveal the password as you type it, so type carefully.
- Closing the browser does not sign you out. On a shared reception computer, always use
  the **Logout** button at the end of your shift.
- The login screen is always in English. You can switch to Arabic once you are inside.

### How to sign out
**Who this is for:** Everyone.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Where:** the **Logout** button at the far end of the top bar — on the right-hand end in English, on the left-hand end in Arabic. You can do this from any page.
**Steps:**
1. Find **Logout** at the far end of the top bar — on the right in English, on the left
   in Arabic. It is outlined in red with a door icon.
2. Click **Logout**.
**You will know it worked when:** The button reads **"Logging out..."** and you are
returned to the Login screen.
**Good to know:**
- There is no "are you sure?" question. One click signs you out straight away, so save
  your work first.
- On a narrow phone screen only the icon shows, without the word.
- This is the only reliable way to end your session on a shared computer.

### How to reset a forgotten password
**Who this is for:** Everyone. You do not need to be signed in.
**Before you start:** Know your username. Your account needs a working email address
on file.
**Open this page:** [Sign in](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/login)
**Steps:**
1. On the Login screen, click the small **Forgot?** link beside **Password**.
2. Type your username into **Username**.
3. Complete the **"I'm not a robot"** check — on this screen it sits *below* the button.
4. Click **Continue**.
5. Check your email for a 6-digit code and type it into **Email verification code**.
6. Click **Verify Email**.
7. The code carries over into **Authentication code**. Correct it here if you mistyped.
8. Type a new password of at least 6 characters into **New password**.
9. Type the same password again into **Confirm password**.
10. Click **Reset Password**.
**You will know it worked when:** A green **"Password reset successfully."** message
appears and you go straight into the portal.
**Good to know:**
- The code is confirmed at the final step rather than at **Verify Email**, so if the
  last step reports a problem, the most likely cause is a mistyped code. Use **Resend
  Code** to get a fresh one and start again from the code step.
- Do not refresh the browser part-way through — you will return to a blank first step.
- If the masked email shown ends in `@peekaboo.com`, treat it as a placeholder rather
  than your real address.

### How to change the portal language
**Who this is for:** Everyone.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Where:** the flag button in the top bar, near the right-hand end. You can do this from any page.
**Steps:**
1. In the top bar, find the button showing a flag and the current language — **English**
   or **العربية**.
2. Click it. A small dropdown opens.
3. Click **English** or **العربية**. The active one carries a small dot.
**You will know it worked when:** The whole portal changes language immediately. In
Arabic the layout mirrors: the sidebar moves to the right, text aligns right and arrows
flip direction.
**Good to know:**
- Your choice is saved for that browser on that device only. Choosing Arabic at
  reception will not change it on your own laptop.
- A few items stay in English in Arabic mode — the **Peekaboo** name, some month labels
  and a handful of status words. Anything not yet translated shows in English rather
  than appearing blank.
- Clicking outside the dropdown closes it without changing anything.

### How to switch between light and dark mode
**Who this is for:** Everyone, including on the Login screen.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Where:** the small square icon button in the top bar, between the flag button and **Logout**. You can do this from any page.
**Steps:**
1. Click the small square icon button in the top bar, between the language picker and
   **Logout**.
2. Alternatively, scroll to the bottom of the sidebar to the panel labelled **Theme**
   with the sub-text **"Light - Dark"** and click the toggle there. Both do the same job.
**You will know it worked when:** The page recolours instantly.
**Good to know:**
- The icon shows the mode you will switch *to*, not the one you are in. A **moon** means
  you are currently in light mode; a **sun** means you are in dark mode.
- Your choice is remembered in that browser for next time.
- If you have never chosen, the portal follows your computer's own light/dark setting.

### How to collapse or expand the sidebar
**Who this is for:** Everyone.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Where:** the small square panel button at the very start of the top bar — the far left-hand end in English, the far right-hand end in Arabic. You can do this from any page.
**Steps:**
1. Click the small square panel icon at the start of the top bar — far left in English,
   far right in Arabic.
2. Alternatively press **Ctrl+B**, or **Cmd+B** on a Mac, from anywhere in the portal.
**You will know it worked when:** On a computer the sidebar shrinks to a narrow strip of
icons. On a phone or narrow tablet it slides out as a panel over the page instead.
**Good to know:**
- Hover over an icon while collapsed to see its name.
- Your choice is remembered for about a week.
- On a phone, tapping any sidebar link closes the sidebar for you.

### How to find out what your role is
**Who this is for:** Everyone.
**Before you start:** A screen at least 1024 pixels wide — the role badge is hidden on
smaller screens.
**Where:** the top bar, just to the right of the filled **Peekaboo Care** badge. You can do this from any page.
**Steps:**
1. Look at the top bar. Beside the filled **Peekaboo Care** badge there is an outlined
   badge showing your role name, for example **Teacher** or **Accountant**.
2. To see what that role can reach, look at the sidebar. It lists only the pages you are
   permitted to open.
**You will know it worked when:** You can read your role name in the top bar.
**Good to know:**
- If the badge is missing, your browser window is too narrow. Widen it or use a larger
  screen.
- Whole groups disappear from the sidebar if you have access to nothing inside them, so
  two colleagues in different roles see very differently shaped menus.
- Access is set per role, never per person. To change what you can see, an administrator
  changes your role's access or moves you to a different role.

### How to understand your menu
**Who this is for:** Everyone, especially new starters.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Where:** the sidebar — the menu running down the left-hand edge of the screen in English, or down the right-hand edge in Arabic.
**Steps:**
1. Look down the sidebar. Pages are gathered into named groups.
2. Match what you want to do to its group:
   - **Center** — your nursery's own details, sessions and subscription.
   - **Operations** — the **Live Dashboard**, **Children Attendance**, **Staff
     Attendance**, **Daily Reports** and the **Calendar**.
   - **People** — **Children** and **Staff**.
   - **Communication** — **Messages**.
   - **Early Childhood** — **Classes**, **Age Groups**, **Curriculum**, **Learn Area**,
     **Attainment**, **Assessments**, **Observations** and **Assessment Report**.
   - **Finance** — money going out: **Suppliers**, **Expense Types**, **Expenses**,
     **Payment Voucher** and **Purchases**.
   - **Account** — money coming in: **Services**, **Invoices**, **Payment Modes**,
     **Receipts** and **Balance by Class**.
   - **Reports & Admin** — **Attendance Reports** and **Roles & Access**.
   - **Admissions CRM** — **CRM Leads** and **Referrals & Sources**.
3. Click a group to open it, then click the page you need.
**You will know it worked when:** The page opens in the main area to the side of the
sidebar.
**Good to know:**
- There is no search box that covers the whole portal. Each page has its own search, so
  go to the right page first and search there.
- The browser tab always reads "Peekaboo Portal" whichever page you are on. Read the
  heading at the top of the content area to confirm where you are.
- If you land on a page reading **"Not Found"**, you have most likely mistyped an address
  or followed an old bookmark. Click **Open App** to return to the Live Dashboard. Your
  session is untouched and you are still signed in.

### How to get help when a page says your role could not be determined
**Who this is for:** Everyone; an administrator completes the fix.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [Staff](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/staff)
**Steps:**
1. If you see **"Your role could not be determined"**, note the small grey line beneath
   the message.
2. Send that line to your administrator.
3. Your administrator opens **Staff** from the sidebar, under **People**, finds you,
   clicks the amber pencil, chooses a value in **Role** and clicks **Update**.
4. Sign out and sign back in.
**You will know it worked when:** You reach the Live Dashboard normally.
**Good to know:**
- Signing out and back in before a role is assigned will not change anything, so contact
  your administrator first.
- This is a different message from **"Access denied"**, which means you do have a role
  but it does not include that particular page.

### How to change a staff member's password
**Who this is for:** Administrators and anyone who manages staff records.
**Before you start:** The staff member must already exist.
**Open this page:** [Staff](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/staff)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Staff** from the sidebar, under **People**.
2. Find the person and click the **amber pencil** on their card or row.
3. On the **Details** tab, clear the **Password** field and type the new password. Click
   the eye icon to check what you typed.
4. Click **Update**.
**You will know it worked when:** A green **"Updated successfully"** message appears.
Give the new password to the staff member.
**Good to know:**
- Staff cannot change their own password from inside the portal. They can use the
  **Forgot?** link on the login screen, or ask you to set one here.
- Passwords are visible in the Password column of the Staff table view, so treat that
  screen as confidential and avoid leaving it open at reception.
- If the save reports a missing field, it names one at a time. Fill it in and click
  **Update** again.

---

## 2. Children and families

### How to add a new child
**Who this is for:** Registrars, principals and anyone who manages child records.
**Before you start:** Nothing for the basic record. To place the child into a class you
will also need an active class and an active session.
**Open this page:** [Children](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/children)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Children** from the sidebar, under **People**.
2. Click **Add Child**. It is at the top right-hand corner of the page.
3. On the **Child Data** tab, type the child's full name into **Child Name**.
4. Pick the date of birth in **Birth Date**.
5. Optionally set **Gender**, **Nationality**, **First Language** and **Social Security**.
6. Choose the **Parent** from the dropdown (the short list that drops down when you click
   the box).
7. If the parent is not listed, click the small **[+]** button immediately beside the
   **Parent** box and add them first. They are then selected for you automatically.
8. Set the seven toggles (small on/off switches) to match the family's signed consent
   form: **Status**, **Take Pictures**, **Pic Nursery**, **Pic Media**, **Pic Social**,
   **Staff Child** and **Child SEN**.
9. Optionally fill **Notes** and **Medical Notes**.
10. On a wide screen, add a photo by clicking the upload box, or by clicking
    **Browse ...** beneath it.
11. Click **Save Child** at the bottom of the form.
**You will know it worked when:** A green **"Saved successfully"** message appears and
the child shows in the list as a card with a green **Active** pill.
**Good to know:**
- Only **Child Name** and **Birth Date** are required. **Child Picture** carries a red
  asterisk but a child saves perfectly well without a photo.
- Save the child before you add health records, pickup people or registrations. Those
  three tabs need a saved record first. After saving, reopen the child from the list to
  carry on.
- The photo box responds to clicking rather than dragging, and is only available on
  larger screens. Use a desktop computer if you need to add a picture.
- One parent is linked per child. Record the second parent inside the parent's own
  record, and anyone else who may collect the child on the **Pickup** tab.

### How to edit a child's details
**Who this is for:** Registrars, principals and anyone who manages child records.
**Before you start:** The child record exists.
**Open this page:** [Children](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/children)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Children** from the sidebar, under **People**.
2. Find the child using the **"Search by name or ID…"** box or the dropdowns.
3. Click the child's card, or the **amber pencil** on the card.
4. Change what you need on the **Child Data** tab.
5. Click **Update Child**.
**You will know it worked when:** A green **"Updated successfully"** message appears and
you return to the list.
**Good to know:**
- Use **Cancel** rather than the browser Back button to return to the list, because the
  form opens in place of the list.
- **Update Child** saves the **Child Data** tab. The **Health**, **Pickup** and
  **Registrations** tabs save through their own **Save** buttons and are stored as soon
  as you save them, so **Cancel** does not undo those.
- Treat the age shown on a child's card as a rough guide and work from **Birth Date**
  when the exact age matters.

### How to search and filter the children list
**Who this is for:** Everyone who works with children's records.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [Children](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/children)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Children** from the sidebar, under **People**.
2. Type into **"Search by name or ID…"**. It matches the child's name or their **Social
   Security** number and ignores capital letters.
3. Use **All Status** to show only **Active** or **Inactive** children.
4. Use **All Classes** to narrow to one classroom.
5. Use **All Age Groups** to narrow to one age band.
6. Click **Clear** to start again.
**You will know it worked when:** The cards narrow instantly and the count in the header
updates.
**Good to know:**
- **Clear** resets the search, class and age filters. Set **All Status** back by hand if
  you have changed it.
- If nothing matches you will see **"No children match the current filters."**
- This page shows cards rather than a sortable table, and the list is read on screen.

### How to deactivate a child who has left
**Who this is for:** Registrars and principals.
**Before you start:** The child record exists.
**Open this page:** [Children](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/children)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Children** from the sidebar, under **People**, and open the child.
2. On the **Child Data** tab, switch the **Status** toggle **off**.
3. Click **Update Child**.
**You will know it worked when:** The card's pill turns red and reads **Inactive**.
**Good to know:**
- This is the recommended way to retire a child who has left. They stay in the system
  with all their history, and you can reactivate them if the family returns.
- Deactivated children drop out of the **Active** filter, so your day-to-day lists stay
  clean.

### How to delete a child record
**Who this is for:** Registrars and principals.
**Before you start:** Be certain — deletion is permanent.
**Open this page:** [Children](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/children)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Children** from the sidebar, under **People**.
2. Click the **red bin** on the child's card.
3. A dialog headed **"Delete Child"** asks you to confirm.
4. Click **Yes**.
**You will know it worked when:** **"Deleted successfully"** appears and the child is
removed from the list.
**Good to know:**
- If you see **"Cannot delete this child because related records exist."**, the child
  still has linked information such as registrations, pickup people, health records,
  attendance history or invoices. Deactivating is the better choice in that case, and it
  keeps the history you may need later.
- Clicking **Cancel**, or clicking the dimmed background outside the box, abandons the
  deletion quietly. Nothing is removed.

### How to record an allergy, medical condition or special diet
**Who this is for:** Registrars, room leads and principals.
**Before you start:** The child record must already be saved.
**Open this page:** [Children](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/children)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Children** from the sidebar, under **People**, and open the child.
2. Click the **Health** tab. The tabs run across the top of the child's record.
3. Click **Add Health**.
4. Choose the condition in the **Health Description** dropdown.
5. If the condition is not in that list, click **Add New Health**.
6. Type the condition into **Health Name**.
7. Choose a **Health Type** — **Allergy**, **Chronic**, **Skin**, **Neurological**,
   **Dietary**, **Respiratory** or **Other**.
8. Click **Save** to add it to the list, then choose it in **Health Description**.
9. In **Comment**, type the child-specific detail: severity, symptoms, the medication to
   give and exactly what staff must do.
10. Click **Save**.
**You will know it worked when:** A green **"Health added."** message appears, the row
shows in the table, and a red allergy warning appears on the child's card in the
Children list.
**Good to know:**
- This is the safety-critical route. Only a structured Health record raises the red
  allergy warning on the Children list, so record allergies here rather than only in
  **Medical Notes**.
- For a food intolerance or special diet, choose the **Dietary** type. There is no
  separate diet screen.
- **Comment** is the only place child-specific detail can go, so be explicit and write
  it as if for someone covering the room for the first time.
- To correct a health record, delete it with the red bin and add it again. Copy the
  **Comment** text somewhere first so the safety-critical wording is not retyped from
  memory.
- **Add New Health** adds the condition to the list used for every child, so keep the
  naming consistent across your nursery.
- It is worth also summarising the key points in **Medical Notes** on the **Child Data**
  tab and clicking **Update Child**, so it appears on the main record too.

### How to add an authorised pickup person
**Who this is for:** Registrars and room leads.
**Before you start:** The child record must already be saved.
**Open this page:** [Children](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/children)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Children** from the sidebar, under **People**, and open the child.
2. Click the **Pickup** tab.
3. Click **Add Pickup**.
4. Type the person's full name into **Name**.
5. Choose the **Relation** — **Father**, **Mother**, **Grandfather**, **Grandmother**,
   **Uncle**, **Aunt**, **Sibling**, **Nanny** or **Other**.
6. Enter the **Phone** and **Email**. Both are optional, but a phone number is strongly
   advised.
7. Click **Save**.
**You will know it worked when:** A green **"Pickup added."** message appears and the
person shows in the table.
**Good to know:**
- Four things are stored: name, relation, phone and email. The list itself is the whole
  record, so keep it accurate and review it each term.
- Remove people promptly when authorisation is withdrawn — click the red bin and confirm
  **"Delete Pickup Person"**.
- Pickup entries can be edited. Click the amber pencil, change the fields and click
  **Update**.
- There is no limit on how many people you add.
- Check this list yourself at the door before releasing a child, and confirm identity in
  person. The attendance board records the time of collection rather than the name.

### How to add a parent or guardian
**Who this is for:** Registrars and principals.
**Before you start:** You must be inside a child's form.
**Open this page:** [Children](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/children)
**Steps:**
1. Open a child, or start a new one. You must be on the **Child Data** tab.
2. Click the **[+]** button next to the **Parent** dropdown.
3. In the **Add New Parent** window, type their name into **Parent Name**.
4. Enter their email address into **Email 1**.
5. Optionally fill **Phone 1**, **Parent Country**, **Address**, **Phone 2**, **Email 2**
   and **Social Security**.
6. Check the login details. **User Name** is filled in for you. To get a different one,
   click the circular-arrow button beside the box, or type your own.
7. Replace the pre-filled **Password** with one of your own choosing.
8. Leave **Use Apps** on **Yes** to give the parent access to the parent app.
9. Set **Use Mail** to control whether they receive email notifications.
10. Leave **Parent Status** on **Active**.
11. Under **Spouse Contact Details**, record the second parent — **Spouse Name**, **Spouse
    Phone** and **Spouse Email**.
12. Under **Emergency Contact Details**, fill **Contact Name**, **Phone 1** and **Phone 2**.
13. Click **Save**. This closes the **Add New Parent** window.
14. Back on the main form, click **Save Child** or **Update Child** to attach the parent
    to the child.
**You will know it worked when:** A message confirms the parent was added, the parent is
selected in the **Parent** dropdown, and the child saves with them attached.
**Good to know:**
- The last step is essential. Creating the parent alone does not link them — you must save the
  child afterwards.
- Change the pre-filled password before saving. It is there for convenience rather than
  security.
- Only **Parent Name** and **Email 1** are required, but fill in the phone numbers and
  emergency contact while you have the family in front of you. Take care to get the
  details right at this point, as parent records are created here and cannot be edited
  afterwards from elsewhere in the portal.
- One parent is linked per child. The second parent goes in **Spouse Contact Details**,
  and anyone else who may collect the child goes on the **Pickup** tab.

### How to register a child into a class
**Who this is for:** Registrars and principals.
**Before you start:** An active class and an active session must exist. The child must be
saved, with their **Parent** already set.
**Open this page:** [Children](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/children)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Children** from the sidebar, under **People**, and open the child.
2. Click the **Registrations** tab.
3. Click **Add Registration**.
4. Choose the **Class**. Only active classes are listed.
5. Set the **From / To Date** pair. Both are required.
6. Under **Days**, click each day the child attends. Use **Select all** to tick them all.
