7. Choose the **Timing** — the session, shown for example as `Morning (08:00–12:00)`.
8. Leave **Status** on **Active**.
9. Click **Save**.
**You will know it worked when:** A green **"Registration added."** message appears and
the row shows with a green **Active** badge. The child now appears in attendance, daily
reports and observation lists for that class.
**Good to know:**
- Set the child's **Parent** and save the child before adding the registration. The
  parent is copied onto the registration, and this is what allows daily reports to be
  emailed to the family later.
- Check the **To** date falls after the **From** date before you save.
- The **Days** you tick genuinely matter. A child enrolled only Monday and Wednesday will
  not appear in Daily Reports on a Tuesday, which is exactly as intended.
- A child may hold several registrations, which is useful when they change room mid-year.
- This is where a child is enrolled into a class. The **No. of Children** figure on the
  Classes page is a capacity you type yourself rather than a live count.
- If the **Class** list is empty, create an active class first. If the **Timing** list is
  empty, create an active session on **My Center**.

---

## 3. Staff

### How to add a staff member and create their portal login
**Who this is for:** Administrators and principals.
**Before you start:** The roles you want to assign must already exist.
**Open this page:** [Staff](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/staff)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Staff** from the sidebar, under **People**.
2. Click **Add Staff**.
3. On the **Details** tab, complete the seven required fields: **Employee Name**,
   **Nationality**, **DOB**, **Phone 1**, **Email 1**, **User Name** and **Role**.
4. Choose the **Role** first.
5. Then click the circular-arrow button next to **User Name**, so the username it
   generates reflects that role.
6. Replace the default **Password** with a real one. Click the eye icon beside the box to
   check what you typed.
7. Set **Gender**, **Employee Status**, **Employee Code**, **Phone 2**, **Email 2** and
   **Address** as needed.
8. Switch to the **More...** tab, at the top of the form.
9. On that tab, add **Emergency Contact Name**, **Emergency Contact Number**,
   **Capabilities**, **Joining Date**, **Employee Last Day**, **Employee Note**, **Social
   Security**, **Degree** and **Diploma**. All are optional.
10. Click **Save** — the same button is available from either tab.
11. Give the new member their **User Name** and **Password**.
**You will know it worked when:** A green **"Added successfully"** message appears and
the person shows in the list with a green **Active** pill. They can sign in straight away.
**Good to know:**
- Adding a staff member is how you create their portal login. There is no separate
  user-creation screen.
- The **Role** you pick decides what they can see in the portal.
- Usernames must be unique across the nursery. If the save is refused, try a different
  username.
- Record qualifications as text in **Capabilities**, **Degree** and **Diploma**. The
  photo is the only file this page accepts, so keep certificates in your usual HR file.
- Check **Joining Date** and **Employee Last Day** read the right way round before saving.
- If the save reports a missing field, it names one at a time. Fill it in and click
  **Save** again.

### How to edit a staff member
**Who this is for:** Administrators and principals.
**Before you start:** The staff record exists.
**Open this page:** [Staff](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/staff)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Staff** from the sidebar, under **People**.
2. Find the person and click the **amber pencil** on their card or row.
3. Change what you need across the **Details** and **More...** tabs. Switching tabs keeps
   what you have typed.
4. Click **Update**.
**You will know it worked when:** A green **"Updated successfully"** message appears.
**Good to know:**
- Changing someone's **Role** changes what they can open in the portal. Ask them to sign
  out and back in to pick up the change.
- Both tabs carry their own **Update** and **Cancel** buttons — either works.

### How to deactivate a staff member who has left
**Who this is for:** Administrators and principals.
**Before you start:** The staff record exists.
**Open this page:** [Staff](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/staff)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Staff** from the sidebar, under **People**.
2. Find the person and click the **thumbs-up icon** on their card or row. The tooltip
   reads **"Deactivate"** when they are active and **"Activate"** when they are not.
**You will know it worked when:** The status flips immediately and **"Status updated"**
appears.
**Good to know:**
- This is the recommended way to retire a leaver while keeping their history.
- There is no confirmation step, so check you have the right person before clicking.
- Click the same button again to reactivate someone who returns.

### How to delete a staff member
**Who this is for:** Administrators and principals.
**Before you start:** Be certain — deletion is permanent.
**Open this page:** [Staff](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/staff)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Staff** from the sidebar, under **People**.
2. Click the **red bin** on the person's card or row.
3. Confirm the **"Delete Employee"** dialog by clicking **Yes**.
**You will know it worked when:** **"Deleted successfully"** appears.
**Good to know:**
- If you see **"Employee could not be deleted"**, the person is linked to other records
  such as attendance history. Deactivate them instead — it keeps the history intact and
  removes them from active lists.

### How to search and filter the staff list
**Who this is for:** Administrators and principals.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [Staff](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/staff)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Staff** from the sidebar, under **People**.
2. Click the icon buttons in the header to choose **Table view** or **Card view**. Card
   view is what you see by default.
3. Switch to **table view** — the search and filter controls live there.
4. Type into the box labelled **Search:** to match the employee name.
5. Use the **All Roles** dropdown to narrow by role.
6. Use the **All** dropdown to show **Active** or **Inactive** staff.
**You will know it worked when:** The table narrows instantly.
**Good to know:**
- Search matches the name, so search by name rather than email, phone or username.
- Card view keeps your filters applied but hides the controls. Switch back to table view
  to see or change what is filtered.
- Your view choice resets to Card each visit.
- The table view shows staff passwords, so avoid leaving it open on a shared screen.
- To keep a copy of staff data outside the portal, copy the rows you need from the table
  into a spreadsheet.

### How to assign a teacher to a class
**Who this is for:** Principals and anyone who manages classes.
**Before you start:** The class exists, and the person's **Role** on the Staff page is a
teaching role.
**Open this page:** [Classes](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/early-childhood/classes)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Classes** from the sidebar, under **Early Childhood**.
2. Find the class and click the **amber pencil** on the card, or the pencil in the
   **Actions** column in table view.
3. On the **Class Details** tab, scroll to **"Teachers assigned to this class"**.
4. Click a teacher's chip to assign them. The chip highlights and the counter on the
   right increases, reading for example **"3 selected"**.
5. Repeat for every teacher. Click a highlighted chip again to unassign.
6. Click **Save Changes**.
**You will know it worked when:** A green message confirms the class was updated, and the
teachers' avatars appear in the **Teachers** column of the class table.
**Good to know:**
- This is done on the class, not on the staff record.
- Several teachers can be assigned to one class.
- If someone is missing from the picker, check their **Role** on the **Staff** page —
  only teaching roles appear here.
- These assignments feed the classroom, observation and attendance views, so keep them
  current when rooms change.

---

## 4. Attendance

### How to mark a child present for today
**Who this is for:** Room leads, teachers and assistants.
**Before you start:** The child must be an active child record.
**Open this page:** [Children Attendance](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/attendance)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Children Attendance** from the sidebar, under **Operations**.
2. Check the date in the header band is today's date.
3. Optionally click a classroom pill to narrow the list, or leave **All** selected.
4. Find the child's row.
5. In the **Update Status** column, click **Present**, or **Late** if they arrived after
   the session start.
**You will know it worked when:** **"Attendance updated"** appears, the **Status** column
shows a green **Present** pill or an amber **Late** pill, and an **"In: HH:MM"** line
shows the arrival time.
**Good to know:**
- The arrival time is stamped with the current clock time. To record a different time,
  use **Edit**.
- This page always works on today. Mark the register on the day for the times to be right.
- Every active child appears each day. One with no entry yet shows a grey **Not Marked**.
- A late child counts as present in the Live Dashboard figures.
- On a narrow screen the **Update Status** column is hidden. Use **Edit** instead, or
  switch to a tablet or computer.

### How to mark a child absent or excused
**Who this is for:** Room leads, teachers and assistants.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [Children Attendance](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/attendance)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Children Attendance** from the sidebar, under **Operations**.
2. Find the child's row.
3. In the **Update Status** column, click **Absent** for an unexplained absence, or
   **Excused** for an authorised one such as a planned holiday.
4. To record the reason, click **Edit**, type into **Notes** and click **Save Changes**.
**You will know it worked when:** **"Attendance updated"** appears and the status pill
turns red for **Absent** or blue for **Excused**.
**Good to know:**
- **Absent** and **Excused** clear the check-in and check-out times, which is right for a
  child who never arrived. If a child was here and then went home, use **Edit** to set a
  check-out time instead of marking them absent.
- Use **Excused** for authorised absence — **On Leave** applies to staff rather than
  children.
- Notes are stored on the record for your own entry. Tell the room lead directly and note
  the reason in your usual day book as well, so the reason is visible to the team.
- Children whose register is never marked count in neither the Present nor the Absent
  totals on the dashboard, so mark every child each day for the figures to add up.

### How to correct a child's attendance times or notes
**Who this is for:** Room leads, teachers and assistants.
**Before you start:** The child appears on today's board.
**Open this page:** [Children Attendance](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/attendance)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Children Attendance** from the sidebar, under **Operations**.
2. Find the child's row and click **Edit**.
3. A panel headed **"Edit Child Attendance"** slides in from the side.
4. Set the **Status** dropdown first — **Present**, **Absent**, **Late** or **Excused**.
5. Set **Check-In** and **Check-Out** with the time pickers.
6. Add or amend the **Notes**.
7. Click **Save Changes**.
**You will know it worked when:** A message confirms the save and the row updates.
**Good to know:**
- Set the status first, then the times. Choosing **Absent** or **Excused** clears both
  time boxes and greys them out; choosing **Present** or **Late** fills the check-in with
  the current time if it was empty.
- If the time boxes are greyed out, switch the status to **Present** or **Late** first.
- Attendance for today is the record that counts, so correct entries the same day
  wherever you can.

### How to check a child out at the end of the day
**Who this is for:** Room leads, teachers and assistants.
**Before you start:** The child must be marked **Present** or **Late**, have a check-in
time, and not yet be checked out.
**Open this page:** [Children Attendance](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/attendance)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Children Attendance** from the sidebar, under **Operations**.
2. Find the child's row.
3. In the **Actions** column, click **Checkout**.
**You will know it worked when:** **"Checkout time marked"** appears and an
**"Out: HH:MM"** line appears under the status pill.
**Good to know:**
- The current clock time is stamped for you. To record a different time, use **Edit** and
  the **Check-Out** time picker.
- If the **Checkout** button is not showing, the child is not marked Present or Late, has
  no check-in time, or has already been checked out.
- Check the child's **Pickup** tab and confirm identity at the door before releasing them.
  The portal records the time of collection; who collected the child is confirmed by you
  in person and in your usual signing-out book.

### How to filter the attendance board by classroom
**Who this is for:** Room leads, teachers and assistants.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [Children Attendance](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/attendance)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Children Attendance** from the sidebar, under **Operations**.
2. Across the top of the table, click a classroom pill to show only that room's children.
3. Click **All** to clear the filter.
**You will know it worked when:** The table narrows to the selected classroom.
**Good to know:**
- A child with no class assigned appears under **All** with a **"-"** for Classroom, and
  is hidden by the specific classroom pills. If a child is missing from their room's list,
  check their registration.
- This board covers today, so use **Attendance Reports** to look back over a period.

### How to view attendance for a past date
**Who this is for:** Room leads, principals and administrators.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [Attendance Reports](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/attendance-reports)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Attendance Reports** from the sidebar, under **Reports & Admin**.
2. Leave the toggle on **Children**.
3. Set **From** and **To** to the dates you want.
4. Read the grid: **P** is Present, **A** is Absent, **L** is Late, **V** is Leave, **E**
   is Excused and **-** means not marked.
**You will know it worked when:** A grid appears with one row per child and one column
per day.
**Good to know:**
- This is your history view, and it is read-only. Because today's board is the place
  entries are made, mark the register accurately on the day.
- If you see **"No students or staff found for this period."**, widen the date range or
  clear the classroom filter.
- Weekend columns are tinted red and are left out of the totals.

### How to clock a staff member in
**Who this is for:** Room leads, principals and whoever runs the staff register.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [Staff Attendance](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/staff-attendance)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Staff Attendance** from the sidebar, under **Operations**.
2. Optionally click a role pill to narrow the list, or leave **All** selected.
3. Find the employee's row.
4. Click **Check in**.
**You will know it worked when:** **"Staff attendance saved."** appears, the check-in pill
shows the time in green instead of `--:--`, and the status becomes **Present**.
**Good to know:**
- The current clock time is stamped for you. If the **Check in** button is not showing, a
  time already exists — use **Edit** to change it.
- This page works on today.
- If someone is missing from the list, check their staff record is active.

### How to clock a staff member out
**Who this is for:** Room leads, principals and whoever runs the staff register.
**Before you start:** The employee must have a check-in time and no check-out time.
**Open this page:** [Staff Attendance](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/staff-attendance)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Staff Attendance** from the sidebar, under **Operations**.
2. Find the employee's row and click **Checkout**.
**You will know it worked when:** **"Staff attendance saved."** appears and the second
pill shows the time.
**Good to know:**
- If **Checkout** is not showing, they have not been checked in or are already checked out.
- Times are recorded for your records. Calculate hours worked in your own payroll process.

### How to mark a staff member absent or on leave
**Who this is for:** Room leads, principals and whoever runs the staff register.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [Staff Attendance](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/staff-attendance)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Staff Attendance** from the sidebar, under **Operations**.
2. Find the employee's row.
3. Click **Absent** if they did not attend, or **Leave** for authorised leave.
**You will know it worked when:** **"Staff attendance saved."** appears and a tick icon
appears on the button you clicked.
**Good to know:**
- These two buttons clear the check-in and check-out times, which is right for a full day
  away. If someone worked part of the day, use **Edit** instead so the times are kept.
- Use **Leave** for authorised absence — **Excused** applies to children rather than staff.
- The quick button reads **Leave** while the summary card and dropdown read **On Leave**.
  They mean the same thing.

### How to mark a staff member late or set exact staff times
**Who this is for:** Room leads and principals.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [Staff Attendance](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/staff-attendance)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Staff Attendance** from the sidebar, under **Operations**.
2. Find the employee's row and click **Edit**.
3. The **"Edit Staff Attendance"** dialog opens.
4. Set **Status** — **Present**, **Late**, **Absent** or **On Leave**.
5. Enter **Check-In** and **Check-Out** times as needed.
6. Add **Notes** if required.
7. Click **Save Changes**.
**You will know it worked when:** A message confirms the save and the row updates.
**Good to know:**
- **Late** is set here rather than from a quick button on the row.
- This dialog keeps whatever times are in the boxes, including when you choose **Absent**
  or **On Leave**. Clear the times by hand if the person was away all day.
- If the row was not marked before, the dropdown opens on **Present**.

---

## 5. Daily reports for parents

> **Recording illness, injury or temperature.** Write anything clinical or
> operational — a temperature reading, a bump, a child who seemed unwell — into the
> **Note to parents** box on the report, and tell the room lead in person before you go
> home. The mood and meal selections are a summary of the day for the family; the
> **Note to parents** box is where the important detail belongs, and it is the part
> parents read first. For an ongoing condition, allergy or medication, record it on the
> child's **Health** tab in **Children** so it raises a warning on their card.

### How to create and save a daily report as a draft
**Who this is for:** Teachers, room leads and assistants.
**Before you start:** The child must be registered into a class, with today among their
enrolled days.
**Open this page:** [Daily Reports](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/daily-reports)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Daily Reports** from the sidebar, under **Operations**.
2. Click **New Report**. It is at the top right-hand corner of the page.
3. Under **Select Class**, click the classroom pill.
4. In the left-hand child list, click the child's name.
5. Fill in the sections that appear — all are optional:
   - **Today I Was** — click **Great**, **Good**, **Okay**, **Unsettled** or **Upset**.
   - **Meals & Drinks** — for each meal click **All**, **Most**, **Some** or **None**,
     then set the time and type a comment. For **Milk**, type the quantity into the **ML
     amount** box.
   - **Nap Time** — click **Slept Well**, **Brief Nap**, **No Nap** or **N/A**, and fill
     in the **From** and **To** times.
   - **Daily Care** — type free text into **Water**, **Juice**, **Medicine** and
     **Hygiene**.
   - **Items Needed** — click **Yes** or **No** for **Diapers**, **Clothes**, **Wipes**
     and **Cream**.
   - **Teacher Note** — write the **Note to parents** first, then **Activities &
     behaviour**.
6. Click **Save Draft**.
**You will know it worked when:** **"Daily report saved"** appears, a green line reads
**"Report saved for {child name}"**, and a green tick appears beside the child's name in
the list.
**Good to know:**
- Write the **Note to parents** before **Activities & behaviour**. That box is the heart
  of the report and the one parents read first.
- Record any illness, injury or temperature reading in the **Note to parents** box, and
  tell the room lead in person. That is the correct procedure for anything a parent or
  your colleagues must know about.
- Choose a mood deliberately for every report. A report saved without one shows as
  **Good**, so pick the one that matches the day.
- For nap, **Slept Well** and **N/A** are the two selections that carry through most
  clearly. Add the detail — how long, how settled — into the nap times and the **Note to
  parents** box.
- Write refused meals into the meal comment box as well as clicking **None**, so the
  family sees it in words.
- Type the milk quantity into the **ML amount** box rather than relying on the quick
  buttons beside it.
- Photos and videos are sent to parents from **Messages** rather than attached here.
- A new report is written against today's date, so write each day's report on the day.
- If a child you have just saved still shows "Report not created", reload the page.

### How to publish a daily report
**Who this is for:** Teachers, room leads and assistants.
**Before you start:** You are in the New Report view with a child selected.
**Open this page:** [Daily Reports](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/daily-reports)
**Steps:**
1. Fill in the report.
2. Click **Publish**.
**You will know it worked when:** **"Daily report published"** appears and the child's
card shows a green **Published** badge instead of the amber **Draft**.
**Good to know:**
- **Publish** marks the report as finished. To send it to the family by email, use
  **Publish & Email**, or email it afterwards from the list.
- Within one sitting, **Save Draft** followed by **Publish** updates the same report. If
  you leave the New Report view and come back, reopen the report from the list rather
  than starting a new one, so you do not end up with two copies for the same day.

### How to publish a daily report and email it to the parent
**Who this is for:** Teachers, room leads and assistants.
**Before you start:** The child must have a linked parent with an email address.
**Open this page:** [Daily Reports](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/daily-reports)
**Steps:**
1. Fill in the report.
2. Click **Publish & Email** at the right-hand end of the button row.
3. The button shows a spinner while the report saves and sends.
**You will know it worked when:** A message reads **"Report published and emailed to {n}
recipient(s)"**.
**Good to know:**
- Check the child's **Parent** field has an email address before you rely on this.
- If the message tells you the report saved but the email was not sent, go to the list
  view and use the envelope icon to send it.

### How to email an already-saved daily report
**Who this is for:** Teachers, room leads and assistants.
**Before you start:** A report must already exist for that child on that date.
**Open this page:** [Daily Reports](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/daily-reports)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Daily Reports** from the sidebar, under **Operations**.
2. Set the date picker to the report's date.
3. Optionally choose a classroom from the **All Classrooms** dropdown.
4. Find the child's card in the left-hand list.
5. Click the **envelope icon** on the right of the card.
**You will know it worked when:** A message reads **"Report emailed to {n} recipient(s)"**
or **"Report sent successfully"**.
**Good to know:**
- A greyed-out envelope means no report exists for that child on that date. Create one
  first.
- If sending does not succeed, check the child has a parent with an email address on file.
- This is also how you re-send a report after editing the teacher note.

### How to review a daily report for a past date
**Who this is for:** Teachers, room leads and principals.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [Daily Reports](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/daily-reports)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Daily Reports** from the sidebar, under **Operations**.
2. Set the **date picker** to the date you want. Past and future dates are both allowed.
3. Optionally narrow by classroom using the **All Classrooms** dropdown.
4. Click the child's card.
5. Read the detail panel on the right.
**You will know it worked when:** The panel shows the mood chip, Meals, Daily Care, Items
Needed, Nap, Comment and Teacher Note sections.
**Good to know:**
- This page has a date picker, so past reports can be browsed freely.
- If a child does not appear, check their enrolment period covers that date and that
  their enrolled weekdays include that day.
- If the card shows **"Report not created"**, no report was written that day.
- If the list looks empty when the page first loads, pick a specific classroom from the
  dropdown.
- To confirm a family has seen something important, telephone them.
- To give a parent a copy, email the report to them rather than printing it.

### How to edit the teacher note on a report already sent
**Who this is for:** Teachers, room leads and principals.
**Before you start:** The report exists.
**Open this page:** [Daily Reports](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/daily-reports)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Daily Reports** from the sidebar, under **Operations**.
2. Set the date picker to the report's date.
3. Click the child's card to open the detail panel.
4. Scroll to the **Teacher Note** heading and click the **pencil icon** beside it.
5. Edit the text.
6. Click **Save**.
**You will know it worked when:** A message reads **"Report updated"**.
**Good to know:**
- Reload the page and read the note back to confirm your wording before you move on.
- This route updates the teacher note. To change meals, nap or mood, re-enter the report
  from the New Report view in one sitting.
- Editing the note does not re-send the email. Use the envelope icon afterwards if the
  parent should see the change.

---

## 6. Curriculum, observations and assessment

> **Set-up order.** Build the framework before teachers record anything: **Curriculum**,
> then **Age Groups**, **Attainment** and **Learn Area**, then **Aspects**, then
> **Classes**. Attainment levels must exist before an observation can be saved.

### How to create a curriculum
**Who this is for:** Principals and curriculum leads.
**Before you start:** Nothing — this is the top of the tree.
**Open this page:** [Curriculum](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/early-childhood/curriculum)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Curriculum** from the sidebar, under **Early Childhood**.
2. Use the right-hand form, headed **Add Curriculum**.
3. Type the framework's name into **Curriculum Name**.
4. Optionally fill **Description**.
5. Optionally click **Browse** to attach a cover image.
6. Leave **Enabled** ticked.
7. Click **Save Curriculum**.
**You will know it worked when:** A green **"Curriculum saved successfully."** message
appears and the record joins the left-hand list.
**Good to know:**
- If clicking **Save Curriculum** does not appear to do anything, check **Curriculum
  Name** is filled in — it is the one field that must have a value.
- Every age group, attainment level and learning area belongs to a curriculum, so start
  here when setting up the module.
- To edit, click the curriculum's name or its pencil icon. **Clear** returns the form to
  adding a new one.
- Read the status word rather than the colour of the pill beside it.
- Re-saving without picking a new image keeps the existing one.

### How to create an age group
**Who this is for:** Principals and curriculum leads.
**Before you start:** A curriculum must exist.
**Open this page:** [Age Groups](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/early-childhood/age-groups)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Age Groups** from the sidebar, under **Early Childhood**.
2. Use the right-hand form, headed **Add Age Group**.
3. Enter a short code into **Code**.
4. Choose the parent curriculum in **Curriculum**.
5. Enter the lower bound in months into **From Month**.
6. Enter the upper bound in months into **To Month**.
7. Optionally pick a **Color**.
8. Fill in **Description**.
9. Click **Save Age Group**.
**You will know it worked when:** A green **"Age group saved successfully."** message
appears and the group joins the left-hand list, sorted by From Month.
**Good to know:**
- Always fill in **Description**. The Classes page shows the description in its **Age
  Group** dropdown, so a blank one appears as an empty choice when creating a class.
- If clicking **Save Age Group** does not appear to do anything, check the **Code** field
  is filled in.
- To edit one, click its pencil icon — the heading changes to **Edit Age Group**.
  **Clear** abandons the edit.

### How to add a learning area
**Who this is for:** Principals and curriculum leads.
**Before you start:** A curriculum must exist.
**Open this page:** [Learn Area](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/early-childhood/learning-area)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Learn Area** from the sidebar, under **Early Childhood**.
2. Use the right-hand form, headed **Add Learn Area**.
3. Type the domain name into **Learn Area**, for example *Communication and Language*.
4. Choose the parent in **Curriculum**.
5. Optionally fill **Area Curriculum** — this short code is what teachers see on the
   filter pills in the Observations screen.
6. Optionally fill **Description** and pick a **Color**.
7. Leave **Enabled** ticked.
8. Click **Save Learn Area**.
**You will know it worked when:** A green **"Learn area saved successfully."** message
appears.
**Good to know:**
- If clicking **Save Learn Area** does not appear to do anything, check the **Learn Area** name is
  filled in.
- Save the learning area before adding aspects to it.
- Read the status word rather than the colour of the pill.

### How to add an aspect to a learning area
**Who this is for:** Principals and curriculum leads.
**Before you start:** The learning area must already be saved.
**Open this page:** [Learn Area](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/early-childhood/learning-area)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Learn Area** from the sidebar, under **Early Childhood**.
2. In the left table, click the **pencil** on the area you want. The right panel switches
   to **Edit Learn Area** and a **Learning Aspects** section appears.
3. Click **Add Aspect**.
4. Type the aspect name into **Aspect**.
5. Optionally fill **Description**.
6. Leave **Enabled** ticked.
7. Click **Save Aspect**.
**You will know it worked when:** A green **"Aspect saved successfully."** message
appears, the dialog closes and the aspect joins the list.
**Good to know:**
- The **Learning Aspects** section only appears once the area is saved, so save the area
  first and reopen it with the pencil.
- Fill in the **Aspect** name before saving — the dialog will attempt to save whatever it
  is given.
- Aspects are the parent of objectives, so check what is linked before deleting one.
- Use **Cancel** to close the dialog without saving.
- If the aspect list does not look updated, reload the page.

### How to set up attainment levels
**Who this is for:** Principals and curriculum leads.
**Before you start:** A curriculum must exist.
**Open this page:** [Attainment](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/early-childhood/attainment)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Attainment** from the sidebar, under **Early Childhood**.
2. Use the right-hand form, headed **Add Attainment**.
3. Type the level name into **Attainment**, for example *Emerging*.
4. Choose the parent in **Curriculum**.
5. Enter the numeric rank into **Mark** — 1 for the lowest level, rising for higher ones.
   This controls the display order.
6. Write what the level means into **Description**.
7. Pick a **Color** to represent the level.
8. Click **Save Attainment**.
9. Repeat for every level in your scale.
**You will know it worked when:** A green **"Attainment saved successfully."** message
appears and the level joins the left list, sorted by Mark.
**Good to know:**
- Whatever your nursery creates here is your scale. Set these up before teachers begin
  recording observations, because every objective on an observation carries an
  attainment level.
- Teachers see the **Description** in the observation dropdowns, so write the description
  to match the level name. Keeping the two consistent avoids confusion across the team.
- Give each level a different **Mark** so the order is predictable.
- If clicking **Save Attainment** does not appear to do anything, check the **Attainment** name is
  filled in.

### How to record an observation
**Who this is for:** Teachers and room leads.
**Before you start:** A class, a teacher, a registered child, a curriculum with a
learning area, at least one aspect, one age group, one objective and one attainment level.
**Open this page:** [Observations](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/early-childhood/observation)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Observations** from the sidebar, under **Early Childhood**.
2. Choose the classroom in **Class**.
3. Choose the observing teacher in **Teacher**.
4. Choose the child in **Children**. This list fills once a class is chosen.
5. Adjust **Date** if the observation was not today.
6. Give it a short name in **Observation Title**.
7. Type what you saw into **Observation**.
8. Note the planned follow-up in **Next Step**.
9. Link at least one objective — see the next how-to.
10. Click **Save Observation**.
**You will know it worked when:** **"Saved successfully"** appears, the form clears, and
the record joins the **Saved Observations** table below.
**Good to know:**
- Put everything you want kept into **Observation** and **Next Step**. Those two boxes
  are the record of what you saw.
- Saving commits the record straight away — there is no draft step.
- Pick the **Age Group** yourself; it is not filled in from the child's age.
- Photos and videos are shared with parents through **Messages** rather than attached to
  an observation.
- If the child dropdown does not fill after choosing a class, reload the page and try
  again.
- If your new record is missing from the list below, reload the page.

### How to link objectives to an observation
**Who this is for:** Teachers and room leads.
**Before you start:** You are part-way through filling in an observation.
**Open this page:** [Observations](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/early-childhood/observation)
**Steps:**
1. Work through the **Add Objectives** panel.
2. Under **Programs**, click the curriculum pill.
3. Under **Learn Areas**, click the learning area pill.
4. Under **Learn Aspects**, click the aspect pill.
5. Under **Age Groups**, click the age group pill. Objectives appear once both an aspect
   and an age group are selected.
6. Optionally choose a level in **Age Group Attainment** to pre-fill the rating for each
   objective you tick.
7. In the table, click an objective's text to select it. A tick appears and the row tints.
8. In that row's **Attainments** dropdown, choose the level the child demonstrated. Every
   selected objective needs one.
9. Repeat for as many objectives as apply. Use the page numbers beneath the table to see
   more.
10. Check the right-hand **View Selected Objectives** panel, which groups your picks and
    shows each rating in its colour.
11. To remove one, click the **trash** icon on its row in that panel.
**You will know it worked when:** The counter chip reads **"{n} selected"** and you can
save the observation.
**Good to know:**
- If the table reads **"No data available in table"**, choose both an aspect and an age
  group.
- Choosing an attainment selects the objective for you.
- You can pull objectives from several areas or aspects — change the pills and carry on,
  and your earlier picks are kept. Changing the **curriculum** starts the selection
  afresh, so settle on the curriculum first.
- Clicking an already-active pill deselects it.
- Objectives are read from your curriculum framework. If one is missing, ask your
  provider to add it.

### How to view, edit or delete a saved observation
**Who this is for:** Teachers, room leads and principals.
**Before you start:** At least one saved observation.
**Open this page:** [Observations](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/early-childhood/observation)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Observations** from the sidebar, under **Early Childhood**.
2. Scroll to the **Saved Observations** table.
3. Click the **eye** icon to view. A dialog headed **"Observation Details"** shows the
   Observation text, Next Step and the objective count. Click **Close** to dismiss it.
4. Click the **pencil** icon to edit. The form reloads with that record. Change the title,
   observation text, next step, date, objectives or attainments, then click **Save
   Observation**.
5. Click the **trash** icon to delete, then confirm.
**You will know it worked when:** Editing shows **"Saved successfully"** and deleting
shows **"Deleted successfully"**.
**Good to know:**
- Saved observations stay editable, so you can refine the wording later.
- **Class**, **Teacher** and **Child** are fixed once saved and show as **"(Cannot be
  changed)"**. If one of those is wrong, delete the record and enter it again.
- The view dialog shows the objective count rather than listing each one. Use the pencil
  to see the objectives themselves.
- The table lists every observation, so use your browser's find function on long lists.

### How to generate an assessment report
**Who this is for:** Teachers, room leads and principals.
**Before you start:** Children must be registered into the class for the period.
**Open this page:** [Assessment Report](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/early-childhood/assessment-report)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Assessment Report** from the sidebar, under **Early Childhood**.
2. Pick the start of the period in **From Date**.
3. Pick the end in **To Date**.
4. In the **Class** dropdown, choose a specific class.
5. Click **Generate Report**. The button reads **"Generating..."** while it works.
6. A **Generated Reports** table appears with one row per registered child. For the first
   child, choose a learning area in the **Area** dropdown.
7. Type into the **Comments** box. Click **+ Add Comment** for additional boxes.
8. Click **Save Comments** for that row.
9. Repeat for every remaining child.
**You will know it worked when:** **"Report generated successfully"** appears when you
click **Generate Report**,
each **Save Comments** shows **"Assessment saved"**, and the row moves into the saved
table below.
**Good to know:**
- Choose a specific class each time. The dropdown opens on **All classes**, which is a
  starting position rather than a selection, so pick your class before clicking
  **Generate Report**.
- Check **From Date** falls before **To Date**.
- Rows are saved one child at a time, so set aside a quiet slot for a full class.
- Blank comment boxes are ignored, so leave any you do not need.
- The date and class filters control what is generated rather than what the saved table
  below shows.

### How to edit comments on a saved assessment report
**Who this is for:** Teachers, room leads and principals.
**Before you start:** A saved assessment report row exists.
**Open this page:** [Assessment Report](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/early-childhood/assessment-report)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Assessment Report** from the sidebar, under **Early Childhood**.
2. Scroll to the lower saved-reports table. Use the **Search** box if needed.
3. Click **Show Comment** on the row to read the existing text.
4. Click **Update** to open the **Comment Editor**.
5. Edit the text.
6. Click **Save Comment**.
**You will know it worked when:** **"Comment saved successfully"** appears.
**Good to know:**
- Read the existing comments with **Show Comment** before you edit. Where a child has
  several comments, the editor brings them together into one box joined by commas and
  saves them back as a single comment, so decide on the final wording before saving.
- The **Search** box matches child name, class name, title, area and comments.

### How to export or print an assessment report
**Who this is for:** Teachers, room leads and principals.
**Before you start:** At least one saved assessment report row.
**Open this page:** [Assessment Report](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/early-childhood/assessment-report)
**Steps (the whole table):**
1. Open **Assessment Report** from the sidebar, under **Early Childhood**, and scroll to
   the saved-reports table.
2. Optionally narrow the rows using the **Search** box — exports follow the filter here.
3. Click one of the four buttons above the table:
   - **CSV** downloads a spreadsheet file.
   - **Copy** copies the rows to your clipboard and confirms **"Report rows copied"**.
   - **Print** opens a print dialog for a table titled **"Assessment Reports"**.
   - **PDF** opens the same print dialog.
**Steps (one child's formatted report):**
1. Find the child's row and, in the **Assessment Reports** column, click the **Assessment
   Report** button.
2. A new window opens with the formatted report and the print dialog appears.
3. Choose **"Save as PDF"** as the destination for a PDF file, or print it.
**You will know it worked when:** The file downloads, the clipboard fills, or the print
dialog opens.
**Good to know:**
- To get a PDF, choose **"Save as PDF"** as the destination in your browser's print
  dialog. That is the final step for both the **PDF** and **Print** buttons.
- If you see **"Could not open the PDF report window."**, allow pop-ups for the portal
  and try again.
- The four buttons are unavailable while the table is loading or empty.
- Exports contain the columns **Child**, **Class**, **Report**, **Area**, **From**, **To**
  and **Comments**.

### How to read the Assessments summary page
**Who this is for:** Principals and curriculum leads.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [Assessments](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/early-childhood/assessment)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Assessments** from the sidebar, under **Early Childhood**.
2. Read the three counters: **Total Assessments**, **Children Assessed** and **Total
   Comments**.
3. Click **Open Observations** or **Open Assessment Report** to go to the pages where the
   work is done.
**You will know it worked when:** You see three numbers and two navigation cards.
**Good to know:**
- **Total Assessments** counts generated assessment report rows. If your team has
  recorded observations but not yet generated reports, this reads zero — check the
  **Saved Observations** table on the Observations page for observation counts.
- The counters briefly show zero while the page loads. Give it a moment before reading
  them.

---

## 7. Messages and the calendar

> **What Messages is.** Messages is a one-way noticeboard to parents. You publish a post
> and the families you selected receive it. Parents do not reply inside it, so give a
> phone number in the post when you need an answer back.

### How to send a message to one parent
**Who this is for:** Room leads, registrars and principals.
**Before you start:** The child must exist and be linked to a parent.
**Open this page:** [Messages](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/messages)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Messages** from the sidebar, under **Communication**.
2. In the tab strip, click the type you want — **Announcement**, **Notification**,
   **Event**, **Newsletter**, **Media** or **Video Media**. The **Create {Type}** dialog
   opens.
3. In the left panel under **Select Classes**, click the child's class.
4. Under **Children**, tick the one child. The message reaches that child's parent.
5. Type a title into **Title**.
6. Add your **Description**.
7. Leave **Publish later** switched **off** to send immediately.
8. Click **Publish Now**.
**You will know it worked when:** A green **"Created successfully"** message appears and
the post joins the history below with a green **Published** dot.
**Good to know:**
- Pick the class first, then tick the children. Choosing a class clears any children you
  had already ticked.
- Ticking a child is what governs delivery — selecting only a class is not enough.
- Clicking a type tab both filters the history and opens the compose dialog. If you only
  wanted to filter, press Escape or click **Cancel**; the filter stays applied.
- Parents receive the post rather than joining a conversation, so include a phone number
  or invite them to call if you need a reply.
- If your new post is missing from the history, reload the page.

### How to message a whole class
**Who this is for:** Room leads, registrars and principals.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [Messages](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/messages)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Messages** from the sidebar, under **Communication**.
2. Click the type tab you want, for example **Notification**.
3. Under **Select Classes**, click the class. Do this first.
4. Above the children list, click **Select All**. Every child in that class is ticked.
5. Enter a **Title**.
6. Add your **Description**.
7. Click **Publish Now**.
**You will know it worked when:** A green **"Created successfully"** message appears.
**Good to know:**
- The per-child ticks govern delivery, so **Select All** after choosing the class is the
  step that reaches everyone in the room.
- Choosing a class clears any children already ticked, so never tick before choosing.
- Use **Unselect All** to clear the ticks and start again.

### How to message every parent in the nursery
**Who this is for:** Principals and registrars.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [Messages](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/messages)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Messages** from the sidebar, under **Communication**.
2. Click the type tab you want.
3. Do not select any class. Leave **Select Classes** untouched.
4. Click **Select All** above the children list. With no class chosen, the list holds
   every child in the nursery.
5. Enter a **Title** and a **Description**.
6. Click **Publish Now**.
**You will know it worked when:** A green **"Created successfully"** message appears.
**Good to know:**
- Check the ticked count looks like your whole roll before publishing.
- For anything urgent, follow up by telephone as well.

### How to attach a file to a message
**Who this is for:** Room leads, registrars and principals.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [Messages](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/messages)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Messages** and click **Announcement**, **Notification**, **Event** or
   **Newsletter**. These four offer the general attachment box.
2. Choose the recipients and enter a **Title**.
3. Find the **Attachment** box and click it.
4. Pick your file, for example a PDF newsletter. The filename appears in the box.
5. Optionally use the **Icon** box for a small thumbnail image.
6. Click **Publish Now**.
**You will know it worked when:** The post appears in the history with a paperclip chip
showing the filename, which opens in a new tab when clicked.
**Good to know:**
- Keep attachments small. A large file takes a long time to upload and may be refused, so
  compress a big PDF before attaching it.
- For photos use the **Media** tab, and for video use **Video Media**.

### How to send photos to parents
**Who this is for:** Teachers, room leads and principals.
**Before you start:** Your images must be JPG or PNG and under 1 MB each.
**Open this page:** [Messages](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/messages)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Messages** and click the **Media** tab.
2. Select the class, then tick the children, or click **Select All**.
3. Enter a **Title**.
4. Click the **Media images** box and select your photos.
5. Check the thumbnails. To drop one, click the small **X** on its corner.
6. To add more, click the box again — new files join the existing ones.
7. Click **Publish Now**.
**You will know it worked when:** The post appears in the history with up to four
thumbnails; a fifth and beyond show as a **+N** overlay.
**Good to know:**
- Keep each image under 1 MB. Resize photos before you start, because if one file is over
  the limit the selection is cleared and you re-pick them all.
- The message names the first oversized file, so check the others too when resizing.
- JPG and PNG are the formats to use here.
- Check the family's photo consent settings on the child's record before sending images.

### How to send a video to parents
**Who this is for:** Teachers, room leads and principals.
**Before you start:** The video must be MP4, WebM, OGG or MOV and under 35 MB.
**Open this page:** [Messages](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/messages)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Messages** and click the **Video Media** tab.
2. Select the class and tick the children.
3. Enter a **Title**.
4. Click the **Video** box and choose one file. The helper text reads **"Select one MP4,
   WebM, OGG, or MOV video (max 35 MB)."**
5. Click **Publish Now**.
**You will know it worked when:** The post appears in the history with a video player.
**Good to know:**
- One video per post. Publish a second post for another clip.
- A video is required for this post type, so choose the file before clicking publish.
- Trim or compress long clips to stay under 35 MB.

### How to write a message now and publish it later
**Who this is for:** Room leads, registrars and principals.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [Messages](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/messages)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Messages** and click your chosen type tab.
2. Select recipients and fill in the **Title** and **Description**.
3. Switch **Publish later** **on**.
4. Click **Publish Now** — the button keeps this label. The post is saved unpublished.
5. To find it again, switch the **Unpublished** toggle **on** in the history header. It
   shows an amber dot.
6. When you are ready, click the **pencil** icon, switch **Publish later** **off**, and
   click **Update & Send**.
**You will know it worked when:** The dot turns green and the post reads **Published**.
**Good to know:**
- **Publish later** is off to begin with, so posts go out immediately unless you switch
  it on.
- This holds a post ready rather than scheduling it, so diarise a reminder to come back
  and publish it at the right moment.
- If you cannot find your saved post, check the **Unpublished** toggle is switched on.

### How to find an old message
**Who this is for:** Anyone who uses Messages.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [Messages](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/messages)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Messages** from the sidebar, under **Communication**.
2. Type a word from the title into **Keyword**.
3. Optionally set **From** and **To** dates.
4. Press Enter, or click the round magnifier button.
5. Narrow further with the **Types** dropdown.
6. Switch **Unpublished** on to include unpublished items.
7. Use the **<** and **>** buttons at the bottom to page through, ten per page.
8. Switch between **Timeline View** and **List View** using the buttons in the history
   header.
**You will know it worked when:** The history narrows to your search.
**Good to know:**
- **Keyword**, **From** and **To** apply when you press Enter or click the magnifier.
  **Types** and **Unpublished** apply as soon as you change them.
- Searches cover your whole archive rather than just the page you are looking at.
- The **"{n} published on this page"** line counts the current page of ten.

### How to edit a message you have already sent
**Who this is for:** Room leads, registrars and principals.
**Before you start:** The post exists.
**Open this page:** [Messages](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/messages)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Messages** and locate the post.
2. Click the **pencil** icon on the card, or on the row in **List View**.
3. The dialog reopens as **"Edit {Type}"** with the content loaded.
4. Make your changes.
5. Click **Update & Send**.
**You will know it worked when:** **"Updated successfully"** appears and the change
applies to the published post.
**Good to know:**
- Editing changes the post in place. Parents who have not yet opened it see the new
  version; anyone who already read it saw the earlier wording, so send a fresh post for
  an important correction.
- Clearing an icon or attachment during an edit removes it from the post.

### How to delete a message
**Who this is for:** Room leads, registrars and principals.
**Before you start:** The post exists.
**Open this page:** [Messages](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/messages)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Messages** and locate the post.
2. Click the **bin** icon.
3. Confirm by clicking **Yes**.
**You will know it worked when:** **"Deleted successfully"** appears and the post
disappears from the history.
**Good to know:**
- Deleting removes the post going forward. A parent who already opened it will have seen
  it, so telephone the family if a correction matters.
- If the post is still listed, reload the page.

### How to confirm a message went out
**Who this is for:** Anyone who uses Messages.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [Messages](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/messages)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Messages** from the sidebar, under **Communication**.
2. Find the post in the history.
3. Check the status dot is **green**. Hovering shows **Published**.
**You will know it worked when:** The dot is green and reads **Published**.
**Good to know:**
- The status tells you the post was released to families.
- For anything urgent — a closure, a collection change, a health matter — telephone the
  parent as well and note the call in your day book.

### How to add a calendar event
**Who this is for:** Principals, registrars and room leads.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [Calendar](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/calendar)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Calendar** from the sidebar, under **Operations**.
2. Click **Add Event**, at the top right-hand corner of the page.
3. Alternatively, click directly on the day you want in the month grid. That fills in
   the date for you.
4. Type the **Event title**.
5. Choose the **Event type**: **Holiday**, **Trip**, **Performance**, **Meeting**,
   **Class**, **Assessment**, **Reminder** or **Other**.
6. Set the **Start date** and **End date**.
7. Set **Start time** and **End time**.
8. Optionally add a **Description**.
9. Under **Branches**, click one class to limit the event to it, or leave all unselected
   for a nursery-wide event.
10. Click **Save event**.
**You will know it worked when:** **"Event created"** appears and the event shows as a
coloured chip on the month grid.
**Good to know:**
- The buttons under **Branches** are your classes, and one can be chosen at a time.
- Leaving the times blank makes the event run from 00:00 to 23:59, which is how you
  record an all-day event.
- Create each occurrence of a weekly event separately. A multi-day event is one
  continuous event.
- Check **End date** is not before **Start date**.
- To be certain parents see something, also publish it as an **Event** post from
  **Messages**, which targets parents directly.

### How to edit a calendar event
**Who this is for:** Principals, registrars and room leads.
**Before you start:** The event exists.
**Open this page:** [Calendar](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/calendar)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Calendar** from the sidebar, under **Operations**.
2. Click the event in the month grid. The detail dialog opens.
3. Click **Edit**.
4. Change what you need.
5. Click **Save event**.
**You will know it worked when:** **"Event updated"** appears.
**Good to know:**
- You can also switch to **Agenda** view and click the **pencil** icon on the event's row.
- If you changed a date or time parents rely on, send a **Messages** post as well.

### How to delete a calendar event
**Who this is for:** Principals, registrars and room leads.
**Before you start:** The event exists. Check you have the right one.
**Open this page:** [Calendar](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/calendar)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Calendar** from the sidebar, under **Operations**.
2. Click the event to open its detail dialog.
3. Read the title and date to confirm it is the right event.
4. Click **Delete**.
**You will know it worked when:** **"Event deleted"** appears and the event disappears.
**Good to know:**
- The event is removed the moment you click **Delete**, so confirm the title and date
  first. The same applies to the bin icon in **Agenda** view.
- If you remove an event families were told about, let them know with a **Messages** post.

### How to move around the calendar and filter it by class
**Who this is for:** Anyone who uses the Calendar.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [Calendar](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/calendar)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Calendar** from the sidebar, under **Operations**.
2. Use the **<** and **>** chevrons beside the month name to change month.
3. Click **Today** to jump back to the current month.
4. Use **Month** for the grid or **Agenda** for a chronological list.
5. To filter, click a class name in the filter strip. Click **All Classes** to clear it.
**You will know it worked when:** The grid or agenda updates immediately.
**Good to know:**
- **Month** and **Agenda** are the two views. Agenda is the easier read on a phone, and
  is what you get by default on a narrow screen.
- Filtering by class still shows nursery-wide events, which is usually what you want.
- Each day cell shows up to three events; extras appear as **"+N more"**.
- The week starts on Monday.

---

## 8. Admissions enquiries

> **The pipeline.** A lead moves through these stages: **New Lead**, **KYC**, **Book a
> Tour**, **Admission**, **Accepted**, **Evaluation**, **Registered**, **Lost** and
> **Rejected**. Those are the names shown on the badges in the **CRM Leads** list, and
> they are the ones to use when talking to colleagues.

### How to add a new enquiry
**Who this is for:** Registrars and admissions staff.
**Before you start:** At least one referral and one communication source must exist.
Both are required on every lead.
**Open this page:** [CRM Leads](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/crm/leads)
**Steps:**
1. Open **CRM Leads** from the sidebar, under **Admissions CRM**.
2. On the toolbar above the list, click **Add**.
3. Work down the form from the top. In **1. Child Details**, enter **Child Name**, **Date
   of Birth** and **Nationality/Country**.
4. Check the **Gender** radio button.
5. Optionally add a **Child Photo** under 5 MB, **Age Group**, **Child ID Number**, the
   language dropdowns, **Previous Nursery**, **Transport Type**, the **Staff Member's
   Child** toggle and **General Notes**.
6. In **2. Primary Parent/Guardian**, enter **Name**, **Primary Phone** and **Primary
   Email**.
7. If there is a second guardian, in **3. Second Parent/Guardian** click **Add Second
   Parent or Guardian** and fill the three fields. Leave the section open once you have
   filled it in.
8. Optionally complete **4. Emergency Contact**.
9. In **5. Photography Consent**, set **"Allow photos?"** and tick the boxes under
   **SPECIFIC USES APPROVED:** to match what the family agreed.
10. In **6. Special Educational Needs**, set **"Has SEN?"** deliberately for this child.
    It opens on **Yes**, so switch it off where that does not apply, and fill in **SEN
    Details** where it does.
11. In **7. Enquiry Details**, choose **Referral Source** and **Record Source**.
12. Optionally set **Enquiry Date**, and in **8. Visit & Joining** the **Joining Date**,
    **Visit Notes** and **Joining Notes**.
13. Click **Save** in the sticky bar at the bottom.
**You will know it worked when:** A green **"New lead saved successfully."** message
appears and the lead shows in the list with a **New Lead** badge.
**Good to know:**
- Fill the form from top to bottom. Required fields are reported one at a time in order,
  so working downwards is much quicker.
- The eight required fields are **Child Name**, **Date of Birth**, **Nationality/Country**,
  the guardian's **Name**, **Primary Phone**, **Primary Email**, **Referral Source** and
  **Record Source**.
- Set the photo consent boxes deliberately for every family, because they are submitted
  as they stand.
- Check email addresses and phone numbers carefully as you type them.
- If either dropdown in section 7 is empty, ask your administrator to add entries on
  **Referrals & Sources** before you continue.

### How to edit an enquiry
**Who this is for:** Registrars and admissions staff.
**Before you start:** The lead exists.
**Open this page:** [CRM Leads](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/crm/leads)
**Steps:**
1. Open **CRM Leads** from the sidebar, under **Admissions CRM**.
2. Find the lead — type into **"Search by name or ID..."** if needed.
3. Click the **amber pencil** circle at the left of the lead's row, or click the lead's
   name and then the amber pencil in the profile panel.
4. Change whatever is needed across the eight sections.
5. Click **Update**.
**You will know it worked when:** A green **"Lead updated successfully."** message
appears.
**Good to know:**
- The photo area starts empty each time you edit. Leave it untouched to keep the stored
  photo, or choose a new file to replace it.
- **Cancel** discards your changes straight away, so click **Update** before leaving.
- To record why a family did not enrol, add a **Note** to the lead.

### How to search, sort and filter the enquiries list
**Who this is for:** Registrars and admissions staff.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [CRM Leads](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/crm/leads)
**Steps:**
1. Open **CRM Leads** from the sidebar, under **Admissions CRM**.
2. Type into **"Search by name or ID..."**. It matches student name, parent name, phone,
   email and lead id.
3. Click **Sort** to reverse the order of **Student Name**. The chevron shows the
   direction.
4. Click **Filter** to open the panel, then set any of **Date Range**, **Approved Date
   Range**, **Age Upto**, **Nationality**, **Source**, **Referral**, **Joining Date**,
   **Grade**, **Admission**, **Flow** or **Operator**.
5. Click **Apply Filter** to tidy the panel away — filtering is already live as you
   change each control.
6. Click **Reset** to clear the search box and every filter.
**You will know it worked when:** The list narrows. An empty result reads **"No leads
match these filters."**
**Good to know:**
- Sorting works on **Student Name**.
- To find leads at a particular stage, sort by name and scan the coloured badges, or read
  the pipeline counts on the Live Dashboard's **CRM** tab.
- **Reset** becomes available once a filter or search term is active.

### How to change an enquiry's status
**Who this is for:** Registrars and admissions staff.
**Before you start:** The lead exists.
**Open this page:** [CRM Leads](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/crm/leads)
**Steps:**
1. Open **CRM Leads** from the sidebar, under **Admissions CRM**.
2. Find the lead and click its coloured **status badge** on the right of the row.
3. Pick the new status from the menu: **New Lead**, **Book a Tour**, **Tour Taken**,
   **Offer Letter**, **Admission**, **Lost**, **Accepted** or **Rejected**.
4. The **Move Lead** dialog opens, showing the lead's details.
5. Set **Set Reminder Date** — your follow-up date.
6. Optionally type an **Admin Comment** describing why.
7. To send the parent a specific message, expand **Advanced Options** and write it under
   **Parent Email Message**. If left blank, the **Admin Comment** is sent instead.
8. Click **Confirm & add task**.
**You will know it worked when:** A green **"Lead moved and task added successfully."**
message appears, the badge changes colour, and a task is recorded against the lead.
**Good to know:**
- Every status change records a task and a reminder date, which gives you a full history
  of each enquiry.
- Two menu entries produce a differently named badge: choose **Tour Taken** and the badge
  reads **KYC**; choose **Offer Letter** and it reads **Evaluation**. These are the same
  stage under both names.
- **Registered** is set from the task dialog rather than this menu.
- Read the badge text rather than its colour when scanning the list, since **Lost** is the
  only one shown in red.
- If you get an amber message rather than green, the status changed but the parent email
  did not go out. Telephone or email the family directly.

### How to book and record a nursery tour
**Who this is for:** Registrars and admissions staff.
**Before you start:** The lead exists.
**Open this page:** [CRM Leads](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/crm/leads)
**Steps (book the tour):**
1. Open **CRM Leads** and click the lead's **status badge**.
2. Choose **Book a Tour**.
3. In the **Move Lead** dialog, set **Set Reminder Date** to the day you want prompting.
4. Put the agreed visit date and time in the **Admin Comment** box, for example *"Tour
   booked Tue 14 Oct, 10:30"*.
5. Click **Confirm & add task**.
**Steps (record the exact slot):**
1. Click the lead's **name** to open its detail view.
2. In the middle **Tasks** column, click the **+** button.
3. Set **Lead Status** to **Book a Tour**.
4. Set **Date** to the exact date and time of the visit.
5. Set **Set Reminder Date**, then click **Add Task**.
**Steps (after the tour):**
1. Return to the list, click the lead's **status badge** and choose **Tour Taken**.
2. Set **Set Reminder Date** for your next follow-up and click **Confirm & add task**.
3. To record how the visit went, open the lead for editing, go to **8. Visit & Joining**
    and write in **Visit Notes**, or add a note.
**You will know it worked when:** The lead moves through the pipeline and each movement
is captured as a task.
**Good to know:**
- A tour is recorded as a status plus a task. The **Date** field on a task is where the
  precise date and time belong.
- Also put the tour in your **Calendar** so the team can see it, and keep your own diary
  for reminders.
- Once you have chosen **Tour Taken**, the badge reads **KYC**, which is the same stage
  under the other name.

### How to add a note to an enquiry
**Who this is for:** Registrars and admissions staff.
**Before you start:** The lead exists.
**Open this page:** [CRM Leads](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/crm/leads)
**Steps:**
1. Open **CRM Leads** from the sidebar, under **Admissions CRM**.
2. Click the lead's **name** to open its detail view.
3. Look at the **Notes** column on the right.
4. Click into **Add New Note** and type the note.
5. Click **Add Note**.
**You will know it worked when:** **"Note added successfully."** appears and the note
shows with today's date.
**Good to know:**
- Notes are dated today automatically. If you are writing up an earlier conversation,
  mention the date in the text of the note.
- To change a note, click the **amber pencil** beside it, edit and click **Update Note**.
  To remove one, click the red bin and confirm.
- Notes are the place to record why a family did not enrol, and anything else you want
  the next person to know.

### How to create a follow-up task on an enquiry
**Who this is for:** Registrars and admissions staff.
**Before you start:** The lead exists.
**Open this page:** [CRM Leads](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/crm/leads)
**Steps:**
1. Open **CRM Leads** and click the lead's **name**.
2. In the middle **Tasks** column, click the **+** button.
3. Choose the **Lead Status** this follow-up relates to — **New Lead**, **KYC**, **Book a
   Tour**, **Admission**, **Accepted**, **Evaluation**, **Registered**, **Lost** or
   **Rejected**.
4. Set **Date** — when the task itself happens.
5. Set **Set Reminder Date** — your follow-up date.
6. Type an **Admin Comment** describing what needs doing.
7. Optionally drag files onto the **Attachment** area, or click it to browse.
8. Optionally write the parent's email message in the editor on the right.
9. Click **Add Task**.
**You will know it worked when:** **"Task added successfully."** appears and the task
shows in the lead's Tasks column and the list view's Tasks panel.
**Good to know:**
- Record progress by adding a new task at the next status. That builds a clear history of
  the enquiry and keeps the latest step at the top.
- Write a colleague's name into the **Admin Comment** when the follow-up is theirs, and
  tell them directly.
- Parent emails are sent when a task is created, so write the message before clicking
  **Add Task**.
- **Registered** is set from this dialog.
- The Tasks panel on the list page shows the ten most recent tasks across all leads.

### How to mark an enquiry as lost or rejected
**Who this is for:** Registrars and admissions staff.
**Before you start:** The lead exists.
**Open this page:** [CRM Leads](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/crm/leads)
**Steps:**
1. Open **CRM Leads** and click the lead's **status badge**.
2. Choose **Lost** if the family withdrew, or **Rejected** if the nursery declined.
3. Set **Set Reminder Date** — use today for a closed enquiry.
4. Record the reason in the **Admin Comment** box.
5. Click **Confirm & add task**.
6. Optionally add a fuller explanation as a note.
**You will know it worked when:** The badge turns red for **Lost**, or shows **Rejected**.
**Good to know:**
- The **Admin Comment** is where the reason is captured, so write it clearly enough for
  a colleague reading it months later.
- Closed enquiries stay in the list as your record of the conversation.
- Read the badge text rather than the colour, since **Rejected** and **New Lead** share
  the same shade.

### How to record that an enquiry has been accepted
**Who this is for:** Registrars, admissions staff and principals.
**Before you start:** The lead exists.
**Open this page:** [CRM Leads](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/crm/leads)
**Steps:**
1. Open **CRM Leads** and click the lead's **name**.
2. Click the **Approve** button, at the top right-hand corner of the lead's detail view.
   It reads **"Approving..."** while it works.
**You will know it worked when:** **"Lead approved successfully."** appears, the status
becomes **Accepted**, and the button reads **"Approved"**.
**Good to know:**
- **Approve** sets the status to **Accepted** in one click, without a task or reminder.
- If you want the change recorded as a task with a follow-up date and a parent email, use
  the status badge menu and choose **Accepted** instead.
- Accepting records the decision. Creating the child's record is the next step.

### How to move an accepted family into enrolment
**Who this is for:** Registrars and principals.
**Before you start:** The lead has reached **Accepted**, and you have access to
**Children**.
**Open this page:** [CRM Leads](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/crm/leads)
**Steps:**
1. Open **CRM Leads** and click the lead's **name**.
2. Note down what you need from the detail panel: **Student Name**, **Parent Name**,
   **Phone**, **Email**, **DOB**, **Nationality** and **Grade**. Open the lead for editing
   to copy the guardian details, emergency contact, photo-consent settings and SEN
   information.
3. Open **Children** from the sidebar, under **People**.
4. Create the child record, entering the details you gathered.
5. Add the parent, any health records, pickup people and the class registration.
6. Return to **CRM Leads**, click the lead's **name**, then the **+** in the **Tasks**
   column.
7. Set **Lead Status** to **Registered**, fill **Date** and **Set Reminder Date**, add an
   **Admin Comment** such as *"Child record created in Children module"*, and click **Add
   Task**.
**You will know it worked when:** The child exists in **Children** and the lead shows
**Registered**.
**Good to know:**
- Enter the details into **Children** yourself, checking each one against the enquiry as
  you go. This is a good moment to confirm spellings and contact numbers with the family.
- Keep the lead. It is your history of the enquiry, its notes and its tasks.
- **Registered** is set from the task dialog rather than the badge menu.

### How to add a referral or a communication source
**Who this is for:** Administrators and principals.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [Referrals & Sources](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/crm/referrals-sources)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Referrals & Sources** from the sidebar, under **Admissions CRM**.
2. Decide which list you need. **Referrals** on the left is how the family heard about
   you. **Communication Sources** on the right is which channel the enquiry arrived
   through.
3. Click the round **+** button in that panel's heading.
4. Type the **Name**.
5. Choose the **Type**. For a referral: **Digital**, **Printed**, **Signs**,
   **Recommendation**, **Event** or **Others**. For a communication source: **General**,
   **Admission** or **Communication**.
6. Click **Add**.
**You will know it worked when:** **"Added successfully."** appears and the entry is
available in the lead form's dropdowns.
**Good to know:**
- This page is open to administrators. If you do not see **Referrals & Sources** in your
  sidebar, ask your administrator to add the entry for you.
- Every lead needs a referral and a record source, so keep both lists populated — an
  empty list stops admissions staff saving new enquiries.
- Check the top-five ranking cards at the top of the page before deleting an entry, so
  you know how widely it is used.
- Each panel has its own search box, matching that list's names.
- To rename one, click the amber pencil and click **Save Changes**.

### How to attach a referral or source to an enquiry
**Who this is for:** Registrars and admissions staff.
**Before you start:** The referral or source must exist.
**Open this page:** [CRM Leads](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/crm/leads)
**Steps:**
1. Open **CRM Leads** and open the lead for editing.
2. Scroll to **7. Enquiry Details**.
3. Choose **Referral Source** — how the family heard about you.
4. Choose **Record Source** — the channel the enquiry came through.
5. Click **Update**.
**You will know it worked when:** A green **"Lead updated successfully."** message
appears.
**Good to know:**
- Both are set when the lead is created, so this is how you correct them later.
- The names differ slightly between screens: **Referral Source** appears as **Referral**
  on the detail view and filters, and **Record Source** appears as **Source**.
- Change these one lead at a time, from the edit form.

### How to edit an admissions email template
**Who this is for:** Registrars, admissions staff and principals.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [CRM Leads](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/crm/leads)
**Steps:**
1. Open **CRM Leads** from the sidebar, under **Admissions CRM**.
2. Click **Email Templates**, at the top right-hand corner of the page header.
3. In the left **Lead Status** column, click the status whose email you want to change.
4. Edit the **Subject** field.
5. Edit the message in the editor beneath it.
6. Click **Update**.
7. Click **CRM · Leads** at the top left to return to the list.
**You will know it worked when:** **"Email template updated successfully."** appears.
**Good to know:**
- Click **Update** before switching to another status, so each edit is saved as you go.
- Both **Subject** and the message body need content.
- Editing a template does not send anything. It is used the next time a lead is moved to
  that status or a task is added at that status.
- Write the text in full rather than relying on automatic name substitution, and check
  the wording with your provider if you want personalised fields.

### How to export admissions figures
**Who this is for:** Principals and registrars.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [Live Dashboard](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Live Dashboard** from the sidebar, under **Operations**.
2. Click the **CRM** tab.
3. Click the **Details** view pill.
4. Click **Export to Excel** in the month navigation bar. It appears while the Details
   view is active.
**You will know it worked when:** A file downloads with one row per centre and age band,
and the columns **Center**, **Age**, **New Leads**, **KYC**, **Tour Taken**,
**Application**, **Evaluation**, **Approved**, **Registered** and **Total Lost**.
**Good to know:**
- The file is a CSV, which opens in Excel perfectly well.
- Three column names differ from the badges on the CRM Leads page: **Tour Taken** here is
  **Book a Tour** there, **Application** is **Admission**, and **Approved** is
  **Accepted**. Rejected enquiries are counted separately from these columns.
- The export covers all centres and bands regardless of the month arrows.
- For a list of individual enquiries, use the search and filters on **CRM Leads** and read
  or copy what you need from the screen.

---

## 9. Invoices and payments

> **How money in fits together.** Set up a **Service**, raise an **Invoice** for a child,
> record a **Receipt** when the family pays, and read the family's position on **Balance
> by Class**. Receipts are recorded against a child, so **Balance by Class** is where you
> see what a family owes overall.

### How to add a service
**Who this is for:** Accountants, principals and administrators.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [Services](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/services)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Services** from the sidebar, under **Account**.
2. Click **Add Service**. It is at the top right-hand corner of the page.
3. Enter the name into **Services Name**, for example *Tuition Term 1*.
4. Enter the standard price charged to parents into **Services Fee**.
5. Enter what it costs the nursery into **Services Cost**. Enter `0` if you do not track
   this.
6. Describe it in **Services Description**.
7. Choose **Services Type** — **Services**, **Inventory**, **Resources** or **Expense**.
8. Leave **Services MOQ** at `0` unless advised otherwise. It needs a value.
9. Set **Tax Exempt** to **Yes** if the service carries no VAT.
10. Enter the VAT percentage into **Services VAT**, between 0 and 100. Enter `5` for 5%.
11. Click **Save**.
**You will know it worked when:** The service appears in the table.
**Before it appears on an invoice or purchase form, reload that page.**
**Good to know:**
- Changing a service's fee or VAT applies to new invoices. Invoices already raised keep
  the price they were saved with, which is what you want for your records.
- **Services Name** and **Services Description** hold up to 255 characters each.
- Leave **Services MOQ** and **Services VAT** filled in — both need a value to save.
- The search box matches service name and type.

### How to create an invoice
**Who this is for:** Accountants, registrars and principals.
**Before you start:** The child must exist, and at least one service must be configured.
**Open this page:** [Invoices](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/invoices)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Invoices** from the sidebar, under **Account**.
2. Click **Add Invoice**, at the top right-hand corner of the page. The **Add New
   Invoice** form opens.
3. Set the **Date**.
4. Choose the **Type**.
5. Choose the **Child** being billed.
6. Leave **Tax Exempt** on **No** unless this invoice carries no VAT.
7. In the **Services** table, on the first row, choose a **Service**. **Fee** and **VAT
   (%)** fill in automatically.
