8. Set **QTY**. Adjust **Fee** if charging a non-standard price. Enter **DISC (%)** if a
   discount applies, otherwise leave `0`.
9. **DISC Amnt**, **VAT Amount** and **Total** calculate themselves.
10. To bill more than one thing, click **Add Service** and repeat steps 7 and 8. To drop a
    row, click the **×** at its end.
11. Check **Total VAT** and **TOTAL** in the footer.
12. Click **Save**.
13. Find the new invoice in the left-hand list and click it to preview.
**You will know it worked when:** The invoice appears as a card showing the date, the
child's name, a sky-blue **INV** badge and the amount, with the full invoice on the right.
**Good to know:**
- Reopen the invoice from the list after saving and check it reads as you expect. That
  quick check is worth making part of your routine.
- VAT is charged on the discounted amount. For example, a fee of 1000 with a 10% discount
  and 5% VAT gives a discount of 100, a base of 900, VAT of 45 and a total of 945.
- Discounts are a percentage per line. Apply the same percentage to each line if you want
  a whole-invoice discount.
- Toggling **Tax Exempt** here keeps your lines and sets VAT to zero, so it is safe to
  change part-way through.
- Each invoice is created for one child at a time. For a whole class, work down your
  register and raise them one by one.
- **Add Invoice** closes the form if you click it while the form is open.
- Newly added children and services appear once you reload this page.

### How to add a payment mode
**Who this is for:** Accountants and administrators.
**Before you start:** Get the ID values from whoever maintains your chart of accounts.
**Open this page:** [Payment Modes](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/payment-modes)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Payment Modes** from the sidebar, under **Account**.
2. Use the **Add Payment Mode** form on the right.
3. Enter the account number into **Account Number**.
4. Enter the name staff will see into **Description**, for example *Cash*, *Cheque* or
   *Bank Transfer*.
5. Enter a whole number into **Chapt ID**.
6. Optionally fill **QB Account**.
7. Enter a whole number into **Chart ID**.
8. Optionally fill **Charges ID**. Left blank it is saved as `0`.
9. Set **Trace Checks** to **Yes** or **No** as your accountant advises.
10. Click **Save**.
**You will know it worked when:** **"Payment mode added"** appears and the list refreshes.
**Good to know:**
- The **Description** is what appears in every **Mode of Payment** dropdown across
  Receipts and Payment Vouchers, so make it unambiguous.
- Payment modes must exist before a receipt or a payment voucher can be saved.
- **Chapt ID**, **Chart ID**, **Charges ID** and **Trace Checks** are accounting codes —
  ask your accountant for the right values.
- Reload the Receipts or Payment Voucher page before a new mode appears in their
  dropdowns.

### How to record a payment and create a receipt
**Who this is for:** Accountants, registrars and principals.
**Before you start:** The child must exist and at least one payment mode must be
configured.
**Open this page:** [Receipts](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/receipts)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Receipts** from the sidebar, under **Account**.
2. Click **Add Receipt**. The **Add Receipt** form opens.
3. Set **Transaction Date**.
4. Choose the **Child** who paid.
5. Choose the **Type**.
6. Optionally fill **Reference**, up to 11 characters, such as a cheque number.
7. Optionally choose a **Services** tag. It prints on the receipt.
8. Fill in **Description**. This is required.
9. Click **Add Line** to create your first payment row. The table starts empty.
10. On the line, fill **Value Date**, **Mode of Payment**, **Payment Detail** and **Amount
    / Cheque**.
11. For a split payment, click **Add Line** again and fill the second row. To remove a
    row click its **×**.
12. Click **Save**.
13. Click the receipt in the left list to see the printable version and its **Total** line.
**You will know it worked when:** The receipt appears as a card with the date, the child's
name, an amber type badge and the amount.
**Good to know:**
- Click **Add Line** before entering payment details — this form opens with no rows, which
  catches most people out the first time.
- **Description** is required and is the most common reason a receipt will not save.
- Add up the payment rows yourself before saving; the total appears once saved.
- A receipt is recorded against a child. Record whatever the family actually paid,
  including part payments, and read their overall position on **Balance by Class**.
- A cheque is recorded as received on the day you enter it, so note the clearing status in
  **Description** if that matters to you.
- If you see **"Receipt details failed to load"** while editing, click **Cancel** and
  reopen the receipt rather than saving.

### How to check whether a family has paid
**Who this is for:** Accountants, registrars and principals.
**Before you start:** Payments should be recorded as receipts.
**Open this page:** [Balance by Class](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/balance-by-class)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Balance by Class** from the sidebar, under **Account**.
2. Filter to the child's class, or type the child's name into **"Search student..."**.
3. Read the **Balance** column.
**You will know it worked when:** You see the child's row with **Billed**, **Received**
and **Balance**.
**Good to know:**
- A balance of zero means the family is square. A positive balance in grey means they
  still owe that amount. A negative balance in red means they have paid ahead and are in
  credit.
- Read the colour carefully: red means overpaid, not overdue.
- This is the child's overall position across all their invoices and receipts. To see
  which specific charges are outstanding, compare their invoices and receipts on those two
  pages.

### How to view outstanding balances by class
**Who this is for:** Accountants and principals.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [Balance by Class](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/balance-by-class)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Balance by Class** from the sidebar, under **Account**.
2. By default it shows every child in every class, with no date cut-off.
3. Click a **class chip** to narrow to one classroom, or **All Classes** to go back.
4. To see the position at a past date, set the **As of** date. Only invoices and receipts
   dated on or before that day are counted.
5. To find one family, type into **"Search student..."**. It matches child name, parent
   name, student code and class name.
6. Read each row: **Billed** with a green `+`, **Received** with a red `-`, and
   **Balance** with an `=`.
**You will know it worked when:** You see one row per child.
**Good to know:**
- The figures are shown without a plus or minus sign on the number itself, so read the
  colour and the `+`, `-` or `=` prefix to tell the direction.
- The class chips filter the list. Add the rows up yourself for a class total, or export
  **Invoices** and **Receipts** and total them in a spreadsheet.
- The **As of** date gives you everything up to that day. For a single month, take the
  figures at the end of the month and at the end of the previous month and compare.
- Children with no invoices and no receipts appear with zeros.
- To keep a copy, copy the figures into a spreadsheet, or work from the Invoices and
  Receipts exports.

### How to print or email a receipt
**Who this is for:** Accountants, registrars and principals.
**Before you start:** The receipt exists. For emailing, the child's parent needs an email
address on file.
**Open this page:** [Receipts](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/receipts)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Receipts** from the sidebar, under **Account**.
2. Click the receipt in the left-hand list. The printable version loads on the right.
3. To print, click **Print** in the preview header. A new window opens with the print
   dialog.
4. To email it to the parent, click **Email**. The button reads **"Sending..."**.
**You will know it worked when:** The print dialog opens, or the parent receives the
receipt as a PDF attachment.
**Good to know:**
- Emailing a receipt sends the family a proper PDF, which makes it the neatest way to give
  a parent their copy.
- To save a PDF yourself, click **Print** and choose **"Save as PDF"** as the destination.
- If you see **"No parent email found for this child."**, add an email address to the
  child's parent record.
- If nothing happens on **Print**, allow pop-ups for the portal and try again.
- The nursery name, phone, email and TRN printed on a receipt come from a standard
  template. Ask your provider to set these to your nursery's details.
- Print receipts one at a time from the preview.

### How to print or email an invoice
**Who this is for:** Accountants, registrars and principals.
**Before you start:** The invoice exists. For emailing, the child's parent needs an email
address.
**Open this page:** [Invoices](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/invoices)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Invoices** from the sidebar, under **Account**.
2. Click the invoice card in the left-hand list. The full preview loads on the right.
3. To print, click **Print** in the preview header.
4. To email it to the parent, click **Email**.
**You will know it worked when:** The print dialog opens, or a message confirms
**"Invoice emailed to <address>"**.
**Good to know:**
- To save a PDF, choose **"Save as PDF"** as the destination in the print dialog.
- Check the preview on screen before sending it to a printer.
- If you see **"No parent email found for this child."**, add an email address to the
  child's parent record.
- The nursery name, phone, email and TRN come from a standard template. Ask your provider
  to set these to your nursery's details.
- Print invoices one at a time from the preview.

### How to search and sort invoices or receipts
**Who this is for:** Accountants, registrars and principals.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [Invoices](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/invoices)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Invoices** or **Receipts** from the sidebar, under **Account**.
2. Type into the search box. **"Search invoices..."** matches invoice number, date, amount
   and child name. **"Search receipts..."** matches child name, receipt id and type.
3. Click **Sort** to flip the date order. Both pages open with the newest first.
**You will know it worked when:** The left-hand list narrows and reorders.
**Good to know:**
- Search by child name to see one family's invoices or receipts together.
- To look at a particular month, search or scroll the list, which is ordered by date.
- On Receipts, the first receipt loads into the preview when the page opens.

---

## 10. Expenses and suppliers

> **Three separate records.** **Purchases**, **Expenses** and **Payment Vouchers** are
> recorded independently and each links to a **Supplier**. Recording something in one does
> not create it in the others, so agree with your team which record to use for what, and
> keep to it.

### How to add a supplier
**Who this is for:** Accountants and administrators.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [Suppliers](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/suppliers)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Suppliers** from the sidebar, under **Finance**.
2. Use the **Add Supplier** form on the right.
3. Enter the vendor's name into **Supplier Name**.
4. Optionally fill **Supplier Phone**, **Supplier Email**, **Supplier Address** and
   **Supplier Terms**, for example *Net 30*.
5. In **Supplier VAT**, enter a number between 0 and 100. Leave the `0.00` default if you
   are unsure.
6. Optionally add a **Supplier Description**.
7. Click **Save**.
**You will know it worked when:** The supplier appears in the list on the left and the
header count increases.
**Good to know:**
- After adding a supplier, reload the **Expenses**, **Purchases** or **Payment Voucher**
  page before they appear in its dropdown. Press F5 and they are there.
- Leave the `0.00` in **Supplier VAT** rather than clearing it, as a blank value is not
  accepted.
- The VAT figure here is a reference note. Expense and purchase VAT comes from the service
  or from what you type on the form.
- To edit, click the pencil on the row — the heading becomes **Edit Supplier** and the
  button becomes **Update**.
- Use the **"Search suppliers..."** box, which matches name, phone and email.
- Keep a supplier record even for a vendor you no longer use, so historical expenses keep
  their link.

### How to create an expense type
**Who this is for:** Accountants and administrators.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [Expense Types](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/expense-types)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Expense Types** from the sidebar, under **Finance**.
2. Use the **Add Expense Type** form on the right.
3. Enter the category into **Type Name**, for example *Cleaning Supplies*.
4. Optionally fill **Description**.
5. Click **Save**.
**You will know it worked when:** The type appears in the list and is immediately
available in the **Expense Supply** dropdown on the Expenses page.
**Good to know:**
- An expense type must exist before an expense can be recorded against it.
- This is the one list in Finance that appears in its dropdown without reloading.
- Check whether a type is in use before deleting it. If the deletion is refused, the type
  is still linked to expenses — keep it.
- Use the **"Search expense types..."** box, which matches name and description.

### How to record an expense
**Who this is for:** Accountants and administrators.
**Before you start:** An expense type and a supplier must exist. Have the receipt or
invoice file ready, at a maximum of 2 MB.
**Open this page:** [Expenses](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/expenses)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Expenses** from the sidebar, under **Finance**.
2. Click **Add Expense**. It is at the top right-hand corner of the page.
3. In **Expense Supply**, pick the spending category.
4. In **Type**, pick the accounting journal type. This is a different dropdown from the
   one above.
5. Set the **Date**.
6. Choose the **Supplier**.
7. In **Amount**, enter the net cost, excluding VAT.
8. In **VAT**, type the VAT amount if any.
9. Optionally fill **Description**.
10. Under **Attachment**, drag the file onto the dashed box or click **Browse ...**. An
    attachment is required on a new expense.
11. Check the preview. **Preview** opens a full-screen view.
12. Click **Save**.
**You will know it worked when:** The expense appears in the table with the date, supply,
supplier, net amount and an attachment badge with **View** and **Download** buttons.
**Good to know:**
- Work out the VAT yourself and type it in. The figures are stored exactly as you enter
  them, and the table shows the net amount.
- **Expense Supply** is your own category and **Type** is the accounting journal type.
  Both are required and they are easy to mix up, so read the labels.
- Keep the attachment under 2 MB. Scan receipts at a moderate quality.
- When editing, leave the attachment alone to keep the existing file.
- For a monthly total, export to CSV and sum the column in a spreadsheet.
- The search box matches supplier name, expense type name and description.
- Reload the page if a newly added supplier is missing from the dropdown.

### How to create a payment voucher
**Who this is for:** Accountants and administrators.
**Before you start:** A supplier and at least one payment mode must exist.
**Open this page:** [Payment Voucher](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/payment-voucher)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Payment Voucher** from the sidebar, under **Finance**.
2. Click **+ Add Payment Voucher**.
3. Set **Transaction Date**.
4. Choose the **Supplier**.
5. Choose the **Type**.
6. Optionally fill **Reference** and **Description**.
7. In the payment table, fill all four columns on the first row: **Value Date**, **Mode of
   Payment**, **Payment Detail** and **Amount / Cheque**.
8. To split the payment, click the small **+** circle in the table header for another row.
   Remove a row with its red bin.
9. Click **Save**.
**You will know it worked when:** The voucher appears in the table. Its **Amount** is the
sum of the payment rows, and its **Number** is generated for you.
**Good to know:**
- Add the rows up yourself before saving. The total appears in the **Amount** column once
  saved.
- The bin is hidden when only one row remains, so there is always at least one line.
- If you see **"Payment voucher details failed to load"** while editing, click **Cancel**
  and reopen the voucher rather than saving.
- Saving is the final step, and vouchers stay editable afterwards. Agree a checking
  routine within your team, since there is no separate approval step in the portal.
- To inspect one, click the **eye** icon to open **Payment Voucher Details**, then
  **Close**.
- The search box matches voucher number, type, supplier name and reference.
- Reload the page if a newly added supplier or payment mode is missing from a dropdown.

### How to raise a purchase
**Who this is for:** Accountants and administrators.
**Before you start:** A supplier and the services you are buying must exist.
**Open this page:** [Purchases](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/purchases)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Purchases** from the sidebar, under **Finance**.
2. Click **+ Add Purchase**. The **Add New Purchase** form opens.
3. Set the **Date**.
4. Choose the **Type**.
5. Choose the **Supplier**.
6. Set **Tax Exempt** now, before entering any lines.
7. In the **Purchases** table, first row, choose a **Service**. **Fee** and **VAT (%)**
   fill in from the service record.
8. Enter **QTY** and adjust **Fee** if the price differs.
9. **VAT Amount** and **Total** calculate themselves.
10. For more items, click **Add Purchase** on the section bar, which adds a row. Remove a
    row with the red bin at its start.
11. Check the footer **Total VAT** and **Total**, which update as you type.
12. Click **Save**.
**You will know it worked when:** The purchase appears in the table with its
**Purchase#** and a VAT-inclusive **Amount**.
**Good to know:**
- Set **Tax Exempt** before entering any lines. Changing it afterwards clears them,
  because it switches to a different catalogue of services and sets VAT to zero.
- The **Fee** stays editable so you can record the price you actually paid. The VAT
  percentage comes from the service record.
- The calculation is: fee times quantity, plus VAT at the service's percentage. Purchases
  do not carry a discount column.
- If you see **"Purchase details failed to load"** while editing, click **Cancel** and
  reopen the purchase rather than saving.
- For a total across purchases, export to CSV and sum in a spreadsheet.
- Reload the page if a newly added supplier or service is missing from a dropdown.

### How to export a finance table
**Who this is for:** Accountants and principals.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [Expenses](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/expenses)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Expenses**, **Purchases** or **Payment Voucher** from the sidebar, under
   **Finance**. These three pages carry the export toolbar.
2. Find the row of four small buttons above the table: **Copy**, **PDF**, **Print** and
   **CSV**.
3. Choose one:
   - **CSV** downloads a spreadsheet file immediately.
   - **Copy** puts the table on your clipboard as text you can paste straight into Excel.
   - **Print** opens a print-ready page in a new tab and launches the print dialog.
   - **PDF** opens the same print dialog.
**You will know it worked when:** The file downloads, the clipboard fills, or the print
dialog opens.
**Good to know:**
- To get a PDF, choose **"Save as PDF"** as the destination in your browser's print
  dialog.
- An export contains the full table rather than just your filtered view, so filter or sort
  the spreadsheet after downloading.
- Filenames are the same each time, so rename each download with a date as soon as it
  arrives — otherwise you end up with `purchases.csv` and `purchases (1).csv`.
- The files handle Arabic text and accented names correctly in Excel, and Arabic prints
  right-aligned.
- If nothing happens on **PDF** or **Print**, allow pop-ups for the portal and retry.
- For a single invoice or receipt, open the record and use its own **Print** button.

---

## 11. Your nursery's settings

> **My Center is your nursery.** **My Center** is where you change your own nursery's
> phone number, address, logo, VAT details and session times. If someone asks where to
> change the nursery's details, the answer is **My Center**.

### How to update your nursery's details
**Who this is for:** Principals and administrators.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [My Center](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/my-center)
**Steps:**
1. Open **My Center** from the sidebar, under **Center**.
2. Stay on the **Center Details** tab.
3. Edit whichever group you need:
   - **Basic information** — **Center Name**, **Contact Title**, **Contact Name**,
     **Capacity** and **Center Description**.
   - **Contacts** — **Phone 1/2/3**, **Fax 1/2/3** and **Email 1/2/3**.
   - **Social Media** — **Website**, **Facebook**, **Twitter**, **Instagram**,
     **YouTube** and **Cloud Folder**.
   - **Address** — **Country**, **Currency Code**, **GMT Timezone** and **Address**.
   - **Financial** — **VAT TRN Number** and **Payment Details**, your bank or IBAN text
     for parents.
   - **Map** — **Latitude** and **Longitude**.
4. Click **Update** at the foot of the page.
**You will know it worked when:** A green **"Center updated successfully"** message
appears.
**Good to know:**
- The real save button is **Update** at the very bottom of the page. The blue "Update
  Center" text at the top is the page heading.
- Nine fields must have a value: **Center Name**, **Contact Title**, **Contact Name**,
  **Center Description**, **Phone 1**, **Email 1**, **Country**, **GMT Timezone** and
  **Address**.
- **Center Logo** carries a red asterisk but the page saves without one.
- If you see **"Center id not found. Please refresh and try again."**, reload the page and
  try again.
- Your daily time slots are set on the **Sessions** tab. Put term dates and holidays in
  the **Calendar**.

### How to add a session
**Who this is for:** Principals and administrators.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [My Center](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/my-center)
**Steps:**
1. Open **My Center** from the sidebar, under **Center**.
2. Click the **Sessions** tab.
3. On the right, under **Add Session**, type the **Session Name**, for example *Morning*.
4. Set **From Time**.
5. Set **To Time**, which must be later than the from time.
6. Leave the **Status** toggle on **Active**.
7. Click **Save**.
**You will know it worked when:** **"Session saved successfully."** appears and the
session joins the list on the left.
**Good to know:**
- Sessions are the **Timing** options offered when registering a child into a class. If
  that dropdown is empty, create an active session here.
- Only active sessions appear in the registration dropdown. Deactivating one hides it from
  new registrations and leaves existing registrations untouched.
- Sessions read **"Not Active"** here while children and staff read **"Inactive"**. Both
  mean the same thing.
- To edit, click the pencil on the row — the heading becomes **Edit Session** and the
  button becomes **Update**.
- To delete, click the red bin and confirm **"Delete Session"**. If the deletion is
  refused, registrations still point at that session — deactivate it instead.

### How to change your nursery's logo
**Who this is for:** Principals and administrators.
**Before you start:** An image file. Use a JPG or PNG under 2 MB.
**Open this page:** [My Center](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/my-center)
**Steps:**
1. Open **My Center** from the sidebar, under **Center**, and stay on the **Center
   Details** tab.
2. Scroll to **Center Logo**.
3. Drag an image file onto the dashed box, or click the box or **Browse ...** and choose a
   file.
4. The new logo previews immediately. The magnifier shows it full-screen and the **X**
   clears it.
5. Click **Update** at the foot of the page.
**You will know it worked when:** A green **"Center updated successfully"** message
appears.
**Good to know:**
- This uploader accepts drag-and-drop as well as clicking.
- Stick to a JPG or PNG under 2 MB, which is the size the platform expects elsewhere.
- Remember to click **Update** at the bottom — previewing the image does not save it.

### How to buy or renew your subscription
**Who this is for:** Principals and owners.
**Before you start:** A payment card. Your nursery must not already hold a valid or
pending subscription.
**Open this page:** [Packages](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/packages)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Packages** from the sidebar, under **Center**.
2. Read the badge at the top right-hand corner of the page. It tells you your current
   subscription state.
3. If you already subscribe, also read the **Valid until**, **Children limit** and
   **Package units** cards beneath it.
4. Click **Select package** on the plan you want.
5. Set the **Start date**, **Number of months** between 1 and 60, and **Package units**
   between 1 and 100.
6. Check the **Order summary** panel on the right. **Children capacity**, **Subscription
   period**, **End date** and **Total** update as you change the figures.
7. Under **Card details**, complete **Name on card**, **Card number**, **Expiry month**,
   **Expiry year** and **Security code**.
8. Click **Pay {amount}**. The button reads **"Processing..."**.
**You will know it worked when:** **"Package purchased successfully."** appears.
**Good to know:**
- A package is your nursery's own subscription to the Peekaboo software. It is not a
  parent-facing fee plan and does not appear on any parent invoice. Parent fees live under
  **Account**.
- One subscription at a time. If you already hold a valid or pending one, the buttons stay
  unavailable until it lapses.
- All five card fields are needed together.
- Card details go straight to the payment provider.
- The plan catalogue is set by the platform, so contact your provider to discuss options.
- Use **Back to packages** to change plan before paying.

---

## 12. Roles and access (Admin only)

> **Who can use this section.** **Roles & Access** is open to administrators. If you
> cannot see it in your sidebar, ask your administrator to make the change for you.
> Access is set per role, never per person.

### How to create a role
**Who this is for:** Administrators.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [Roles & Access](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/roles)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Roles & Access** from the sidebar, under **Reports & Admin**.
2. Click **Add role**. It is at the top right-hand corner of the page.
3. Type the **Role name**.
4. Leave **Status** as **Active**, or set **Inactive** to create it switched off.
5. Click **Create role**.
6. Note the **ID** number shown on the new role's card.
7. Grant its access next — see the following how-to.
**You will know it worked when:** The new role appears in the list showing **Active** and
its **ID**, and the **Total roles** counter increases.
**Good to know:**
- A new role starts with nothing selected, so complete the access step before assigning
  anyone to it.
- People are given a role on the **Staff** page.
- Create a dedicated role when one person needs different access from their colleagues.

### How to change what a role can access
**Who this is for:** Administrators.
**Before you start:** The role exists.
**Open this page:** [Roles & Access](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/roles)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Roles & Access** from the sidebar, under **Reports & Admin**.
2. Find the role — use the **"Search roles…"** box if needed.
3. Click **Manage access** on its card, or click the role's name.
4. The access panel opens on the right, headed **Access assignment**, showing *"{n} of
   {total} modules selected"*.
5. Scroll the grouped module list and click a tile to tick or untick it. Ticked tiles are
   outlined and tinted. **Select all** ticks everything and **Clear all** unticks
   everything.
6. Click **Save access**.
7. Click **Close** when finished.
**You will know it worked when:** A success message appears and the counter updates.
**Good to know:**
- Changes to your own role take effect immediately. Everyone else picks them up after
  signing out and back in.
- The tile name is the access name used throughout the portal — for example **"Our Loved
  Ones"** covers Children and **"Daily Report"** covers attendance and daily reports.
- This changes access for everyone holding that role. For a single person, create a
  dedicated role and move them to it from the **Staff** page.
- The panel has no search box, so scroll to find the tile you want.
- **Roles & Access**, **Referrals & Sources** and **Centers** are opened by administrators
  and principals rather than through these tiles.

### How to deactivate or delete a role
**Who this is for:** Administrators.
**Before you start:** The role exists. Check who holds it first.
**Open this page:** [Roles & Access](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/roles)
**Steps (deactivate):**
1. Open **Roles & Access** from the sidebar, under **Reports & Admin**.
2. On the role's card, click the coloured dot button with the tooltip **"Toggle status"**.
**Steps (delete):**
1. Open **Staff** from the sidebar, under **People**, and use the **All Roles** filter to
   see who currently holds the role. Move those people to another role first.
2. Return to **Roles & Access** and click the **red bin** on the role's card.
3. Confirm in the dialog.
**You will know it worked when:** The status label flips between **Active** and
**Inactive**, or the role is removed and a success message appears.
**Good to know:**
- The status toggle acts on one click, so check you have the right card.
- Always check the **Staff** page before deleting a role, so nobody is left without
  access.
- Where you only want to adjust what a role can reach, edit its access rather than
  deactivating it.

### How to give someone access to a page
**Who this is for:** Administrators.
**Before you start:** You need to know which role the person holds.
**Open this page:** [Staff](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app/staff)
**Steps:**
1. Work out which access name controls the page. For example, **Children** needs **"Our
   Loved Ones"**, **Staff** needs **"Our Heroes"**, the attendance and daily-report pages
   need **"Daily Report"**, **Messages** and **Calendar** need **"Communication"** or
   **"Console"**, and the admissions pages need **"CRM"**, **"Leads"** or
   **"Admissions"**.
2. Open **Staff** from the sidebar, under **People**, and look at the person's record to
   find their role.
3. Open **Roles & Access**, click **Manage access** on that role, tick the tile from step
   1, and click **Save access**.
4. Ask the user to sign out and back in.
**You will know it worked when:** The user's sidebar includes the new page.
**Good to know:**
- This changes access for everyone holding that role. For one individual, create a new
  role, grant it the right modules, then reassign that person from the **Staff** page.
- Other users pick up changes after signing out and back in. Only your own role updates
  live.
- **Roles & Access** and **Referrals & Sources** are opened by administrators, and
  **Centers** by principals. To give someone those pages, change their role on the
  **Staff** page.

---

## 13. Finding, filtering and exporting information

### How to search a list
**Who this is for:** Everyone.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Where:** on whichever list page you are already using. Every list page has its own search box; there is no single search box for the whole portal.
**Steps:**
1. Open the page you want.
2. Find the search box. It usually sits at the top right-hand corner of the table, or
   just above the cards.
3. Type your search term. Most lists filter as you type.
4. On **Messages**, press Enter or click the round magnifier button to run the search.
**You will know it worked when:** The list narrows to matching rows.
**Good to know:**
- Each page searches its own nominated fields rather than every column, so search by the
  most obvious identifier — usually a name or a number.
- There is no single search box covering the whole portal. Go to the page that holds the
  information and search there.
- If a list looks empty, clear the search box to check whether it is genuinely empty or
  simply filtered. The count line beneath the table shows **"(filtered from N)"** when
  records are hidden.

### How to sort a table by a column
**Who this is for:** Everyone.
**Before you start:** You need a page with the standard table — **Payment Modes**,
**Services**, **Expense Types**, **Expenses**, **Payment Voucher**, **Purchases** or
**Suppliers**.
**Where:** on whichever table page you are already using. Click the heading at the top of a column.
**Steps:**
1. Open the page.
2. Click a column header. The first click sorts ascending.
3. Click the same header again to flip to descending.
**You will know it worked when:** The rows reorder under that column.
**Good to know:**
- To return to the original order, reload the page.
- Sorting keeps you on the page you are viewing, unlike searching, which returns you to
  page one.
- On **Invoices**, **Receipts** and **CRM Leads**, use the page's own **Sort** button
  instead. Invoices and Receipts sort by date, and CRM Leads by student name.
- Where a column header does not reorder the rows, use the page's search and filter
  controls to find what you need.

### How to change how many rows show
**Who this is for:** Everyone.
**Before you start:** You need a page with the standard table.
**Where:** on whichever table page you are already using. Look just above the table, on the left-hand side.
**Steps:**
1. Open the page.
2. Find the control reading **Show** [dropdown] **entries**.
3. Choose **10**, **25** or **50**.
**You will know it worked when:** The table shows that many rows and the count line
updates.
**Good to know:**
- Changing the page size returns you to page one.
- **Suppliers**, **Expense Types** and **Payment Modes** show every row on one page, so
  scroll rather than paging.
- On **Staff**, read the list in table view and use the search and role filters to narrow
  it.

### How to move between pages of a list
**Who this is for:** Everyone.
**Before you start:** A list long enough to be paged.
**Where:** on whichever list page you are already using. Look at the bottom right-hand corner of the table.
**Steps:**
1. Look at the bottom right of the table.
2. Click the left or right chevron buttons to step one page at a time, or click a numbered
   page button to jump.
3. Read the bottom-left line, which reads for example **"Showing 1 to 10 of 42 entries"**.
**You will know it worked when:** The rows change and the count line updates.
**Good to know:**
- On **Messages**, use the **<** and **>** buttons at the bottom of the history, which
  pages ten at a time.
- Searching first is usually quicker than paging through a long list.
- The count line tells you the full size of the list, which is useful before exporting.

### How to filter a list by date
**Who this is for:** Everyone.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Where:** on whichever page you need the dates for. The list in step 1 below tells you where the date control sits on each one.
**Steps:**
1. Open the page that holds the date control:
   - **Attendance Reports** — set **From** and **To**.
   - **Daily Reports** — set the date picker to the day you want.
   - **Messages** — set **From** and **To**, then press Enter or click the magnifier.
   - **CRM Leads** — click **Filter** and use **Date Range**.
   - **Balance by Class** — set the **As of** date.
   - **Live Dashboard**, Finance tab — click a month pill, or use **Custom Date** for an
     older month.
2. Read the results.
**You will know it worked when:** The list or grid updates to the period you chose.
**Good to know:**
- **Attendance Reports** covers up to about a year at a time. Run a long history in
  shorter blocks.
- **Balance by Class** counts everything up to the **As of** date. Compare two dates to
  see the movement in between.
- On **Invoices**, **Receipts**, **Expenses**, **Purchases** and **Payment Voucher**,
  export to CSV and filter by date in your spreadsheet.

### How to export a list to a spreadsheet
**Who this is for:** Accountants, principals and administrators.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Where:** on whichever page you want the figures from. The list in step 1 below names the pages that offer an export.
**Steps:**
1. Open a page that offers an export:
   - **Expenses**, **Purchases** or **Payment Voucher** — click **CSV** above the table.
   - **Attendance Reports** — click **CSV**.
   - **Assessment Report** — click **CSV** above the saved-reports table.
   - **Live Dashboard**, CRM tab, **Details** view — click **Export to Excel**.
2. The file downloads to your browser's downloads folder.
3. Open it in Excel or your spreadsheet program.
**You will know it worked when:** The file appears in your downloads and opens with your
data.
**Good to know:**
- These files are CSVs, which open in Excel perfectly well, including the one labelled
  **Export to Excel**.
- On the finance pages, an export contains the full table rather than just your filtered
  view. Filter the spreadsheet after downloading. On **Assessment Report**, the export
  follows the **Search** box.
- Rename each download with a date straight away, since filenames repeat.
- Arabic and accented names display correctly in Excel.
- For pages without an export — **Children**, **Staff**, **Suppliers**, **Services**,
  **Payment Modes**, **Expense Types**, **Balance by Class**, **Invoices** and
  **Receipts** — copy the rows from the screen into a spreadsheet, or use the **Copy**
  button where the page offers one.

### How to copy a finance table to your clipboard
**Who this is for:** Accountants and administrators.
**Before you start:** Open **Expenses**, **Purchases**, **Payment Voucher** or the
**Assessment Report** saved table.
**Where:** on whichever of the four pages named below you are already using. Look just above the table.
**Steps:**
1. Find the **Copy** button above the table.
2. Click it. A message confirms the rows were copied.
3. Open Excel and paste.
**You will know it worked when:** The table appears in your spreadsheet with its columns
intact.
**Good to know:**
- This is the quickest route when you only need the figures in a spreadsheet and do not
  want a file.
- As with the CSV, a copy from a finance page contains the full table.

### How to print a page or a record
**Who this is for:** Everyone.
**Before you start:** Allow pop-ups for the portal in your browser.
**Where:** on whichever page holds the thing you want to print. The list in step 1 below tells you which button to use on each one.
**Steps:**
1. Identify which route applies:
   - A finance table on **Expenses**, **Purchases** or **Payment Voucher** — use **Print**
     or **PDF** above the table.
   - An attendance report — use **Print / PDF**.
   - A single invoice or receipt — open the record and click **Print**.
   - An assessment report — use the toolbar **Print** for the table, or the per-row
     **Assessment Report** button for one child.
2. A new window opens with a printable layout and your browser's print dialog appears.
3. To save a PDF, change the **Destination** setting to **"Save as PDF"**.
4. Click **Save**.
**You will know it worked when:** The document prints, or saves as a PDF file.
**Good to know:**
- **"Save as PDF"** in your browser's print dialog is the final step for every **PDF** and
  **Print** button. The one document the portal sends as a ready-made PDF is a receipt
  emailed to a parent.
- If nothing happens, allow pop-ups for the portal and retry.
- Arabic printing is supported, with right-aligned text and correct column order.
- To give a parent a copy of a daily report, email it to them from the report list.

### How to read the Live Dashboard
**Who this is for:** Principals, room leads and administrators.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [Live Dashboard](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app)
**Steps:**
1. Open **Live Dashboard** from the sidebar, under **Operations**. It opens on the **Main
   Dashboard** tab.
2. Check the date under the title matches today.
3. On **Main Dashboard**, read **Children Present**, **Absent Today**, **Active Staff**,
   **Classroom Occupancy**, **Late Arrivals** and **Upcoming & Pinned**.
4. Click **Finance** for the current month's billed figure, the green **% Collected**
   badge and the **Late** amount.
5. Click **CRM** for the nine pipeline cards and the **Calendar**, **Graphs** and
   **Details** view pills.
**You will know it worked when:** You see the three tabs' figures, refreshed automatically
every 60 seconds.
**Good to know:**
- Mark every child's register each day for the present and absent figures to add up to
  your roll. Children who were never marked appear in neither total.
- Late children count as present.
- **Active Staff** counts who is on the payroll. For who clocked in today, use **Staff
  Attendance**.
- **Classroom Occupancy** compares enrolment with capacity, while the "{n} present" line
  under the room name is who turned up.
- If a figure reads zero unexpectedly, or a red message appears, reload the page before
  acting on it.
- The dashboard is a summary view. Open the relevant page from the sidebar to work with
  the underlying records.
- The page refreshes itself every minute. Reload the browser for an immediate update.
- If you work very early in the morning, check the date under the title before reading the
  attendance figures.

### How to change the dashboard's date range
**Who this is for:** Principals and accountants.
**Before you start:** Nothing.
**Open this page:** [Live Dashboard](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app)
**Steps (Finance month):**
1. Open the **Live Dashboard** and click the **Finance** tab.
2. The row of pills at the top opens on the current month.
3. Click any pill to switch month. The pills cover last month and the next seven.
4. To reach an older month, use the **Custom Date** control on the right of that row and
   pick any month and year.
**Steps (CRM calendar month):**
1. Click the **CRM** tab. Use **‹** and **›** to step months, or **Today** to jump back.
**You will know it worked when:** The Finance figures update for the chosen month, or the
CRM calendar moves.
**Good to know:**
- The Finance month returns to the current month when you switch tabs or reload, so note
  down any figures you need as you go.
- Two Finance figures are all-time by design: the footer **"Across all months … still
  unpaid"** line and the **Outstanding Balances** list.
- **Late** on the Finance tab means billed minus collected for that month. Check the
  invoice dates before treating an amount as overdue.
- The CRM month arrows move the calendar. The nine pipeline cards, the graphs and the
  details table are all-time figures.
- Use the nine pipeline cards for admissions counts, along with the **Source** and
  **Referrals** bar charts.
- The **Main Dashboard** tab always shows today.

*This library covers the everyday tasks nursery staff carry out in the portal. If a task
you need is not here, ask your principal or administrator, who can check whether your role
includes that page.*

<!-- ================= 04-faq ================= -->

# Frequently Asked Questions

Short answers to the things nursery staff ask most. Bold text is exactly what you
will see on screen, so you can scan a page and match it.

Two things worth knowing before you start:

- **Nothing happens on its own.** Every email, every update and every record exists
  because a person clicked something. The portal sends no reminders, no alerts and no
  automatic messages.
- **Your menu shows what you can open.** If a colleague has a page you do not, that is
  your role's settings, not a mistake.

Where an answer mentions a how-to, you will find it in *How To — Everyday Tasks*. The
portal is at **[https://peek.peek-a-boo.app](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/login)**, and
every page has its own direct link in *Every Page, One Click Away*.

---

## Signing in and getting started

**How do I log in?**
Go to **[https://peek.peek-a-boo.app](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/login)** and type the
username and password your administrator gave you. Then tick the **"I'm not a robot"**
security check and click **Sign In**. The security check is compulsory every time. See
*How to sign in to the portal*.

**Where do I sign in from?**
From **[https://peek.peek-a-boo.app](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/login)** in your web
browser. It takes you straight to the **Login** screen. There is nothing to install and
no app to download for staff. See *How to sign in to the portal*.

**Is my username my email address?**
Not necessarily. Your administrator chooses your username when they create your staff
record, and it may be a short name rather than an email address. Ask them if you are
unsure.

**I forgot my password — what do I do?**
Click **Forgot?** on the login screen and follow the three steps: your username, the
code emailed to you, then your new password. See *How to reset a forgotten password*.
A successful reset signs you straight in.

**How do I reset my password?**
Use **Forgot?** on the login screen. That is the self-service route, and it is the one
to use if you are locked out. See *How to reset a forgotten password*.

**Can I change my own password once I am signed in?**
Not from inside the portal — there is no profile or account-settings page. Either use
**Forgot?** on the login screen, or ask your administrator to set a new one on your
staff record. See *How to change a staff member's password*.

**The reset code isn't working.**
Ask for a fresh code and try again, taking care to type it exactly. The code is checked
when you submit your new password, so a wrong or expired one shows there rather than at
the code step. See *How to reset a forgotten password*.

**What are the password rules?**
A new password must be at least six characters. There is no other requirement, so
choose something you will remember and would be happy for nobody else to guess.

**How do I log out?**
Click **Logout** at the far end of the top bar. See *How to sign out*. On a shared
reception computer, make this the last thing you do on every shift.

**Does closing the browser log me out?**
No — your session is kept in the browser and survives closing the tab or the window.
Always click **Logout** deliberately on any machine other people use. See *How to sign
out*.

**Someone else's account was still open on the reception computer.**
That is why the **Logout** button matters. Log them out, sign in as yourself, and make
explicit logging out a standing instruction for shared machines. See *How to sign out*.

**Should we share one login for the room?**
No. Give every member of staff their own account. Shared logins make it impossible to
know who recorded what, and the portal keeps no record of who changed a record.

**How do I switch to Arabic?**
Use the language picker in the top bar — the flag and name with the chevron. The whole
portal flips right-to-left. See *How to change the portal language*.

**Will my language choice follow me to another computer?**
No. Language, theme and the sidebar width are remembered in each browser on each
device, not on your account. Set them once on each machine you use. See *How to change
the portal language*.

**Where's the dark mode?**
There are two switches and either will do — one in the top bar and one in the sidebar
footer under **Theme**. They stay in sync with each other. See *How to switch between
light and dark mode*.

**How do I make the sidebar smaller?**
Click the collapse arrow at the very start of the top bar. See *How to collapse or
expand the sidebar*. The collapsed state is remembered in that browser for about a
week.

**What role am I?**
Your role name is on a badge in the top bar, next to the **Peekaboo Care** badge. See
*How to find out what your role is*. On a phone or narrow tablet the badge is hidden —
check on a wider screen.

**How do I know what I'm allowed to do?**
Your sidebar is the honest answer. It lists exactly the pages your role may open and
hides the rest. See *How to understand your menu*.

**It says my role could not be determined.**
Your account needs a role assigned to it. Ask an administrator to set the **Role** on
your staff record, then sign out and back in. See *How to get help when a page says
your role could not be determined*.

**My sidebar is almost empty.**
Your role has few modules granted, or none yet. A menu group with nothing in it
disappears completely, heading and all. Ask your administrator to grant what you need.
See *How to give someone access to a page*.

**I got "Page Not Found".**
Click **Open App** on that screen to return to the dashboard, or use the sidebar. See
*How to understand your menu*. If it takes you to the login form instead, just sign in
again.

**Is there a notifications bell?**
No. There are no alerts, no badge counts and no notification centre anywhere. Check the
pages that matter to you as part of your routine.

**Is there one search box that searches everything?**
No. Each list page has its own search box covering that page only. See *How to search a
list*.

**Where are my profile and account settings?**
There is no profile page and no avatar. The top bar holds your role badge, the language
picker, the theme switch and **Logout**. Your own details are edited on the **Staff**
page by someone with access.

**What is the chat button at the top for?**
It is a display sample, not a way of reaching anyone. To message parents for real, use
**Messages**. See *How to send a message to one parent*.

**Can I ask the assistant in the top bar to do things for me?**
No — nothing typed there reaches anyone or changes anything. Use the relevant page
directly.

**Can I use the portal on my phone?**
For everyday tasks, yes. Tables scroll sideways rather than reflowing, the role badge
is hidden on narrow screens, and the attendance **Update Status** buttons are hidden —
use **Edit** instead. Finance work is much easier on a tablet or desktop.

**My browser tab just says "Peekaboo Portal".**
Every page uses the same tab title, so several open tabs look identical. Pin or order
your tabs deliberately, or work in one tab at a time.

**Who do I ask for access?**
Your nursery's **Admin**. They tick the matching module for your **role**, then you
sign out and back in. See *How to give someone access to a page*.

---

## Children and families

**How do I add a kid?**
Open **Children**, click **Add Child**, fill in the **Child Data** tab and save. See
*How to add a new child*. Only the child's name and birth date are needed to save the
first time.

**How do I add a new student?**
Same thing — the portal calls them children. Go to **Children** and add the child. See
*How to add a new child*.

**How do I add a pupil?**
Pupils are children here. Open **Children** and add them. See *How to add a new child*.

**How do I enrol a new child?**
Create the child record first, then register them into a class. See *How to add a new
child* and *How to register a child into a class*. Without a registration a child
appears in no classroom.

**Where is the list of all our loved ones?**
That is the **Children** page. **Our Loved Ones** is Peekaboo's own name for children,
and also the access name your administrator ticks to unlock that page.

**What does "Our Loved Ones" mean?**
It is Peekaboo's term for the children. It is also the name of the access setting that
opens the **Children** page. See *How to give someone access to a page*.

**How do I edit a child's details?**
Open **Children**, click the child, change what you need and save. See *How to edit a
child's details*.

**Can I change a child's details after saving?**
Yes. Everything on the **Child Data** tab can be edited and re-saved at any time, with
no lock and no approval step. See *How to edit a child's details*.

**The age shown for a child doesn't look right.**
Treat the **Birth Date** on the record as the truth and work the age out from that. The
date of birth is what every other part of the portal uses.

**How do I record an allergy?**
Open the child, go to the **Health** tab, click **Add Health** and choose type
**Allergy**. See *How to record an allergy, medical condition or special diet*. Only a
health record this way raises the red allergy badge on the child's card.

**I typed the allergy into Medical Notes — is that enough?**
Add it as a **Health** record too. The red allergy badge that staff scan for comes from
the **Health** tab, not from free-text notes. See *How to record an allergy, medical
condition or special diet*.

**Where do I put a child's medical condition?**
On the child's **Health** tab, as a health record with the matching type. See *How to
record an allergy, medical condition or special diet*.

**Where do I record a special diet?**
On the child's **Health** tab, choosing type **Dietary**. See *How to record an
allergy, medical condition or special diet*. Do also tell the kitchen and the room
lead — the diet does not feed through to the daily report's meal section.

**Can I correct a health record I typed wrongly?**
Delete the record and add a correct one straight away — that is the route to use. See
*How to record an allergy, medical condition or special diet*. Do it in one sitting so
the right information is always on screen.

**How do I add someone who is allowed to collect a child?**
Open the child, go to the **Pickup** tab and click **Add Pickup**, then enter the name
and relation. See *How to add an authorised pickup person*. You can add as many as you
need.

**Does the portal check the pickup list when a child is collected?**
The check-out button does not ask who collected the child, so keep doing your usual
verbal and visual check at the door. Type the collector's name into the **Notes** box
in the attendance **Edit** panel so there is a written record. See *How to check a
child out at the end of the day*.

**How do I add a parent?**
On the child's **Child Data** tab, click the **[+]** beside the **Parent** dropdown and
fill in the parent's details. See *How to add a parent or guardian*. The new parent is
then selected for that child automatically.

**Can I link two parents to one child?**
One parent is linked per child. Record the second parent in the **Spouse Contact
Details** section of that parent's record, and add anyone else who may collect the
child on the **Pickup** tab. See *How to add a parent or guardian*.

**Where do I put mum's phone number?**
In the parent record linked to the child — open the child, **Child Data** tab, then the
**Parent** section. See *How to add a parent or guardian*.

**How do I put a child into a class?**
Open the child, go to the **Registrations** tab and add a registration with a class,
**From** and **To** dates and a **Timing**. See *How to register a child into a class*.

**How do I put a child into a room?**
A room is a class here. Use the child's **Registrations** tab. See *How to register a
child into a class*.

**How do I put a child into a group?**
Same thing — add a registration on the child's **Registrations** tab. See *How to
register a child into a class*.

**The Class dropdown is empty when I register a child.**
There is no active class to choose yet. Ask whoever manages classes to create or
reactivate one, then try again. See *How to register a child into a class*.

**The Timing dropdown is empty.**
No active session exists yet. Sessions are your daily time bands and are set up on
**My Center**. See *How to add a session*. This is the most common thing that holds up
a registration.

**How do I move a child to a different room?**
Edit the child's **registration**, not the child — open the child, **Registrations**
tab, and change the class. See *How to register a child into a class*.

**How do I change which days a child attends?**
On the child's registration, change the **Days** selection. Days belong to the
registration, not the child record. See *How to register a child into a class*.

**A child has left — do I delete them?**
Set them **Inactive** with the **Status** toggle instead. See *How to deactivate a
child who has left*. Inactive keeps all their history while taking them out of everyday
use.

**How do I archive a child who left?**
**Inactive** is the portal's archive. Flip the **Status** toggle on the child record.
See *How to deactivate a child who has left*.

**I can't delete a child.**
Deleting is held back while the child still has registrations, pickup people or health
records. Remove those first if you genuinely must delete — but setting the child
**Inactive** is almost always the better choice. See *How to deactivate a child who has
left*.

**Why can't I add a health record or pickup person to a new child?**
The **Health**, **Pickup** and **Registrations** tabs open once the child has been
saved at least once. Save the **Child Data** tab, reopen the child from the list, then
add them. See *How to add a new child*.

**How do I find a child quickly?**
Use the search box on the **Children** page, or the filters. See *How to search and
filter the children list*. If a filtered list looks odd, set **Status** back to **All
Status** by hand — the **Clear** button leaves that one as it is.

**How do I see which children have allergies?**
Each child's card carries a red allergy badge with a count, and the page header shows
counters. Scan the list on **Children**. See *How to search and filter the children
list*.

**Where do I record special educational needs?**
Use the child's **Health** tab for anything clinical, and the free-text **Notes** on
the child record for everything else. See *How to record an allergy, medical condition
or special diet*.

**Is there a key person field?**
Not as such. The nearest equivalent is the teacher assigned to the child's class. See
*How to assign a teacher to a class*.

**Can parents log into this portal?**
No. Parents use a separate app. What reaches them is the daily report you email and
whatever you publish on **Messages**.

**Can parents see the child record I'm editing?**
No. Child records, health notes and the pickup list are staff-only.

---

## Staff

**How do I add an employee?**
Open **Staff**, add the employee, fill in their name and pick their **Role**, then
save. See *How to add a staff member and create their portal login*. The role decides
what they can see.

**How do I add a new teacher?**
Teachers are staff records — same page. See *How to add a staff member and create their
portal login*.

**How do I add a new member of staff?**
Open **Staff** and add them. See *How to add a staff member and create their portal
login*.

**Where do I find our heroes?**
**Our Heroes** is Peekaboo's term for staff, and the name of the access setting that
unlocks the **Staff** page.

**What does "Our Heroes" mean?**
It is the access name for the **Staff** page. Tick it for a role and that role can open
the staff list. See *How to give someone access to a page*.

**How do I create a login for a new staff member?**
The login is created with the staff record — set their username and password on the
form as you add them. See *How to add a staff member and create their portal login*.

**The password box already has something in it.**
The new-staff form starts with a placeholder password. Always replace it with something
unique before saving, so no two people share the same credential. See *How to add a
staff member and create their portal login*.

**How do I change a staff member's password?**
Open their record on the **Staff** page and set a new password there. See *How to
change a staff member's password*.

**Who should be able to open the Staff page?**
Keep it to the people who genuinely need it. The staff list carries login details, so
treat it as sensitive and grant **Our Heroes** sparingly. See *How to change what a
role can access*.

**How do I edit an employee's phone number or job title?**
Open **Staff**, click the person, change the fields and save. See *How to edit a staff
member*.

**Someone resigned — what do I do?**
Set their **Status** to **Inactive** rather than deleting them. See *How to deactivate
a staff member who has left*. The one-click status button on the list flips it
straight away, so click deliberately.

**Where do I upload staff certificates or visas?**
On the staff record's document section. See *How to add a staff member and create their
portal login*. Qualifications go in the single-line **Capabilities** box.

**How do I assign a teacher to a class?**
Open the class and add the teacher there, not on the staff record. See *How to assign a
teacher to a class*.

**Which teacher is in which room?**
Look at the class records — teacher assignments live on the class. See *How to assign a
teacher to a class*.

**Can I see how many hours someone worked?**
The portal stores clock-in and clock-out times but does not add them up. Read the times
on **Staff Attendance**, or export the history from **Attendance Reports** and total
them in a spreadsheet. See *How to export a list to a spreadsheet*.

**Is there a staff rota?**
Not as a rota page. Use the **Calendar** for dated events such as training days and
cover arrangements. See *How to add a calendar event*.

**Can one person have extra access without changing everyone in their role?**
Access is set per role rather than per person. Create a role that matches what that
person needs, grant it, and assign them to it on the **Staff** page. See *How to create
a role*.

---

## Attendance

**How do I take the register?**
Open **Children Attendance** and click **Present**, **Absent**, **Late** or **Excused**
on each child's row. See *How to mark a child present for today*. This page works on
today.

**How do I mark a kid present?**
Click **Present** on their row. See *How to mark a child present for today*. The
check-in time is stamped with the current clock time for you.

**How do I check children in?**
Marking them **Present** or **Late** is the check-in. See *How to mark a child present
for today*. Use **Edit** if you need a specific time rather than the current one.

**Why did the check-in time fill itself in?**
That is how it works — the current clock time is stamped when you mark a child
**Present** or **Late**. If a time is already there it is kept. Use **Edit** to set a
different one. See *How to correct a child's attendance times or notes*.

**How do I mark a child absent?**
Click **Absent** on their row. See *How to mark a child absent or excused*. This clears
the check-in and check-out times, and the reason goes in the **Notes** box under
**Edit**.

**What's the difference between Absent and Excused?**
**Absent** means the child is away without it being arranged; **Excused** means it was
authorised in advance — a holiday or an appointment. See *How to mark a child absent or
excused*. Both count towards total absent days on reports.

**How do I mark a child on leave?**
Use **Excused** for an authorised absence. **On Leave** is for staff only. See *How to
mark a child absent or excused*.

**How do I check a child out at home time?**
Click **Checkout** on their row. See *How to check a child out at the end of the day*.
The current clock time is stamped; use **Edit** for a different one.

**How do I record who collected a child?**
Type their name into the **Notes** box in the attendance **Edit** panel. See *How to
check a child out at the end of the day*. Keep the child's **Pickup** tab up to date as
your list of who is authorised.

**Can I fix yesterday's register?**
The attendance boards work on today, so corrections need to be made on the day. If you
find a mistake later, note it for your records outside the portal and tell your room
lead. See *How to view attendance for a past date* for the read-only view of the past.

**Can I back-date attendance?**
Attendance is recorded for today only. Make it part of the morning and home-time
routine so each register is complete before the day ends. See *How to mark a child
present for today*.

**How do I see last month's attendance?**
Open **Attendance Reports** and set the **From** and **To** dates. See *How to view
attendance for a past date*. It is a read-only view, and the range covers up to about a
year.

**What does "Not Marked" mean?**
Nobody has recorded anything for that person today. It does not mean absent. Go back to
**Children Attendance** and mark the missing rows. See *How to mark a child present for
today*.

**Present and Absent don't add up to my roll.**
Children whose register was never marked count as neither. Mark the missing rows and
the figures will settle. See *How to mark a child present for today*.

**What do the letters P, A, L, V and E mean on the report?**
**P** is Present, **A** is Absent, **L** is Late, **V** is a staff member on leave and
**E** is a child excused. A hyphen means nothing was recorded. See *How to view
attendance for a past date*.

**Why is V used for leave instead of L?**
**L** is already in use for Late. **V** and **E** look similar on screen, so hover over
the letter to read the tooltip if you are unsure. See *How to view attendance for a
past date*.

**How do I clock staff in?**
Open **Staff Attendance** and click **Check in** on their row. See *How to clock a
staff member in*. The current time is stamped and their status is set to present.

**How do I clock an employee out?**
Click **Checkout** on their row. See *How to clock a staff member out*. The current
time is stamped and their status is kept.

**How do I mark a staff member on leave?**
Click the **Leave** quick button, or choose **On Leave** in the **Edit** dialog. See
*How to mark a staff member absent or on leave*. The quick button clears both times;
the **Edit** dialog leaves them alone.

**There's no Late button for staff.**
Set lateness through the **Edit** dialog's **Status** dropdown. See *How to mark a
staff member late or set exact staff times*.

**How do I filter the register by room?**
Use the classroom filter at the top of the attendance board. See *How to filter the
attendance board by classroom*.

**Can I export the register?**
Yes, from **Attendance Reports** — **CSV** gives you a spreadsheet file and **Print /
PDF** opens a print window. See *How to export a list to a spreadsheet*. The summary
strip at the top is not included in either.

**Does the portal warn me about staff-to-child ratios?**
No. Check your numbers yourself against the classroom occupancy figures on the **Live
Dashboard**. See *How to read the Live Dashboard*.

**Weekends show on the report but not in the totals.**
Saturday and Sunday are left out of every total, though they still appear as tinted
columns. Public holidays count as ordinary working days, so allow for them when you
quote a figure. See *How to view attendance for a past date*.

**I marked someone absent by mistake after checking them in.**
Marking **Absent** clears both times, so re-mark them **Present** and set the correct
check-in time through **Edit**. See *How to correct a child's attendance times or
notes*.

---

## Daily reports

**How do I write a daily report?**
Open **Daily Reports**, select the child, press **Create Report**, fill in the sections
and save. See *How to create and save a daily report as a draft*.

**Where do I do the journal for each child?**
The journal is the daily report. Open **Daily Reports**. See *How to create and save a
daily report as a draft*.

**Where's the diary?**
Same page — Peekaboo calls it the daily report. See *How to create and save a daily
report as a draft*.

**Where's the day sheet?**
That is the daily report, on **Daily Reports**. See *How to create and save a daily
report as a draft*.

**How do I log what a child ate?**
In the **Meals & Drinks** section — pick **All**, **Most**, **Some** or **None** per
meal and add a comment. See *How to create and save a daily report as a draft*. Put
anything about the afternoon snack in another meal's comment or in the note to parents,
so it is certain to reach the family.

**Does "Publish" send the report to the parent?**
No — **Publish** marks the report finished. To send it, use **Publish & Email**, or the
envelope icon on the child's card afterwards. See *How to publish a daily report and
email it to the parent*. This is the single most useful thing to remember on this page.

**How do I email a report I already saved?**
Click the envelope icon on the child's card in the list view. See *How to email an
already-saved daily report*. If it is greyed out, no report exists yet for that date.

**Can I tell whether a parent opened the report?**
No. The footer always reads **"Parent has not viewed yet"** whatever has happened —
it is a fixed label rather than a read receipt. If it matters, ask the family directly.

**How do I attach a photo to a daily report?**
Photos go to parents through the **Media** tab on **Messages** rather than on the
report. See *How to send photos to parents*.

**Where do I record a nappy change?**
Put it in the **Note to parents** box, which is the free-text field parents always see.
See *How to create and save a daily report as a draft*.

**A child was unwell today — where do I put that?**
Write it in the **Note to parents** box, record anything clinical on the child's
**Health** tab, and tell the room lead in person. Health status and temperature
selections on the report are not retained, so the note and the conversation are what
carry the information. See *How to record an allergy, medical condition or special
diet*.

**Where do I put a temperature reading?**
Type the reading into the **Note to parents** box and tell the room lead in person.
Temperature selections on the report are not retained, so the note is the record that
reaches the parent. See *How to create and save a daily report as a draft*.

**I sent a child home unwell — how do I record it?**
Write it in the **Note to parents** box, add anything clinical to the child's **Health**
tab, and tell the room lead in person. The health status selection on the report is not
retained, so always use the note. See *How to create and save a daily report as a
draft*.

**How do I record a nap properly?**
Always fill in the **From** and **To** times — those are what the parent sees. Add any
detail about how the nap went, such as a short or missed nap, in the note to parents.
See *How to create and save a daily report as a draft*.

**The mood I picked reads as "Good".**
**Good** and **Okay** read back the same way, and an untouched report also publishes as
**Good**. If the mood matters, describe it in the **Note to parents** box. See *How to
create and save a daily report as a draft*.

**Where do I record medication given today?**
Use the free-text **Medicine** box in the **Daily Care** section, and tell the room lead
in person. See *How to create and save a daily report as a draft*.

**Can I change a report after publishing?**
Yes — reports are not locked. Edit the teacher note with the pencil icon or re-open the
report. See *How to edit the teacher note on a report already sent*. Editing does not
re-send the email, so send it again if the parent needs to see the change.

**Can I unpublish a report?**
Press **Save Draft** after publishing and it goes back to draft. See *How to publish a
daily report*.

**What does "Report not created" mean?**
No report has been written for that child on that date. Create one and the envelope
button becomes available. See *How to create and save a daily report as a draft*.

**What's the difference between Draft and Published?**
A **Draft** is saved but not marked finished; **Published** means finished. Neither one
emails the parent — only **Publish & Email** or the envelope does that. See *How to
publish a daily report*.

**How do I look at an old daily report?**
Change the date on **Daily Reports** and open the child's card. See *How to review a
daily report for a past date*.

**A child is missing from the daily reports list.**
Check that their registration covers that date and that the weekday is among their
enrolled days. See *How to register a child into a class*.

**Can I print a daily report?**
Email it to the parent instead, with **Publish & Email** or the envelope icon. See *How
to publish a daily report and email it to the parent*.

**My teacher note turned up in the wrong box.**
Always fill **Note to parents** first. If you only have behaviour text to record, put
something in the first box too, and the two stay where you expect. See *How to create
and save a daily report as a draft*.

**Which rows appear on a class's daily report?**
That is set per class, in the class's daily-report settings.

---

## Curriculum and assessment

**How do I set up a new room?**
Create a class. You will need an age group first. See *How to create an age group*.

**How do I add a group?**
If you mean a room, create a class. If you mean an age band, create an **Age Group**.
See *How to create an age group*.

**How do I create an age group?**
Open **Age Groups**, add one and choose its curriculum. See *How to create an age
group*. Always fill in the description, because other screens show that rather than the
code.

**Does the number of children on a class update itself?**
No — it is a capacity figure you type by hand. Children are enrolled from their own
record. See *How to register a child into a class*.

**How do I set up our curriculum?**
Open **Curriculum** and create it, then add learning areas, aspects and attainment
levels beneath it. See *How to create a curriculum*.

**What's the difference between a learning area and an aspect?**
A learning area is a top-level division of the curriculum; an aspect is a sub-division
inside it, and aspects hold the objectives. See *How to add a learning area* and *How
to add an aspect to a learning area*.

**What does "aspect" mean?**
A sub-area of a learning area, where the objectives live. The printed assessment calls
it a sub-domain. See *How to add an aspect to a learning area*.

**What does "attainment" mean?**
A level on your nursery's own grading scale for how securely a child has grasped an
objective. See *How to set up attainment levels*.

**What are the standard attainment levels?**
There is no built-in scale — whatever levels exist are the ones your nursery created.
Ask your lead rather than assuming. See *How to set up attainment levels*.

**The rating dropdown is empty and I can't save an observation.**
No attainment levels have been set up yet, and every selected objective needs a rating.
Create the levels first. See *How to set up attainment levels*.

**How do I record an observation?**
Open **Observations**, pick class, teacher, child, curriculum, learning area, aspect and
age group, then link at least one objective with a rating and save. See *How to record
an observation*.

**Where do I write down a wow moment I saw?**
That is an observation. See *How to record an observation*.

**What does "objective" mean?**
A single learning goal a child is assessed against, found by combining an aspect with an
age group. You pick them inside an observation. See *How to link objectives to an
observation*.

**How do I add a new objective?**
Objectives are read-only in the portal. Work with the ones already set up for your
curriculum, inside **Observations**. See *How to link objectives to an observation*.

**Can I edit an observation after saving?**
Yes, apart from **Class**, **Teacher** and **Child**, which are fixed once saved.
Ratings can be changed freely. See *How to view, edit or delete a saved observation*.

**How do I make an end-of-term report for a parent?**
Use **Assessment Report** — set the date range and pick one specific class. See *How to
generate an assessment report*.

**My assessment report is empty.**
No observations fall inside the date range you chose. Record observations first, or
widen the range. See *How to generate an assessment report*.

**Why can't I run an assessment report for all classes?**
Generation needs one specific class, so change the dropdown from **All classes** every
time. See *How to generate an assessment report*.

**How do I print a child's progress report?**
Generate the assessment report, then use the per-child **Assessment Report** button or
the toolbar print option and choose **Save as PDF**. See *How to export or print an
assessment report*.

**What's the difference between an observation and an assessment?**
An observation is a single recorded moment tagged against objectives; the assessment
pages summarise all of them. See *How to record an observation* and *How to read the
Assessments summary page*.

**Where do I see how a class is progressing?**
On the **Assessments** summary page. See *How to read the Assessments summary page*.

**Observation notes I typed have gone.**
Put the detail that matters into the objective ratings and the fields that appear on the
saved observation, and keep your own written notes alongside as you always would. See
*How to record an observation*.

---

## Messages and calendar

**How do I send a message to parents?**
Open **Messages**, pick the post type tab, write the post and publish it. See *How to
send a message to one parent*.

**How do I send an announcement to everyone?**
Use **Messages** and select the whole nursery rather than a class. See *How to message
every parent in the nursery*.

**How do I message a whole room?**
Use **Messages** and choose that class. See *How to message a whole class*.

**Can parents reply to a message?**
No — **Messages** is a one-way noticeboard to the parent app. Families contact you by
phone or email, and you reply the same way. See *How to send a message to one parent*.

**Where is the parent inbox?**
There isn't one. **Messages** publishes outward only. Keep your usual phone and email
routes open for families to reach you.

**Can I see whether a parent read a message?**
No. The only status on a post is your own published or unpublished state. If a message
is important, follow it up directly.

**How do I send photos to parents?**
Use the **Media** tab on **Messages**. See *How to send photos to parents*. Images need
to be JPEG or PNG and under 1 MB each.

**How do I send a video?**
Use the **Video Media** tab on **Messages**. See *How to send a video to parents*. MP4,
WebM, OGG or MOV, up to 35 MB.

**Can I write a message now and send it later?**
Yes — save it without publishing, then publish when you are ready. See *How to write a
message now and publish it later*. An unpublished post shows an amber dot and parents
cannot see it.

**How do I edit a message I already sent?**
Find the post in the history and edit it. See *How to edit a message you have already
sent*.

**How do I unsend a message?**
Deleting removes the post going forward, but anyone who already saw it has seen it. See
*How to delete a message*. If a correction matters, publish a follow-up post saying so.

**How do I check a message went out?**
Look for it in the message history with its published state. See *How to confirm a
message went out*.

**How do I find an old message?**
Use the message history and its search. See *How to find an old message*.

**How do I add an event to the calendar?**
Open **Calendar**, add the event and give it a title, a start date and a type. See *How
to add a calendar event*. The type sets the colour.

**How do I add a trip or a parents evening?**
Add it as a calendar event with type **Trip** or **Meeting**. See *How to add a calendar
