event*. To be sure parents see it, also publish an **Event** post on **Messages**.

**How do I make an event repeat every week?**
Create each occurrence by hand — there is no repeat option. See *How to add a calendar
event*.

**The "Branches" field on an event is showing classes.**
Those buttons are classes, and you can pick one. Leave them all unselected for a
nursery-wide event. See *How to add a calendar event*.

**Do parents see calendar events?**
Treat the calendar as your staff planner. If parents must see something, publish it as
an **Event** post on **Messages**. See *How to message every parent in the nursery*.

**How do I delete a calendar event?**
Use the delete control on the event. See *How to delete a calendar event*. Deleting on
the calendar happens straight away, so check you have the right event first.

---

## Admissions enquiries

**How do I add a new enquiry?**
Open **CRM Leads** and click **Add**, then fill in the required fields. See *How to add
a new enquiry*.

**Where do I put a lead?**
On **CRM Leads**. A lead, an enquiry, an applicant and a prospect are all the same
record here. See *How to add a new enquiry*.

**Where do I put an applicant?**
Applicants are enquiries — add them on **CRM Leads**. See *How to add a new enquiry*.

**How do I record a prospect who called about a place?**
Add them as an enquiry on **CRM Leads**, filling the form top to bottom. See *How to
add a new enquiry*.

**Where's the waiting list?**
The **CRM Leads** list is your pipeline. The dashboard's **Waiting List** figure counts
the enquiries at the admission stage. See *How to search, sort and filter the enquiries
list*.

**I can't save a new enquiry.**
The referral source and record source are both required on every enquiry, and they must
already exist. Ask an administrator to add the entry you need. See *How to add a
referral or a communication source*.

**A new enquiry already shows consent ticked and SEN set to Yes.**
Those are the form's starting values. Set consent and the SEN question deliberately for
every family as part of your admissions script, rather than leaving them as they are.
See *How to add a new enquiry*.

**How do I book a tour?**
Set the enquiry's status to **Book a Tour** and give the follow-up task a reminder date.
See *How to book and record a nursery tour*. The status and its task are the tour
record.

**Where do I see the tours booked this week?**
Filter or scan the **CRM Leads** list for enquiries at **Book a Tour** and check the
task dates. See *How to search, sort and filter the enquiries list*.

**How do I change an enquiry's status?**
Click the status badge, pick a new one and set the reminder date. See *How to change an
enquiry's status*. Every status change creates a task.

**I chose "Tour Taken" and it now says KYC.**
Those two names refer to the same stage, and different screens use different wording.
Read the badge text and agree with your team which name you use internally. See *How to
change an enquiry's status*.

**What does KYC mean?**
It is the information-gathering and verification stage on an enquiry. The status menu
also calls it **Tour Taken**, so check which wording your team uses. See *How to change
an enquiry's status*.

**What does "Book a Tour" mean?**
A nursery visit is being arranged and has not happened yet. The dashboard shows the
same stage as **Toured**, so go by the badge on the enquiry. See *How to change an
enquiry's status*.

**What does "Admission" mean on an enquiry?**
The family is moving into the application stage. The dashboard calls the same stage
**Application** and **Waiting List**. See *How to change an enquiry's status*.

**What's the difference between Lost and Rejected?**
**Lost** means the family withdrew or went elsewhere; **Rejected** means the nursery
turned them down. See *How to mark an enquiry as lost or rejected*. Read the badge
text rather than the colour — only **Lost** is red.

**How do I mark an enquiry as lost?**
Click the status badge, choose **Lost**, set the reminder date and record why in the
admin comment. See *How to mark an enquiry as lost or rejected*.

**Where do I write why a family didn't join?**
In the **Admin Comment** on the move dialog, or as a note on the enquiry. See *How to
add a note to an enquiry*.

**How do I accept an application?**
Press **Approve** in the enquiry's detail header. See *How to record that an enquiry
has been accepted*.

**The Approve button is greyed out.**
The enquiry has already been accepted — the button reads **Approved** once it is spent.
Move the enquiry to another status from the badge menu if you need to re-do it. See
*How to change an enquiry's status*.

**What happens after I approve an enquiry?**
Nothing happens on its own. Create the child record yourself and carry on with
enrolment. See *How to move an accepted family into enrolment*.

**How do I turn an accepted enquiry into a child?**
By hand. Copy the details from the enquiry's detail panel and create the child from
scratch. See *How to move an accepted family into enrolment* and *How to add a new
child*.

**What's the difference between registration and enrolment?**
On an enquiry, **Registered** is the end of the admissions pipeline. Actual enrolment is
a **registration** on the child record — the link between a child, a class, a session,
a date range and weekdays. See *How to register a child into a class*.

**I can't set an enquiry to "Registered".**
Add a **task** on the enquiry and set the task's lead status to **Registered**. See
*How to create a follow-up task on an enquiry*.

**How do I set a follow-up reminder on an enquiry?**
Add a task with a date and a reminder date. See *How to create a follow-up task on an
enquiry*. Check your enquiries yourself on those dates — nothing prompts you.

**Does anything remind me on the reminder date?**
No. Build a habit of reviewing the **CRM Leads** list and its task dates as part of your
weekly routine. See *How to search, sort and filter the enquiries list*.

**How do I add a note about what we discussed with a family?**
Use **Notes** on the enquiry. See *How to add a note to an enquiry*. The date is set to
today for you.

**Where do I record how a family heard about us?**
In both the **Referral** and the **Communication Source** on the enquiry. See *How to
attach a referral or source to an enquiry*.

**How do I add a new referral source?**
Open **Referrals & Sources** and add it. See *How to add a referral or a communication
source*. Only an administrator can open that page.

**I can't open Referrals & Sources.**
That page is for administrators. Ask yours to add the entry you need. See *How to add a
referral or a communication source*.

**How do I email a prospective parent?**
Create a task on the enquiry, or move its status, and type the message there. See *How
to create a follow-up task on an enquiry*. Editing a task afterwards does not send
anything again.

**How do I change the wording emailed to enquiries?**
Edit the email template for that status. See *How to edit an admissions email template*.
Save each status before switching to another, and remember that editing a template
sends nothing by itself.

**How do I send a payment link to a family?**
Copy the link out of the composed message and send it from your own email. See *How to
edit an admissions email template*.

**How do I export the enquiries list?**
Use **Export to Excel** on the dashboard's CRM details view, which gives you a
spreadsheet file. See *How to export admissions figures*.

**The dashboard CRM numbers don't match the enquiries list.**
The dashboard uses different wording for the same stages. Trust the badge on the
enquiry row. See *How to search, sort and filter the enquiries list*.

**I edited an enquiry task and its attachments are no longer listed.**
Re-attach the file when you save the edit, and keep the original somewhere outside the
portal. See *How to create a follow-up task on an enquiry*.

---

## Invoices and payments

**How do I bill a parent?**
Create an invoice against the child. See *How to create an invoice*. You need at least
one service to exist first.

**How do I raise a bill for fees?**
A bill is an invoice here, raised against the child rather than the parent. See *How to
create an invoice*.

**Where do I charge tuition?**
On an invoice, picking the tuition **Service** on a line. See *How to create an
invoice*. The fee fills in from the service and you can overtype it.

**How do I mark an invoice as paid?**
Invoices carry no paid or unpaid status. Record the money as a receipt, then read the
family's position on **Balance by Class**. See *How to record a payment and create a
receipt* and *How to view outstanding balances by class*.

**Where do I see if a bill is paid or unpaid?**
Use **Balance by Class**, which shows each child's billed total, received total and the
difference. See *How to view outstanding balances by class*.

**How do I link a payment to an invoice?**
Receipts are attached to a **child** rather than to a single invoice. Write the invoice
reference into the receipt's payment detail text so a colleague can match them by eye.
See *How to record a payment and create a receipt*.

**How do I record that a parent paid?**
Create a receipt for that child with at least one payment line. See *How to record a
payment and create a receipt*. Remember to click **Add Line** — the payment table
starts empty.

**Where's the money stuff?**
Money coming in is the **Account** group — **Services**, **Receipts**, **Invoices**,
**Payment Modes** and **Balance by Class**. Money going out is the **Finance** group —
**Suppliers**, **Expenses**, **Expense Types**, **Payment Voucher** and **Purchases**.

**My receipt won't save.**
Check that the **Description** is filled in and that you have added at least one
complete payment line. See *How to record a payment and create a receipt*.

**The receipt form doesn't show a running total.**
Add the amounts up yourself before saving if the figure matters. The total appears on
the saved receipt. See *How to record a payment and create a receipt*.

**How do I print a receipt for a parent?**
Open the receipt and click **Print**, then choose **Save as PDF** or print it. See *How
to print or email a receipt*.

**How do I email a receipt?**
Open the receipt and click **Email**. See *How to print or email a receipt*. This sends
a properly formatted attachment and is the most reliable document route in the portal.

**How do I email an invoice to a parent?**
Open the invoice and click **Email**. See *How to print or email an invoice*. The child
needs a linked parent with an email address on their record.

**Who owes us money?**
**Balance by Class**. See *How to view outstanding balances by class*. It shows billed,
received and the difference for every child.

**Where do I see arrears?**
On **Balance by Class**. See *How to view outstanding balances by class*.

**A family shows a grey balance — do they owe us?**
Yes. Read the sign in front of the figure rather than the colour: a **+** means the
family still owes, and a **-** means they have paid more than they were billed. See
*How to view outstanding balances by class*.

**Why do plus and minus amounts look the same size?**
All three columns show the amount without a minus sign, so the direction is carried by
the **+**, **-** or **=** prefix. Read the prefix every time. See *How to view
outstanding balances by class*.

**Can I get a class total from Balance by Class?**
The class chips filter the list rather than producing a subtotal. Add the rows up
yourself, or copy them into a spreadsheet. See *How to view outstanding balances by
class*.

**Can I see one month only on Balance by Class?**
It runs from your very first record up to the optional **As of** cut-off, so you can ask
for "everything up to 31 March" rather than March alone. For a single month, compare
two cut-off dates. See *How to view outstanding balances by class*.

**How do I add a fee item to the price list?**
Add a **Service**, with its fee, cost, VAT percentage and type. See *How to add a
service*. Services must exist before any invoice line can be saved.

**Where do I set VAT on a fee?**
On the service — each carries its own default VAT percentage and a **Tax Exempt** flag.
See *How to add a service*. On an invoice line the VAT percentage is fixed.

**Can I change a price on one invoice without changing the price list?**
Yes — the line **Fee** is editable even though it fills in from the service. See *How to
create an invoice*.

**I changed a service's price and old invoices didn't change.**
That is intended. Each invoice line keeps the fee and VAT it was saved with, so your
history stays accurate. Edit an individual invoice if an old one genuinely needs
correcting. See *How to create an invoice*.

**How do I give a discount?**
Per line, as a percentage between 0 and 100. See *How to create an invoice*.

**How do I add cash, cheque or card as a payment option?**
Create them as **Payment Modes**. See *How to add a payment mode*. A payment mode must
exist before any receipt can be saved.

**Does recording a cheque mean the money has cleared?**
The portal treats a cheque as received the moment you record it. Hold off recording it
until it has cleared if your bookkeeping needs that. See *How to record a payment and
create a receipt*.

**Can I invoice a whole class at once?**
Invoices are created one child at a time. Set aside a billing session each month and
work down your class list. See *How to create an invoice*.

**Can I set up recurring monthly invoices?**
Each invoice is created by hand for each period. Put a repeating reminder in your own
diary for billing day. See *How to create an invoice*.

**Can I delete an invoice after the family has paid?**
You can, so be careful — finance records are not locked and there is no undo. Check
twice before deleting anything on **Invoices**. See *How to create an invoice*.

**Why does everything say AED?**
Amounts are labelled AED throughout. The figures themselves are correct — read the
label as "your nursery's currency".

**Can I change the currency?**
The label is fixed. Agree with your team that it means your own currency, and state the
currency plainly on anything you send outside the portal.

**Can I export the invoice list?**
Print or email one invoice at a time from the invoice itself. For a list, copy the
figures into a spreadsheet by hand. See *How to print or email an invoice*.

**A printed receipt has "INV#" in the header.**
The number itself is the correct receipt number. Ignore the label. See *How to print or
email a receipt*.

**How do I search or sort invoices and receipts?**
Use the search box and click a column heading to sort. See *How to search and sort
invoices or receipts*.

---

## Expenses and suppliers

**How do I add a vendor?**
Add a supplier. See *How to add a supplier*. A supplier is needed on every expense,
purchase and payment voucher.

**Where do I record that we spent money?**
As an expense, against a supplier and an expense type. See *How to record an expense*.

**How do I attach the receipt for a cost?**
Use the **Attachment** field on the expense. See *How to record an expense*. Files need
to be under 2 MB.

**My expense attachment won't upload.**
It is almost certainly over the 2 MB limit. Compress or rescan the document at a lower
resolution and try again. See *How to record an expense*.

**How do I set up cost categories?**
Create **Expense Types**. See *How to create an expense type*. An expense type must
exist before an expense can be saved.

**Where do I record a purchase order?**
On the **Purchases** page, line by line with VAT. See *How to raise a purchase*. A
purchase and an expense are separate records.

**My purchase lines all went blank.**
Set **Tax Exempt** before you start entering lines on a purchase. If you switch it
part-way, re-enter the lines. See *How to raise a purchase*.

**How do I pay a supplier?**
Record a payment voucher. See *How to create a payment voucher*.

**What is a payment voucher?**
The record of money leaving your bank to a supplier, split across payment lines. See
*How to create a payment voucher*. A parent payment is a receipt, not a voucher.

**How do I approve an expense before it is paid?**
Agree approvals in your usual way outside the portal, then record the expense once
approved. Records are live the moment they are saved. See *How to record an expense*.

**A supplier has stopped trading — how do I hide them?**
Leave them in place and simply stop selecting them, which keeps your history intact. If
you need the list tidy, rename them with a prefix such as "ZZ" so they sort to the
bottom. See *How to add a supplier*.

**How do I export the expenses table?**
Use the **Copy**, **PDF**, **Print** and **CSV** buttons above the table. See *How to
export a finance table*.

**My export contains more rows than I filtered to.**
The finance exports give you the full table. Download it and filter in your spreadsheet
instead. See *How to export a finance table*.

**My downloaded file has no date in its name.**
Rename it with the date as soon as it downloads, so a second download does not sit
beside it as a copy. See *How to export a finance table*.

**Is there a profit figure anywhere?**
Not in the portal. Export your income and expense tables and work it out in a
spreadsheet. See *How to export a finance table*.

---

## Nursery settings

**Where do I change our nursery's phone number?**
On **My Center**. See *How to update your nursery's details*.

**How do I change our address or email?**
On **My Center**, which holds your nursery's name, address, phones, email, logo and
sessions. See *How to update your nursery's details*.

**How do I change our logo?**
On **My Center**, in the logo field. See *How to change your nursery's logo*. Use a JPG
or PNG.

**What's the difference between Centers and My Center?**
**My Center** is your own nursery's settings — the one you want. **Centers** is a
platform-level screen and is not part of everyday nursery work. See *How to update your
nursery's details*.

**How do I add a new branch?**
Contact your Peekaboo provider. Adding a centre is done at platform level, not from your
nursery's own settings.

**How do I switch between our campuses?**
Which centre you see is fixed by the account you signed in with. Sign in with an account
belonging to the other branch.

**How do I set up our morning and afternoon slots?**
Add them as **Sessions** on **My Center**. See *How to add a session*. Only active
sessions appear in the **Timing** dropdown when registering a child.

**What are Packages?**
Your nursery's own subscription to Peekaboo, bought by card. See *How to buy or renew
your subscription*. It has nothing to do with billing parents.

**How do I buy or renew our subscription?**
On the **Packages** page. See *How to buy or renew your subscription*. Purchasing is
held while a subscription is already live or a payment is being confirmed.

**What does "sub_valid" mean?**
Your nursery has a live, paid subscription, and buying another is held back. See *How to
buy or renew your subscription*.

**What does "sub_payment_pending" mean?**
A payment has been submitted and is being confirmed, so purchasing is held to avoid
double-charging. Wait, and contact your provider if it persists. See *How to buy or
renew your subscription*.

**What's the difference between a service and a package?**
A **Service** is a billable item you charge parents for. A **Package** is your nursery's
own Peekaboo subscription. See *How to add a service* and *How to buy or renew your
subscription*.

---

## Roles and access

**How do I give someone access to a page?**
Find the access name that controls it, tick it for their **role**, then have them sign
out and back in. See *How to give someone access to a page*.

**Who can change permissions?**
Your nursery's **Admin**. If **Roles & Access** is not in your sidebar, you are not
that role. See *How to change what a role can access*.

**I can't see Roles & Access.**
That page belongs to the Admin role. Ask your administrator for what you need. See *How
to give someone access to a page*.

**How do I create a new role?**
On **Roles & Access**, click **Add role**, name it and save — then grant its modules.
See *How to create a role*. A new role starts with nothing ticked.

**Can I give one person extra access without changing everyone?**
Access is per role. Create a role for what that person needs, grant it, and reassign
them on the **Staff** page. See *How to create a role*.

**I ticked the box but they still can't see the page.**
Access is read when the portal loads, so they need to sign out and back in. See *How to
give someone access to a page*.

**What are the roles?**
Principal, Registrar, Teacher, Assistant, Accountant, Admin and Guest, plus an unassigned
state. See *How to find out what your role is*. What each one can actually open depends
on what your administrator ticked.

**What can a Principal do?**
Everything. The Principal sees every page in the portal. See *How to find out what your
role is*.

**What does a Registrar do?**
Front-desk work: enquiries and tours, child and parent records, and enrolling children
into classes. Their exact access is whatever your administrator granted. See *How to
find out what your role is*.

**Can a Teacher see invoices?**
Only if someone ticked **Invoices** for the Teacher role. Nothing is fixed by the role
name alone. See *How to change what a role can access*.

**What is a Guest account for?**
A deliberately limited visitor, auditor or inspector account. What it can open depends
on what was granted to it. See *How to find out what your role is*.

**My role badge shows a name I don't recognise.**
It shows whatever name your account was given, which may be a custom label such as "Vice
Principal". See *How to find out what your role is*.

**Why do teachers get the staff clock-in sheet too?**
One access setting — **Daily Report** — opens all four attendance and daily-report
pages together. See *How to change what a role can access*.

**Someone can see arrears when I only gave them Invoices.**
**Balance by Class** opens for anyone holding **Balance by Class**, **Invoices** or
**Receipts**. Bear that in mind when granting finance access. See *How to change what a
role can access*.

**Can I delete a role?**
Yes, but check the **Staff** page first to see who holds it. Anyone still assigned to a
deleted role will need reassigning before they can work. See *How to deactivate or
delete a role*.

**Deactivating a role didn't ask me to confirm.**
The status dot on a role card flips on one click. Click it deliberately, and prefer
editing a role's access over switching it off. See *How to deactivate or delete a role*.

**How do I see everything a role can open?**
Open **Roles & Access**, click **Manage access** on that role and read the ticked tiles.
See *How to change what a role can access*.

---

## Finding and exporting information

**How do I search a list?**
Type into the search box at the top of the list. See *How to search a list*. Search
looks at the fields that page nominates, usually the name, and only within the rows
already loaded.

**How do I sort a table?**
Click a column heading. See *How to sort a table by a column*. Blank values always sort
to the bottom.

**How do I show more rows at once?**
Use the **Show N entries** control where a page has one. See *How to change how many
rows show*. The options are 10, 25 and 50.

**How do I move between pages of a list?**
Use the page buttons at the foot of the table. See *How to move between pages of a
list*. Searching takes you back to page one; sorting leaves you where you are.

**How do I filter a list by date?**
Use the **From** and **To** date controls where the page has them. See *How to filter a
list by date*.

**How do I export attendance?**
Open **Attendance Reports**, set the range, then click **CSV** for a file or **Print /
PDF** for a print window. See *How to export a list to a spreadsheet*.

**How do I download a list as a spreadsheet?**
**Expenses**, **Purchases** and **Payment Voucher** have a **Copy / PDF / Print / CSV**
toolbar, **Attendance Reports** has **CSV**, and the dashboard's CRM details view has
**Export to Excel**. See *How to export a finance table*. Elsewhere, copy the figures by
hand.

**Which pages have no export?**
**Balance by Class**, the invoice and receipt lists, the enquiries list, daily reports,
**Suppliers**, **Expense Types**, **Services** and **Payment Modes**. Copy what you need
into a spreadsheet, or print the page. See *How to print a page or a record*.

**How do I save something as a PDF?**
Use the page's **Print** or **PDF** button, then choose **Save as PDF** as the
destination in your browser's print dialog. See *How to print a page or a record*.

**I clicked Print and nothing happened.**
Your browser blocked the print window. Allow pop-ups for the portal and try again. See
*How to print a page or a record*.

**Will Arabic export properly?**
Yes. Spreadsheet files carry the marker Excel needs to open Arabic correctly, and print
windows keep the right-to-left direction.

**Why are my export headings in English?**
The admissions export keeps English column headings so the file stays consistent between
downloads. Translate them after export if you need to. See *How to export admissions
figures*.

**How do I copy a finance table quickly?**
Use the **Copy** button above the table and paste into your spreadsheet. See *How to
copy a finance table to your clipboard*.

**How do I read the Live Dashboard?**
It has three tabs — **Main**, **Finance** and **CRM**. See *How to read the Live
Dashboard*. It refreshes itself about once a minute.

**The dashboard numbers look wrong.**
Unmarked registers are the usual cause, since those children count as neither present
nor absent. Reload the page before acting on a figure that reads zero. See *How to read
the Live Dashboard*.

**Is the green "Live" badge telling me something?**
It is decoration and always reads **Live**. Reload the page if you want to be sure a
figure is current. See *How to read the Live Dashboard*.

**What does "Active Staff" on the dashboard mean?**
It counts employees whose record is active — who is on the payroll, not who clocked in
today. For attendance use **Staff Attendance**. See *How to clock a staff member in*.

**What does "Late" mean on the Finance tab?**
Billed minus collected for that month. It is not an overdue figure. For a genuine
arrears position use **Balance by Class**. See *How to view outstanding balances by
class*.

**Can I trust the "Days Late" pill?**
Read it as "days since we billed" rather than days overdue, and treat its colour as
decoration. Use **Balance by Class** for the real position. See *How to view outstanding
balances by class*.

**How do I look at an older month on the dashboard?**
Use the **Custom Date** control on the **Finance** tab — the month pills only go one
month back. See *How to change the dashboard's date range*. Your choice resets when you
switch tabs or reload.

**Can I filter the admissions statistics by date?**
The month arrows move the CRM calendar only; the cards, graphs and details table are
all-time figures. See *How to read the Live Dashboard*.

**The two admissions donut charts look the same.**
Read the numbered cards and the source and referral bar charts, which are the clearest
view of your pipeline. See *How to read the Live Dashboard*.

**Where are the analytics?**
The **Live Dashboard** is your overview. See *How to read the Live Dashboard*.

**Can I select several rows and act on them at once?**
Every action is one row at a time. Plan bulk work as a short focused session rather than
looking for a select-all. See *How to search a list*.

**My search finds nothing even though I can see the record.**
Search looks at the fields that page nominates — usually the name — and only within
rows already loaded. Search the name, or use the page's own filters. See *How to search
a list*.

**My filters reset when I switched from Children to Staff.**
Choose the person type first, then set your filters. See *How to view attendance for a
past date*.

---

## What the portal does and doesn't do

**Can I mark an invoice as paid?**
No — invoices carry no paid or unpaid status. Record the money as a receipt against the
child, then use **Balance by Class** to see what that family still owes. See *How to
record a payment and create a receipt* and *How to view outstanding balances by class*.

**Can I edit attendance for a past date?**
No — the attendance boards work on today only. Make completing the register part of the
morning and home-time routine, and use **Attendance Reports** to view past days
read-only. See *How to view attendance for a past date*.

**Can I reply to a message from a parent?**
No — **Messages** publishes outward to the parent app and has no inbox. Reply by phone
or email as you normally would, and publish a new post if the whole room needs to know.
See *How to send a message to one parent*.

**Can I tell whether a parent read a message?**
No — there are no read receipts anywhere. If something is important, follow it up by
phone. See *How to confirm a message went out*.

**Can I attach photos to a daily report?**
No — the daily report has no photo upload. Send photos to parents through the **Media**
tab on **Messages** instead, which is built for exactly that. See *How to send photos to
parents*.

**Can I record who collected a child?**
Not as a dedicated field. Type the collector's name into the **Notes** box in the
attendance **Edit** panel, and keep the child's **Pickup** tab current as your list of
who is authorised. See *How to check a child out at the end of the day*.

**Does an accepted enquiry become a child record automatically?**
No — you create the child record yourself. Copy the details from the enquiry's detail
panel and add the child, then register them into a class. See *How to move an accepted
family into enrolment*.

**Can I edit a health record?**
Not directly. Delete the record and add a corrected one straight away, in one sitting,
so the right information is always on the child's **Health** tab. See *How to record an
allergy, medical condition or special diet*.

**Can I invoice a whole class in one go?**
No — invoices are created one child at a time. Block out a billing session each month
and work down the class list. See *How to create an invoice*.

**Can I set up recurring invoices?**
No — each period's invoices are created by hand. Put a repeating reminder in your own
diary for billing day. See *How to create an invoice*.

**Can I change the currency?**
No — amounts are labelled AED throughout. The figures are correct, so read the label as
"your nursery's currency" and state your real currency on anything you send outside the
portal.

**Can I undo something?**
No — there is no undo, and deletes are immediate. Prefer setting records **Inactive**
wherever a status toggle exists, and check twice before deleting. See *How to deactivate
a child who has left*.

**Does the portal save as I type?**
No — every form needs an explicit **Save**. Finish each task in one sitting rather than
leaving a form part-filled.

**Does anything happen overnight?**
No — there are no overnight jobs, no automatic register close-off and no automatic
invoicing. Everything in the portal is there because a person entered it.

**Does the portal send emails automatically?**
No — every email is sent by a person clicking a button. There are three: a daily report
to a parent, a receipt or invoice to a parent, and an admissions message on an enquiry.

**Will I be reminded about anything?**
No — there are no reminders, alerts or notifications, including on an enquiry's reminder
date. Build your own routine of checking the pages that matter. See *How to search, sort
and filter the enquiries list*.

**Does the portal alert me about an allergy or a high temperature?**
No. An allergy shows as a red badge on the child's card, which staff need to look for.
For a temperature, write the reading in the **Note to parents** box and tell the room
lead in person.

**Is there an accident or incident log?**
Not in the portal. Record incidents in your nursery's own paper or digital log, note
anything clinical on the child's **Health** tab, and tell parents through the **Note to
parents** box or a phone call. See *How to record an allergy, medical condition or
special diet*.

**Where do parents' complaints go?**
Handle complaints through your nursery's own process. Use **Messages** for anything you
need to broadcast to families. See *How to message every parent in the nursery*.

**Can I see who changed a record?**
No — there is no change history. This is the strongest reason to give every member of
staff their own login rather than sharing one.

**Will the portal warn me before I delete something important?**
Usually not. Treat every delete as final, check what depends on the record first, and
set things **Inactive** where you can instead. See *How to deactivate a child who has
left*.

**Can I export Balance by Class?**
No — that page has no export or print. Export **Invoices** and **Receipts** figures into
a spreadsheet and reconcile there, or copy the rows you need. See *How to export a
finance table*.

**Can I export the enquiries list row by row?**
Not row by row. Use **Export to Excel** on the dashboard's CRM details view for the
summary figures. See *How to export admissions figures*.

**Is there a ratios calculator?**
No. Work your ratios out yourself against the classroom occupancy figures on the **Live
Dashboard**. See *How to read the Live Dashboard*.

**Is there a timesheet or hours-worked total?**
No — clock-in and clock-out times are stored but not totalled. Export the history from
**Attendance Reports** and add it up in a spreadsheet. See *How to export a list to a
spreadsheet*.

**Can I bulk-delete or bulk-edit rows?**
No — every action is one row at a time. There are no tick boxes and no select-all.

**Who do I contact if something isn't working?**
Your nursery's **Admin** for anything about access, and your Peekaboo provider for
everything else. See *Getting Unstuck* for what to gather before you contact them.


---


<!-- ================= 05-getting-unstuck ================= -->

# Getting Unstuck

When the portal does something you did not expect, find the closest heading below. Each
entry explains what is happening in plain terms, tells you exactly what to do, and says
who to ask if it is still not right.

Every heading is written the way you would say it out loud, so scan for the words you
would use.

Two instructions come up often below, so here is exactly what they mean.

**Reload the page** means asking your browser to fetch the page again. Press **F5** on a
Windows computer, or **Cmd+R** on a Mac. You can also click the circular-arrow button
near your browser's address bar.

**Sign out and back in** means clicking the red-outlined **Logout** button at the far end
of the top bar, then signing in again at
**[https://peek.peek-a-boo.app](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/login)**. Closing the browser
is not the same thing and will not have the same effect.

---

### "I can't see a menu item my colleague has."

**What's happening:** Your sidebar lists exactly the pages your **role** is allowed to
open, and hides the rest. Your colleague holds a different role, so their menu is
different. A menu group with nothing in it disappears completely, heading and all,
which makes the two menus look further apart than they really are.

**What to do:**
1. Check your role. It is on the outlined badge in the top bar, just to the right of the
   filled **Peekaboo Care** badge.
2. If you cannot see that badge, your browser window is too narrow. Widen it, or use a
   desktop computer.
3. Ask your colleague to read out their own role badge.
4. If you genuinely need that page, ask your administrator to grant it to your role.
5. Sign out and sign back in afterwards. Access is read when the portal loads, so a
   change made while you are signed in does not appear until you do.

**Still stuck?** Tell your administrator the exact menu item you expect, the role on
your badge, and what you need the page for.

---

### "It says Access denied."

**What's happening:** You reached a page your role does not open — usually by typing an
address, following an old bookmark, or clicking a link a colleague sent. The menu never
disagrees with the rules, so anything not in your sidebar cannot be opened from
elsewhere.

**What to do:**
1. Reload the page once. Occasionally the portal could not confirm your access on the
   first try, and a reload settles it.
2. Navigate from the sidebar rather than from bookmarks.
3. If you need that page as part of your job, ask your administrator to grant it to
   your role, then sign out and back in.

**Still stuck?** Give your administrator the exact web address shown in the browser bar
and your role badge text. Being denied never signs you out — you can carry on working
from the menu straight away.

---

### "It says my role could not be determined."

**What's happening:** You are signed in, but your account has no role attached to it
yet, so the portal cannot work out what you may open. It is usually a newly created
account, or one whose role was removed.

**What to do:**
1. Ask your administrator to open the **Staff** page, find your record and set your
   **Role**.
2. Sign out and sign back in.

**Still stuck?** Tell your administrator your username and the small grey line of text
shown under the message — it tells them exactly what is missing.

---

### "I was signed out in the middle of my work."

**What's happening:** Your session ended and you were returned to the login screen.
Everyday network hiccups do not sign you out, so the usual explanations are that
someone clicked **Logout** on a shared machine, or browsing data was cleared.

**What to do:**
1. Sign in again and carry on.
2. Re-enter anything you had typed but not saved — nothing saves automatically.
3. On shared machines, give each member of staff their own account rather than passing
   one session around.

**Still stuck?** If it happens often on one machine, tell your Peekaboo provider which
computer and browser, and roughly how long you had been working before it happened.

---

### "I closed the browser but I was still signed in when I reopened it."

**What's happening:** That is how the portal works. Your session is kept in the browser
and closing the window does not end it. On a shared reception computer, the next person
to open the portal is still signed in as you.

**What to do:**
1. Always click **Logout** in the top bar at the end of your shift — this is the only
   reliable way to end your session.
2. Make explicit logging out a standing instruction for reception and staffroom
   computers.
3. If you find someone else's session open, log it out before you start.

**Still stuck?** Raise it with your principal as a safeguarding and data point rather
than a technical one. The **Remember me** tick box does not change this.

---

### "I saved it but the list still shows the old value."

**What's happening:** Some lists show their previous contents until they are reloaded.
Your work is saved.

**What to do:**
1. Reload the browser page.
2. If the old value comes back after the reload, open the record, re-enter the change
   and save again, watching for the green confirmation in the top-right corner.
3. Remember that nothing auto-saves — every form needs an explicit **Save**.

**Still stuck?** Tell your Peekaboo provider which page you were on and exactly which
field, and whether the green confirmation appeared.

---

### "It said it saved, but nothing changed."

**What's happening:** A few fields display their standard value when a record is
reloaded rather than the option you picked. Anything you typed into a free-text box is
the most reliable record.

**What to do:**
1. Reload and check which parts came back and which did not.
2. Put the information that matters into the record's main free-text box — on a daily
   report that is **Note to parents**.
3. For anything about a child's health or safety, also tell the room lead in person.

**Still stuck?** Give your Peekaboo provider the page name and the exact field, and say
what you selected and what came back.

---

### "A message with no detail appeared on screen."

**What's happening:** Something did not complete and the portal showed a short general
message rather than a specific one. It usually means the request did not reach the
server, or it came back without an explanation.

**What to do:**
1. Check your internet connection, then reload the page.
2. Try the same action once more. Most of these clear on a second attempt.
3. If it was a save, reload first and check whether the record went through before you
   re-enter it, so you do not create a duplicate.

**Still stuck?** Tell your Peekaboo provider which page you were on, exactly which
button you pressed, and the wording of the message.

---

### "A parent says they never got the daily report."

**What's happening:** **Publish** marks a report finished but does not send it.
Emailing is a separate step. This is the most common mix-up on the page and is easy to
put right.

**What to do:**
1. Open **Daily Reports** and set the date picker to that report's date.
2. Find the child's card in the left-hand list.
3. Click the **envelope icon** on the right-hand side of the card. That sends the report
   you already saved.
4. If the envelope is greyed out, no report exists for that date — create one first.
5. Check the child has a linked parent with an email address on their record. Without
   one, there is nowhere to send it.
6. In future, use **Publish & Email** to do both steps in one click.

**Still stuck?** Note the confirmation wording you saw and tell your Peekaboo provider,
along with the child's name and the date of the report.

---

### "I edited a report I had already emailed — does the parent see the change?"

**What's happening:** Editing a note does not send the report again. The parent still
has the version you sent.

**What to do:**
1. Make your edit and save it.
2. Click the **envelope icon** on the right-hand side of the child's card to send the
   updated report.
3. If the change is urgent or about the child's wellbeing, phone the parent as well.

**Still stuck?** Ask your room lead what your nursery's practice is for corrections
after a report has gone out.

---

### "An upload was refused."

**What's happening:** Some upload slots have a size or file-type limit, and the message
on screen names the file that was too big.

**What to do:**
1. Check the limit for where you are: photos on **Messages** need to be JPEG or PNG
   under 1 MB, videos up to 35 MB, and an expense attachment up to 2 MB.
2. Resize or compress the file, or convert a photo to JPEG or PNG. Phone photos are
   often far larger than 1 MB straight from the camera.
3. Try again with the smaller file.

**Still stuck?** Tell your Peekaboo provider the file size, the file type and which
upload slot you were using.

---

### "One file was too big and it cleared my whole selection."

**What's happening:** The size check runs across everything you picked at once, so a
single oversized file takes the rest of the selection with it.

**What to do:**
1. Check file sizes before you select, especially for photos.
2. Add files in small batches rather than one large selection, so a rejection costs you
   less.
3. Use the hint under the picker — you can add more images after your first selection,
   building the set up gradually.

**Still stuck?** If you regularly send large sets of photos, ask your Peekaboo provider
about the best workflow for your nursery.

---

### "I can't mark this invoice as paid."

**What's happening:** Invoices in Peekaboo do not carry a paid or unpaid status. Money
received is recorded separately, as a receipt against the child.

**What to do:**
1. Record the payment as a receipt on **Receipts**, choosing the child and adding at
   least one payment line.
2. Open **Balance by Class** to see that family's position — billed, received and the
   difference.
3. Write the invoice reference into the receipt's payment detail text so a colleague can
   match them up by eye later.

**Still stuck?** Ask your Peekaboo provider about reporting on outstanding fees. Do not
edit an invoice's description to say "PAID" — nothing reads it and it will appear on
the printed document.

---

### "The invoice didn't change after I recorded a payment."

**What's happening:** Receipts are recorded against the child rather than against a
single invoice, so an invoice looks the same before and after a payment. That is
expected.

**What to do:**
1. Use **Balance by Class** for the family's overall position — it is the figure to
   quote to a parent.
2. Note the invoice reference in the receipt's payment detail text so the link is
   readable by a person.
3. If a family pays part of a bill, record exactly what they paid; the balance will
   show the remainder.

**Still stuck?** Tell your Peekaboo provider which child and which invoice, and what you
expected to see.

---

### "My purchase lines have disappeared."

**What's happening:** Switching **Tax Exempt** part-way through a purchase changes which
services are available and clears the lines already entered.

**What to do:**
1. Set **Tax Exempt** first, before you enter any lines.
2. Re-enter the lines and save.
3. On an invoice the same toggle is safe to change at any time — it is purchases where
   the order matters.

**Still stuck?** Tell your Peekaboo provider how many lines you lost, so they understand
the cost of re-keying.

---

### "The colours on Balance by Class look the wrong way round."

**What's happening:** The colour is not the reliable guide here. The sign in front of
the figure is. A **+** means the family still owes you, and a **-** means they have paid
more than they were billed.

**What to do:**
1. Read the prefix character on every row, not the colour.
2. Cross-check the **Billed** and **Received** columns for that child.
3. Train your team to read the sign — it takes one briefing and removes the confusion
   for good.

**Still stuck?** If you are quoting a figure to a family, check both columns before you
speak, and ask your finance lead if the two do not tell the same story.

---

### "An enquiry stage has a different name on a different screen."

**What's happening:** Some admissions stages have two names — one on the enquiry's
status menu and another on the dashboard. They refer to the same stage.

**What to do:**
1. Go by the badge text on the enquiry row in **CRM Leads**. That is your working
   record.
2. Agree with your team which wording you use internally, and use it consistently in
   conversation.
3. When quoting figures to management, say which screen they came from.

**Still stuck?** Tell your Peekaboo provider which two names you saw and on which
screens, so the wording can be aligned.

---

### "The figures look out of date."

**What's happening:** The dashboard refreshes itself about once a minute and updates
quietly in place, so a change you just made may not have been picked up yet. The green
**Live** badge is decoration rather than a status.

**What to do:**
1. Switch to another window and back, which prompts a refresh.
2. Reload the page if you want to be certain.
3. If a figure reads zero, reload before acting on it.

**Still stuck?** If a figure stays wrong after a reload, tell your Peekaboo provider
which card on which tab, and what you expected.

---

### "I can't find a page in the menu."

**What's happening:** Either your role does not have it, or you are looking for a page
under a different name. Rooms are **Classes**, journals are **Daily Reports**, enquiries
are **CRM Leads**, and bills are **Invoices**.

**What to do:**
1. Check the names above and look again — most "missing" pages are simply named
   differently.
2. If it is genuinely not there, ask your administrator to grant it to your role, then
   sign out and back in.
3. On a narrow screen, open the sidebar with the toggle before deciding an item is
   missing.

**Still stuck?** Tell your administrator what you are trying to do rather than which
page you want — they can point you at the right one.

---

### "The page looks blank."

**What's happening:** The page did not finish loading. It is almost always a momentary
connection problem rather than anything to do with your data.

**What to do:**
1. Reload the browser page.
2. If it is still blank, go back to the dashboard from the sidebar and open the page
   again.
3. Check anything you were part-way through saving, and re-enter it if it did not go
   through.

**Still stuck?** Tell your Peekaboo provider which page, what you had clicked
immediately before, and whether a reload fixed it.

---

### "Part of the screen is still in English when I'm using Arabic."

**What's happening:** A few phrases are shown in their original English wording. The
layout still flips correctly right-to-left, and the screen works normally — it is just
those specific phrases.

**What to do:**
1. Carry on working; the meaning of the surrounding screen is unaffected.
2. Note the exact English phrase and which screen it is on.
3. Remember that your language choice is remembered per browser, so set it again on each
   machine you use.

**Still stuck?** Send your Peekaboo provider the exact phrase and a note of the page —
that is all they need to add the translation.

---

### "My browser keeps offering to translate our Arabic pages."

**What's happening:** The browser is guessing the page language and getting it wrong.
The portal's own Arabic is unaffected.

**What to do:**
1. Dismiss the prompt.
2. Choose the option to stop offering for this site, so it does not come back.

**Still stuck?** Mention it to your Peekaboo provider — it is cosmetic for most people
but worth knowing about if anyone uses a screen reader.

---

### "The search box doesn't seem to do anything."

**What's happening:** Search looks at the fields that page nominates — usually the name
— and only within the rows already loaded. A term sitting in another column will not be
found.

**What to do:**
1. Search on the **name**, which is what most pages match.
2. Use the page's own filters where it has them.
3. Check the footer under the table: if it says **"(filtered from N)"**, search is
   working and simply found nothing.

**Still stuck?** Tell your Peekaboo provider which page and which column you were trying
to search on.

---

### "A search found nothing and now the list looks empty."

**What's happening:** An empty result and an empty list look the same on screen. Your
records are still there.

**What to do:**
1. Clear the search box and the list returns.
2. Check the footer for the **"(filtered from N)"** note, which tells you records exist
   but are hidden.
3. Watch for typos — one wrong letter empties the list.

**Still stuck?** If clearing the search does not bring the list back, reload the page
and try once more before contacting anyone.

---

### "The list is showing every row instead of pages."

**What's happening:** Paging is switched on for some lists and not others, so on some
pages you get the whole table at once.

**What to do:**
1. Use the search box to narrow the list instead of paging through it.
2. Where a **Show N entries** control exists, the options are 10, 25 and 50.
3. Use your browser's own find function to jump to a name in a long list.

**Still stuck?** If a list is long enough to slow you down every day, tell your Peekaboo
provider which one.

---

### "I clicked PDF and it opened a print window."

**What's happening:** That is how the PDF buttons work. The print window is the route to
a PDF — you choose the destination yourself.

**What to do:**
1. In the print dialog, set the destination to **Save as PDF**.
2. Choose where to save it and click save.
3. If nothing opened at all, your browser blocked the window — allow pop-ups for the
   portal and try again.

**Still stuck?** Tell your Peekaboo provider which button on which page, and whether you
saw a blocked-pop-up icon in the address bar.

---

### "I clicked Print and absolutely nothing happened."

**What's happening:** Your browser blocked the new window before it could open. This
affects every print and PDF button in the portal.

**What to do:**
1. Look at the end of your browser's address bar, at the top of the browser window. A
   small blocked-pop-up icon appears there.
2. Click that icon and choose the option that allows pop-ups for this address.
3. Click the print or PDF button again.

**Still stuck?** Ask your administrator or IT support to allow pop-ups for the portal
across your nursery's computers once, and it will not recur.

---

### "My export has more rows than I filtered to."

**What's happening:** The finance exports give you the full table rather than the
filtered view. The file is complete rather than wrong.

**What to do:**
1. Download the file, then filter it in your spreadsheet.
2. Do not bother changing the page size first — that does not affect the export.
3. Rename the file with today's date as soon as it downloads, so repeat downloads do not
   pile up as copies.

**Still stuck?** Tell your Peekaboo provider which table you exported and what you
expected to get.

---

### "Nothing asked me to confirm before it deleted something."

**What's happening:** A few controls act immediately on one click — most notably the
status dot on a role card and delete on the calendar. There is no undo anywhere in the
portal.

**What to do:**
1. Re-create what was removed if you can. For a calendar event, add it again with the
   same details.
2. Before any delete, check what depends on the record — who holds a role, which
   enquiries use a referral.
3. Prefer setting records **Inactive** wherever a status toggle exists, which keeps the
   history.

**Still stuck?** Tell your administrator what was removed and when, so they can check
whether anyone else is affected.

---

### "I can't reply to a message from a parent."

**What's happening:** **Messages** publishes outward to the parent app. It is a
noticeboard rather than a mailbox, so there is nowhere for a reply to land.

**What to do:**
1. Reply by phone or email, exactly as you would for any other family contact.
2. If the whole room needs to know, publish a new post on **Messages**.
3. Use the chat button in the top bar for nothing — it does not reach parents. The
   **Messages** page is the real one.

**Still stuck?** Agree with your manager which channel families should use to reach you,
and tell them clearly at enrolment.

---

### "I can't record who collected a child."

**What's happening:** The check-out button stamps the time but does not ask who
collected the child. The written record goes in the notes.

**What to do:**
1. Click **Checkout** on the child's row.
2. Then click **Edit** on that same row.
3. Type the collector's name into the **Notes** box.
4. Keep the child's **Pickup** tab up to date as your list of who is authorised.
5. Keep doing your usual identity check at the door as well.

**Still stuck?** If your inspection framework requires a specific format, agree the
wording with your manager so every room records it the same way.

---

### "I can't edit attendance for a past date."

**What's happening:** The attendance boards work on today. Past days are there to read
on **Attendance Reports**, but not to change.

**What to do:**
1. Make completing the register part of the morning and home-time routine, so it is
   right before the day ends.
2. If you find an error later, note the correction in your nursery's own records and
   tell your room lead.
3. Use **Attendance Reports** to view what was recorded on a past date.

**Still stuck?** Tell your Peekaboo provider which date and which child, in case they
can help from their side.

---

### "A new enquiry already shows consent ticked."

**What's happening:** The enquiry form opens with some answers already selected,
including the consent boxes and the special educational needs question.

**What to do:**
1. Set every consent answer deliberately for each family, matching what they actually
   agreed.
2. Set the SEN question to **No** unless it genuinely applies — otherwise your figures
   will overstate it.
3. Make this an explicit step in your admissions script, so nobody relies on what the
   form opens with.

**Still stuck?** If enquiries were already created without checking, review them and
correct the consent answers. Tell your manager, since consent is a data-protection
matter.

---

### "Notes I typed seem to have gone."

**What's happening:** Some boxes show their standard value again when a record is
reloaded. The free-text boxes on a record are where information reliably stays.

**What to do:**
1. Reload and check which parts came back.
2. Re-enter anything missing into the record's main free-text box — on a daily report
   that is **Note to parents**, and on an attendance record it is **Notes**.
3. For anything about a child's wellbeing, tell the room lead in person as well.

**Still stuck?** Tell your Peekaboo provider the page and the exact box, and what you
had typed.

---

### "My teacher note appeared in the wrong box."

**What's happening:** The two boxes in the teacher note section are read back in order,
so filling only the second one moves that text up into the first.

**What to do:**
1. Always fill **Note to parents** first.
2. If you only have behaviour text to record, put something in the first box too, even
   a short line.
3. Reload once after saving to check it reads as you intended.

**Still stuck?** Tell your Peekaboo provider which report and what you had typed in each
box.

---

### "I pressed Save and nothing happened at all."

**What's happening:** A required field is empty. On the curriculum setup pages — the
curriculum itself, age groups, attainment and learning areas — a blank **name** stops
the save without a message.

**What to do:**
1. Check the **name** field first. That is the usual answer on those pages.
2. Scroll up the form, as some messages appear above the fold.
3. Fill in anything marked as required and save again, watching for the green
   confirmation.

**Still stuck?** Tell your Peekaboo provider which page and which fields you had filled
in.

---

### "I lost everything I'd typed when I clicked Cancel."

**What's happening:** Nothing auto-saves, and **Cancel** discards the form. Long forms
are safest completed in one sitting.

**What to do:**
1. Re-enter the details.
2. Click the save button — **Save**, **Update** or **Save Changes** — rather than
   **Cancel**.
3. For long forms such as a new enquiry, gather the information before you start.
4. If you are interrupted, save what you have — most records can be re-opened and
   completed later.

**Still stuck?** If you regularly lose work to interruptions, ask your manager whether
a quieter slot in the day would suit this task better.

---

### "I refreshed the page and lost my work."

**What's happening:** Work in progress lives on the screen until you save it. A refresh
starts the form again.

**What to do:**
1. Save before you leave a form, switch tabs or step away.
2. Finish each record in one sitting where you can.
3. If you were part-way through, reload and check whether an earlier save went through
   before you re-enter everything.

**Still stuck?** Note which form it was and tell your Peekaboo provider how far through
you had got.

---

### "I can't register a child — the Timing dropdown is empty."

**What's happening:** Registration needs an active session to choose from, and none
exists yet. This is the single most common thing that holds up a new registration.

**What to do:**
1. Ask whoever manages settings to add a **Session** on **My Center**.
2. Check there is also an active class to choose. A class needs an age group, and an age
   group needs a curriculum.
3. Make sure the child record itself has been saved at least once — the **Health**,
   **Pickup** and **Registrations** tabs open only after that.

**Still stuck?** The setup order is centre, then sessions and age groups, then
curriculum, then classes, then children. Ask your administrator to work through it in
that order.

---

### "I can't delete a child."

**What's happening:** Deleting is held back while the child still has registrations,
pickup people or health records attached. That is protecting their history.

**What to do:**
1. Set the child **Inactive** with the **Status** toggle instead. This is the right
   route for a child who has left, and nothing is lost.
2. If you genuinely must delete, remove the registrations, pickup people and health
   records first.
3. Remember inactive records still appear in list views — change the status filter to
   hide them.

**Still stuck?** Check with your manager before deleting any child record. Inactive is
almost always the answer.

---

### "I can't save an observation — it keeps asking about attainment."

**What's happening:** Every objective you link to an observation needs a rating, and the
rating list is empty until your nursery's attainment levels are set up.

**What to do:**
1. Ask whoever manages the curriculum to create your attainment levels.
2. Return to the observation, give each selected objective a rating, and save.
3. Check with your lead which scale your nursery uses — there is no built-in set.

**Still stuck?** Tell your administrator that the attainment list is empty; it is a
one-off setup task.

---

### "I can't generate an assessment report."

**What's happening:** The report needs one specific class and a valid date range.
Leaving the class dropdown on **All classes** stops it.

**What to do:**
1. Choose a single class from the dropdown.
2. Set both dates, with the **From** date before the **To** date.
3. If the report comes back empty, no observations fall in that range — widen it, or
   record the observations first.

**Still stuck?** Tell your Peekaboo provider the class, the dates and the exact message
you saw.

---

### "The attendance report totals don't match what I counted."

**What's happening:** Weekends are left out of every total, though they still appear as
tinted columns in the grid. Absent, excused and leave are also combined into one absence
figure.

**What to do:**
1. Recount excluding Saturday and Sunday.
2. Remember that public holidays count as ordinary working days, so allow for them.
3. Check for unmarked rows — those count as neither present nor absent.

**Still stuck?** When you quote these figures to anyone, say that weekends are excluded
and public holidays are not. That one sentence prevents most queries.

---

### "The dashboard attendance percentage looks too low."

**What's happening:** Children whose register was never marked are counted as neither
present nor absent, so the totals will not add up to your roll.

**What to do:**
1. Open **Children Attendance** and look for rows reading **Not Marked**.
2. Mark them and the figures settle.
3. Build a mid-morning check into the routine so the register is always complete.

**Still stuck?** If rows stay unmarked because nobody owns that room's register, agree
with your manager who marks it and by what time.

---

### "My filters reset when I switched between Children and Staff."

**What's happening:** Changing the person type on **Attendance Reports** clears the
group filter back to **All**. It is doing that on purpose.

**What to do:**
1. Choose the person type first.
2. Then set your dates and your group filter.
3. Export before you switch, if you have the view you want.

**Still stuck?** Nothing to report here — just set them in that order and it will behave
predictably.

---

### "Pressing Escape doesn't close the confirmation box."

**What's happening:** The confirmation box expects you to choose. Clicking **Cancel** is
the reliable way out.

**What to do:**
1. Click **Cancel** rather than pressing Escape.
2. Read the message before you click — on most pages this is your only checkpoint before
   a delete.

**Still stuck?** Nothing to report. If a dialog ever stays on screen after clicking
Cancel, reload the page.

---

### "There are two theme switches and I don't know which to use."

**What's happening:** There genuinely are two — one in the top bar, one in the sidebar
footer — and they do the same thing.

**What to do:**
1. Use either. They stay in sync.
2. Remember the setting is remembered per browser and per device, so set it on each
   machine you use.

**Still stuck?** Nothing to report; this one is purely a matter of preference.

---

### "I have several portal tabs open and can't tell them apart."

**What's happening:** Every page uses the same browser tab title, so open tabs look
identical.

**What to do:**
1. Work in one tab where you can.
2. If you need two, put them in a deliberate order and remember which is which, or pin
   the one you return to most.

**Still stuck?** Mention it to your Peekaboo provider as a small everyday annoyance
worth fixing.

---

### "Check-in times filled themselves in and I didn't type them."

**What's happening:** Marking a child **Present** or **Late** stamps the current clock
time for you. If a time is already there, it is kept rather than replaced.

**What to do:**
1. Use **Edit** on the child's row if you need a specific time.
2. Be careful marking a child **Absent** after checking them in — that clears both
   times.
3. If you clear a time by accident, re-mark them and set the correct time through
   **Edit** straight away.

**Still stuck?** Tell your room lead what the correct times were, so the day's records
are consistent.

---

### "The child I need isn't on the daily reports list."

**What's happening:** The list shows children whose registration covers that date and
whose enrolled weekdays include that day.

**What to do:**
1. Open the child's record and check the **Registrations** tab — the dates and the
   **Days** selection.
2. Correct the registration if the child has changed days or rooms.
3. Return to **Daily Reports** and the child appears.

**Still stuck?** Tell your administrator which child and which date, and what days they
should be attending.

---

### "I can't open Referrals & Sources, but I can't save an enquiry without one."

**What's happening:** That list is maintained by administrators, and every enquiry needs
a referral and a record source chosen from it.

**What to do:**
1. Ask your administrator to add the entry you need — for example a new school fair or
   a new website.
2. Meanwhile, choose the closest existing entry so the enquiry can be saved, and add a
   note explaining.
3. Update the enquiry once the correct entry exists.

**Still stuck?** Send your administrator a short list of all the sources your nursery
uses, so they can add them in one go.

---

### "A supplier has stopped trading and I want them off the list."

**What's happening:** Suppliers have no active or inactive switch, so the only removal
is deletion — and that risks your historical records.

**What to do:**
1. Leave the supplier in place and simply stop selecting them. This is the safe choice.
2. If the list is cluttered, rename them with a prefix such as "ZZ" so they sort to the
   bottom.
3. Avoid deleting anything a past expense or voucher refers to.

**Still stuck?** Ask your Peekaboo provider before deleting a supplier with history
behind it.

---

### "I deleted a role and now someone can't work."

**What's happening:** Anyone who held that role now has no role, so they see the message
about their role not being determined.

**What to do:**
1. Open the **Staff** page and reassign the affected people to a suitable role.
2. Ask them to sign out and back in.
3. Before deleting any role in future, check the **Staff** page for who holds it.

**Still stuck?** Tell your Peekaboo provider which role was removed and who is affected.

---

### "Access I granted hasn't appeared for my colleague."

**What's happening:** Access is read when the portal loads. A change made while someone
is signed in does not reach them until their next sign-in.

**What to do:**
1. Ask them to click **Logout** and sign back in.
2. Check you ticked the setting for the right **role**, not for a person — access is set
   per role.
3. Confirm they actually hold that role on their staff record.

**Still stuck?** Have them read out their role badge text to you, and compare it against
the role you edited.

---

### "Someone can see more than I intended."

**What's happening:** A few access settings open more than one page. **Daily Report**
opens all four attendance and daily-report pages, and **Balance by Class** opens for
anyone with invoices or receipts access.

**What to do:**
1. Open **Roles & Access**, choose the role and click **Manage access** to read exactly
   what is ticked.
2. Untick anything that role should not have, and have holders sign out and back in.
3. Where one person needs a different mix, create a role for that mix rather than
   loosening an existing one.

**Still stuck?** Ask your Peekaboo provider which pages each access setting covers before
you grant a new one.

---

### "I need a figure the portal doesn't show."

**What's happening:** Some totals are not calculated in the portal — hours worked, staff
ratios, profit, and per-class totals on **Balance by Class**.

**What to do:**
1. Export the underlying data where an export exists — attendance, expenses, purchases
   and payment vouchers all have one.
2. Work the figure out in your spreadsheet.
3. Where there is no export, copy the rows you need by hand and keep the working
   alongside them.

**Still stuck?** Tell your Peekaboo provider which figure you need regularly and what you
use it for.

---

### "I can't work out whether a family has paid."

**What's happening:** Invoices and receipts are separate records, so the answer comes
from the balance rather than from any single document.

**What to do:**
1. Open **Balance by Class** and find the child.
2. Read **Billed**, **Received** and the difference, checking the sign in front of the
   balance.
3. If you need the detail behind it, open that child's invoices and receipts side by
   side.

**Still stuck?** Ask your finance lead to confirm before you discuss a balance with a
family.

---

### "The enquiry statuses in my report don't match what my manager expected."

**What's happening:** The dashboard and the enquiry list use different wording for the
same stages, so two people can describe the same pipeline differently.

**What to do:**
1. Quote figures from one place and say which — the enquiry list badges are the working
   record.
2. Agree a shared vocabulary with your team and write it down.
3. When reporting, include the date you took the figures.

**Still stuck?** Share the two screens with your manager so you agree on the mapping
once.

---

### "I've been asked for something the portal doesn't record."

**What's happening:** Incidents, complaints, staff rotas and formal accident logs are
kept outside the portal.

**What to do:**
1. Use your nursery's own process and records for those.
2. Note anything clinical about a child on their **Health** tab.
3. Tell parents through the **Note to parents** box or a phone call, and tell the room
   lead in person.

**Still stuck?** Ask your manager where your nursery keeps each of these records, so
everyone uses the same place.

---

## When to contact support

Most things on this page you can settle yourself in a minute or two. When you do need
help, a good report gets you an answer far faster than a general one.

**Who to ask:**

- **Your nursery's Admin** for anything about access — a missing menu item, a denied
  page, a role that needs changing, a password reset.
- **Your Peekaboo provider** for everything else.

**Gather these five things before you contact anyone:**

1. **Which page you were on.** Give the name as it appears in the sidebar — for example
   **Daily Reports** or **Balance by Class**.
2. **What you clicked.** The exact button or link, using the wording on screen.
3. **What you expected to happen.**
4. **What actually happened.** Copy the wording of any message you saw, word for word,
   and take a photo or screenshot if you can.
5. **Your role.** Read it from the badge in the top bar on a wide screen.

**Also worth adding:**

- The date and rough time it happened.
- Whether it happens every time, or happened once.
- Whether reloading the page changed anything.
- The child, family, invoice or enquiry involved, by name or reference.
- Which browser and device you were using, and whether a colleague on a different
  machine sees the same thing.

**Before you send it, try these three:** reload the page, sign out and back in, and try
the same action once more. Between them they resolve a large share of everyday
problems — and if they do not, saying you tried them tells support a great deal.


---


<!-- ================= 06-module-guides ================= -->

# Guide to Each Area of Peekaboo

This guide is a tour. It walks you through every area of the portal in the order the
sidebar shows it, and explains what each one is for, who normally works in it, what you
will see when you open it, and — most importantly — how it connects to everything else.

It is meant to be **read**, not looked up. If you want the numbered steps for a specific
task, use the How To guide instead. Read this one first, or read it in the evening of
your first week, and the step-by-step instructions will make far more sense.

Bold text is exactly what appears on your screen, so you can match the words on the page
to the words here.

A note before you start: **your sidebar may be shorter than the list below.** Peekaboo
only shows you the areas your role is allowed to open. If an area described here is not
in your menu, it simply is not part of your job, and that is normal.

---

## The one idea that explains most of the portal

Before the tour, here is the single idea that makes everything else click into place.

**Everything in Peekaboo points at the child record.** Attendance, the daily report that
goes home, observations of a child's learning, invoices, receipts — all of them attach
to one child on the **Children** page.

But a child is not placed in a room directly. The link between a child and a class is a
separate thing called a **Registration**, which you create inside the child's own record.
It ties the child to a class, a session time, a date range and the specific weekdays they
attend.

That one detail explains the most common puzzle new users hit: *"I added the child, but
the teacher can't see them."* Almost always, the child exists but has no registration. A
child with no registration is in the system, visible on the **Children** page, and
invisible everywhere else — no register, no daily report, no class list.

So the order of operations for a new starter is always: **create the child, then register
the child.** Two steps, not one.

---

## Your Dashboard

**Open this area:** [Live Dashboard](https://peek.peek-a-boo.app/app)

### What this area is for

**Live Dashboard** is the view from the office door. It is the page you land on when you
sign in, and it answers the three questions a manager asks at a glance: how full are we
today, how is the money looking this month, and how many families are enquiring about
places.

### Who normally uses it

The principal and the office team, mostly. Room staff tend to pass through it on their
way to the register. Anyone can be given access to it.

### What you will see

A header with today's date and a small green **Live** pill, then three tabs.

**Main Dashboard** shows three headline cards — **Children Present** (a figure like
`42 / 50` with an attendance percentage beneath), **Absent Today** (with the split
between absent and excused shown underneath), and **Active Staff**. Below them sits
**Classroom Occupancy**, one bar per room showing enrolled against capacity, then
**Late Arrivals** listing each child marked late today with their reason and arrival
time, and **Upcoming & Pinned**, which collects pinned announcements and the next few
calendar events.

**Finance** has a month selector and shows what was billed against what was collected
for that month, revenue broken down by age group, and outstanding balances.

**CRM** shows the admissions picture, with nine counter cards and a choice of three
views — **Calendar**, **Graphs** and **Details**.

### The key things you can do here

Read, and change the view. That is genuinely all. You can move the Finance month
backwards and forwards, switch the CRM between its three views, and export the CRM
figures to a spreadsheet. **Nothing on the dashboard can be edited.** Every number you
see was created by somebody on another page.

### How it connects to the rest of the portal

The dashboard is a mirror. It holds no data of its own — it reads what other pages have
recorded and draws it as figures and charts.

That means when a dashboard number looks wrong, **the dashboard is not where you fix
it.** Follow the number back to its source: the attendance figures come from
**Children Attendance**, the occupancy bars come from class capacities on **Classes** and
from registrations on **Children**, the Finance tab comes from **Invoices** and
**Receipts**, and the CRM tab comes from **Leads**.

### Worth knowing

**It refreshes itself about once a minute.** This is the only page in Peekaboo that does.
There is no refresh button and you do not need one — leave it open and the figures update
on their own. It also refreshes when you click back onto the browser tab after being away.

Everywhere else in the portal is the opposite: pages load once and stay still. If a
colleague changes something while you have a page open, reload the page to see it.

**Present and Absent will often not add up to your total roll**, and this is worth
understanding rather than worrying about. Children whose register has not been marked at
all count in neither figure. They are not "absent" — nobody has said anything about them
yet. If the numbers look short, the usual reason is an unmarked register, not a wrong
number. Marking every child every morning keeps the dashboard honest.

A child marked **Late** counts as present in the **Children Present** figure. That is
deliberate — they are in the building.

**Active Staff** counts who is employed and active on your staff list, not who has
clocked in today. For who is actually in the building right now, open **Staff
Attendance**.

**The enquiry cards use different wording from the Admissions page.** A stage that reads
one way on the dashboard may read differently on **Leads**. This matters most around the
tour stages, where the dashboard and the Leads page do not use the same words for the
same point in the journey.

The rule is simple and worth committing to memory: **treat the Leads page as the
authority on any enquiry's stage.** Use the dashboard for the general shape of the month
— are enquiries up, are we converting — and open **Leads** whenever you need to know where
one particular family actually stands.

Finally, the dashboard shows you your centre's position, not a per-room breakdown you can
filter. There is no campus filter and no date range beyond the Finance month selector.

---

## Your Nursery

This group holds your nursery's own settings. Two pages: **My Center** and **Packages**.
Principals also see a third, **Centers**, which is explained below because the similar
names cause real confusion.

### Centers vs My Center — read this once and it will never trouble you again

These two page names look almost identical and mean entirely different things.
